groups, all within the four-part bar) is APPLIED below and this text freezes as the runtime spine. From here,
refinement is filed against the BLUEPRINT (§E) — an objection reopens this sheet only if it shows, in this text,
a lost fact, an unconsented apply, an untrue value or a hidden commit. APPENDIX: the D13/D14 research note with
the owner's rulings (2026-09-01) — reviewers audit its evidence and policy shape (bounded), not the sheet.
Purpose: the runtime truths a fixture-bound mock cannot surface — offline, sync, conflict, cold start, recovery —
written as contracts so design draws them and engineering builds them. Under the ratified spine (v2.2): [R3] P1
privacy for the beta, [R4] multi-device append with single proposal authority, D7 ladder, §9 identity/causality/
decision rules. Decisions, not prose. PROVE marks a gate item, not a claim.

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
A · OPERATIONS, ACCEPTANCE, ENVELOPE, PAYLOAD — what merge can see vs what is sealed (P2-ready, used under P1)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
A1 OPERATION AND ITS IDENTITY. Every client-created operation is IMMUTABLE from its durable local commit. Its
  identity is op_id + canonical_content_commitment, where
    canonical_content_commitment = HMAC(K_identity[key_epoch], "earned/op/v1" || canonical_encode(
      the immutable client envelope EXCLUDING canonical_content_commitment, encryption wrappers, sealing
      transitions and authority metadata; the canonical plaintext payload)).
  The canonical encoding version declares decimal representation, units, Unicode normalization, map ordering,
  set ordering and distinct encodings for absent / null / value. K_identity is separate from the payload-
  encryption keys; rewrapping (P1 key rotation) and P1→P2 migration never re-HMAC existing operations; key
  recovery/rotation preserves historical verification and recurrence identities through an append-only
  key-epoch transition. A P1→P2 resealing that changes payload ciphertext is recorded as an append-only SEALING
  TRANSITION — the original content and commitment are never rewritten. Corrections, reclassifications,
  tombstones and undo requests are NEW operations that target the original. Server acceptance appends a
  separate immutable ACCEPTANCE RECEIPT {op_id, canonical_content_commitment, athlete_log_seq, accepted_at};
  receipts are metadata for client operations, not recursively receipted operations. athlete_log_seq is
  monotonic within the athlete's accepted log. A FRONTIER is the athlete's contiguous watermark W (or its hash)
  — never one operation's number, never advanced by another athlete's writes. op_id deduplication scope is per
  athlete: a replayed op_id returns the original disposition ONLY when the commitment matches; the same op_id
  with a different commitment is rejected as an identity collision, never treated as a replay.
A2 ADMISSION. The envelope carries opaque athlete_id and device_id; the authority VERIFIES both against the
  authenticated account and the enrolled device and never trusts them alone. TWO ORDERS, never confused:
  device_predecessor_op_id gives gap-free TRANSPORT/REPLAY order for one enrolled device (device_seq unique and
  gap-free among acknowledged operations; reuse of a device_seq with a different op_id or commitment is
  rejected); causal_parents[] names only the operations whose accepted effects this operation actually
  observed or relied upon (a later plan edit that depends on an earlier one names it and waits; an unrelated
  weigh-in does not — rig152 Q1: with the predecessor as a mandatory causal parent, one stranded operation
  blocked every later operation on that phone, contradicting state 19's "never blocks other entries"). Every
  device-sequence slot receives an authenticated OPERATION DISPOSITION {op_id, canonical_content_commitment,
  device_id, device_seq, status, athlete_log_seq?, rejection_code?, decided_at, authority_signature}.
  DISPOSITION LIFECYCLE: WAITING is a signed NON-terminal status; it is superseded, append-only, by exactly one
  immutable TERMINAL disposition — ACCEPTED, REJECTED, or REJECTED_DEPENDENCY (its required parent was
  terminally rejected; a child never waits for ever behind a rejected parent); replay of a slot returns its
  current disposition and, once terminal, always the same one; receipt of a predecessor accounts for transport
  order even while its semantic admission waits or fails. REFERENCES: a known same-athlete identity → admit; a
  known cross-athlete identity → REJECT (state 19); an identity not yet accepted → durably defer as WAITING FOR
  DEPENDENCY and admit once its causal parent is ACCEPTED — a legitimate child is never rejected for delivery
  order. Acceptance is deferred until every declared causal parent is ACCEPTED, so a child's athlete_log_seq is
  always greater than its parents'. OFFLINE-WRITE LEASE = an authentic device/schema CAPABILITY: every release
  carries a signed lease {lease_id, schema version, authorized device-sequence range, not_after ≥ the C3
  interval, 30 days}; VALIDITY AT LOCAL COMMIT = the monotonic clock has not passed not_after AND the
  operation's device_seq lies inside the authorized range — both enforced locally, so "Saved" is never said for
  a slot the authority would refuse (rig153 Q2: enforcing time alone said "Saved" at slot 501 of a 500-slot
  range and the authority then rejected it; range exhaustion routes to state 20 exactly like expiry). The
  authority admits every correctly chained operation that carries a legitimately issued, non-revoked
  capability and lies inside its range REGARDLESS of when it arrives — it never rejects an acknowledged
  operation merely because server receipt occurs after not_after, and it never shortens a lease retroactively;
  original bytes stay immutable and reduction uses a deterministic, versioned up-conversion. REVOCATION
  BARRIER (the one qualification of that guarantee): when the athlete revokes a device (state 17) the barrier is
  that device's last ACCEPTED device_seq — operations beyond it are refused, and the athlete is told, at
  revocation time, that unsynced entries on that device will be lost (state 12's declared loss); revocation is
  never silent and never retroactive below the barrier. Expiry is a SANCTIONED-CLIENT WRITE CUTOFF enforced
  locally with signed server time plus a conservative, persisted, monotonic high-water clock: clock rollback
  never extends it (rig152 Q7); if continuity cannot be proven after a restore or reboot the client enters
  state 20 early. "Outside every valid lease" (state 19) means forged, unknown, revoked beyond the barrier, or
  outside the authorized sequence range — never late creation inferred from athlete-attested time.
A3 ENVELOPE (structured; merge-visible; under P2 the plaintext the threat model enumerates), every op:
  op_id · canonical_content_commitment · athlete_id · device_id · device_seq · device_predecessor_op_id ·
  causal_parents[] · class · kind (fact | correction | reclassification | tombstone | plan-mutation | lineage |
  session-start | session-set | session-skip | session-close | session-relationship-resolution |
  set-slot-resolution | proposal-response | undo-request | conflict-resolution | conflict-selection) ·
  target_op_id (REQUIRED for correction, reclassification and tombstone ONLY; the target is never edited) ·
  effective local date, time
  and UTC offset (athlete-attested) · schema_version · lease_id · every quantity carries its unit, or is bound
  to an immutable schema-level unit (a later pounds → kilograms switch never reinterprets a historical 100).
  KIND-SPECIFIC ENVELOPE FIELDS: session-start {planned_split_slot_id | AD_HOC, plan_basis} · session-set
  {session_start_op_id, logical_set_slot} · session-skip {session_start_op_id, lift_lineage_id, skip_scope:
  lift | set (+ logical_set_slot for a set-level skip)} · session-close {session_start_op_id} (no slot) ·
  session-relationship-resolution {sorted candidate session-start ids, component generation, opaque keyed
  answer commitment} · set-slot-resolution {targeted set-operation ids, opaque keyed decision commitment} ·
  lineage {lift_lineage_id, parent_lift_lineage_id?, event_kind} · lift-scoped ops {lift_lineage_id} ·
  body-composition source {opaque linked_weight_op_id, unless the sealed measurement carries attested total
  mass} · plan-mutation {conflict_domain_id, conflict_domain_lineage_id, requested_transaction_id (REQUIRED,
  1:1 with the op), per-member provenance flags athlete_edited | inherited, seen_plan_basis, keyed member-set
  commitment} · undo-request {target_plan_transaction_id, seen_plan_basis, target_effect_digest, keyed
  commitment of the exact compensating group} · conflict-selection (state 4) {conflict_domain_id,
  conflict_domain_lineage_id, requested_transaction_id (1:1 with the op — its uniqueness identity),
  seen_conflict_basis, chosen alternative commitment} — admission and the CAS outcome are ONE atomic authority
  commit: before terminal acceptance either exactly one plan transaction is durable or one durable non-applied
  result exists; replay returns that result · PROPOSAL RESPONSE and CONFLICT RESOLUTION (one schema,
  every field required, mirrored in A4): proposal_issuance_id · proposal_family_id · evidence_generation ·
  decline_fingerprint_digest · seen_dependency_basis · seen_engine_version · offer_digest · chosen_outcome_id ·
  consent_digest (a conflict-resolution additionally names the covered DECISION antichain — responses and
  prior resolutions). consent_digest binds the exact offer, the selected outcome and the exact atomic delta;
  it IS the consent. proposal_snapshot_digest is retired.
  AUTHORITY-APPENDED RECORDS in the same log, each with its own unique athlete_log_seq and its causal parents
  or basis: PROPOSAL ISSUANCE {proposal_issuance_id, proposal_family_id, evidence_generation,
  decline_fingerprint_digest, proposal_dependency_basis, computed_through_watermark, evaluation local date/
  time/zone, reducer/schema version, policy versions, proposal_surface_contract_version (signed; covers the
  engine, eligibility rules and presentation mapper), mapper version, issued_by_device_id, offer_digest, the
  OFFER SNAPSHOT (unselected choices only), allowed outcome_id → consent_digest + exact atomic delta
  commitment} · BASIS CONFIRMATION {instance, recomputed basis, computed_through_watermark, evaluation date/
  zone} · PLAN TRANSACTION {plan_transaction_id, conflict_domain_id, conflict_domain_lineage_id, source
  (plan-mutation op via requested_transaction_id | response op | conflict-resolution op | conflict-selection op
  | undo request + its target), applied basis, offer_digest + consent_digest where sourced from consent,
  target_effect_digest where a compensation, sealed BEFORE and AFTER complete groups with keyed commitments,
  complete-group provenance {authored | consented | resolved | migrated}} — under P2 the envelope carries only
  the opaque domain id and keyed member-set commitments. INSTANCE: the authority CANONICALIZES issuance per key
  (athlete_id, proposal_family_id, evidence_generation, offer_digest); a later issuance ALIASES the existing
  record only when its complete basis, engine, fingerprint, outcome set, consent digests and delta commitments
  are equal — any mismatch is an INSTANCE COLLISION (rejected, logged), never a silent alias.
  ATOMIC DIRECT-EDIT ADMISSION: admission of a plan-mutation operation and creation of its sole PLAN
  TRANSACTION is ONE atomic authority commit; the ACCEPTED disposition is released only after both are durable;
  replay returns the same transaction (Sol 4: an authority crash between the two would drain the outbox as
  "Synced" while the shared plan never changed). An athlete plan edit therefore has ONE reduction node: the
  client op is the immutable request/consent shown locally (state 1); the transaction is the only node
  entering state 4; Undo and History target it.
A4 PAYLOAD (values; encrypted at rest under P1 with server-managed keys, the server decrypts to reduce; sealed
  ciphertext under P2), by class: reading {lb} · food-day {kcal?, protein_g?, missing_total_status?} · steps
  {count} · sleep {hours, quality?} · event {label?, type, interval} · illness {interval, note?} · PAIN
  ATTESTATION {scope: session-only (+ session_start_op_id) | open interval (start; closed only by a later
  targeted close/correction carrying the end), note?} — athlete-attested, never a diagnosis · setup-note {text,
  non-empty} · correction {replacement_fields (≥ 1), incl. replacement effective date/time/offset where
  applicable} · reclassification {replacement_class — ONLY between declared payload-compatible classes (e.g. a
  reading's morning/late class), otherwise a complete validated destination payload with units and effective
  fields is required; the old payload is never reinterpreted under the new class name} · tombstone {reason,
  non-empty} · BODY-COMPOSITION SOURCE
  {kind: measurement | athlete estimate, quantity + unit (lean mass, or body-fat fraction), endpoints, central
  estimate? (low ≤ point ≤ high when present), provenance/author, effective date, attested total mass?} ·
  SESSION FAMILY: session-start {} · session-set {load + unit, reps, reserve} · session-skip {reason?} ·
  session-close {completion_kind} · session-relationship-resolution {the complete PARTITION of the ambiguity
  component into physical workouts (SAME / DISTINCT is only the two-start presentation) + the governing
  workout-plan basis chosen} · set-slot-resolution {the partition of the collision component into duplicate
  classes and distinct attempts, one canonical operation per duplicate class, unique slots for distinct
  attempts} · LINEAGE {event_kind: rename (identity retained; new name) | technique-fork (child identity,
  parent link, carry_rule) | context-fork (identity retained; comparability boundary dated at actual exposure
  — spine Q1/Q2), display names?, carry_rule?} · plan mutation {complete member values + units} · OFFER
  SNAPSHOT (issuance) {exact unselected choices, plan fields and deltas, values, units, evidence operation
  identities, eligibility and guard result, reason/copy parameters, engine version} · RESPONSE SELECTION
  {selected outcome} · conflict-resolution {exact resolution snapshot, complete affected group, chosen values}
  · conflict-selection {the complete alternative chosen}. Names, notes, athlete answers, literal SAME/DISTINCT/
  DUPLICATE tokens and measurements are always sealed payload.
  REQUIRED-FIELD MATRIX (schema-enforced at local commit AND at admission; a missing required field is invalid
  input — never stored, never acknowledged): reading {lb + unit} · steps {count} · sleep {hours} · food-day {≥ 1
  observed field, or missing_total_status} · event {type, interval} · illness {interval start} · pain session-
  only {session_start_op_id, scope} · pain open-interval {start} · setup-note {text} · correction
  {target_op_id, ≥ 1 replacement field} · reclassification {target_op_id, replacement_class ∈ the declared
  compatible set, else the complete destination payload} · tombstone
  {target_op_id, reason} · session-start {planned_split_slot_id | AD_HOC, plan_basis} · session-set
  {session_start_op_id, logical_set_slot, lift_lineage_id, load + unit, reps} · session-skip
  {session_start_op_id, lift_lineage_id, skip_scope (+ slot when set-level)} · session-close
  {session_start_op_id, completion_kind} · session-relationship-resolution {candidate family, complete
  partition, chosen plan basis where SAME} · set-slot-resolution {targeted set ids, complete partition,
  canonical selections, replacement slots} · rename {lift_lineage_id, new name} · technique-fork {child, parent,
  carry_rule} · context-fork {lift_lineage_id, actual-exposure boundary} · plan-mutation {complete member set +
  units, conflict_domain_id, requested_transaction_id, per-member flags, seen_plan_basis} · undo-request
  {target_plan_transaction_id, seen_plan_basis, target_effect_digest, compensating-group commitment} ·
  conflict-selection {conflict_domain_id, conflict_domain_lineage_id, requested_transaction_id,
  seen_conflict_basis, chosen alternative} · proposal-response {all nine response fields}
  · conflict-resolution {all nine + covered decision antichain + exact snapshot + complete group} ·
  body-composition source {quantity + unit, endpoints, provenance, effective date, linked_weight_op_id for a
  body-fat source without attested total mass}. VALIDITY: empty strings, NaN and non-finite numbers are invalid
  locally and at admission — never "present" (rig151 Q7); 0 is an attested zero only where the field's domain
  permits zero (steps yes; load no).
  PRESENCE LAW (everything not required): absent is absent — not 0, not "", not a default. The schema encodes
  absence explicitly, reducers treat only present values as observations, and NO client path ever writes a
  displayed target or estimate into an intake field (spine §8). PROVE: an absent kcal contributes to no intake
  mean, no TDEE window and no floor; an attested 0 does (rig148).
A5 ATOMIC PLAN TRANSACTIONS, CONFLICT DOMAINS, UNDO. Fields sharing an invariant change only as ONE plan
  transaction carrying every coupled field (declared complete member set/hash); no reducer ever exposes a
  partial transaction. CONFLICT DOMAINS are a schema-versioned DISJOINT PARTITION with a stable
  conflict_domain_lineage_id per domain: every field that may be coupled atomically belongs to exactly one
  predeclared encompassing domain (per lift: load vector + set count + rep ceiling; the intake plan; the step
  plan; …), every transaction to exactly one domain, no runtime-composed domains, no overlap (rig151 Q5). Every
  schema version whose leased operations may still arrive normalizes into ONE common disjoint partition;
  domains may not split or overlap across versions without a deterministic old → superdomain mapping that
  preserves each transaction atomically (Sol 5: a late v1 operation in G1 and a v2 operation in G2 over the
  same fields could both win). UNDO: an undo request carries target_plan_transaction_id, seen_plan_basis,
  target_effect_digest and the keyed commitment of the exact compensating group (the target's sealed BEFORE
  group); it acquires no transaction id of its own. Ordinary Undo is available only when its target is the
  UNIQUE causal maximum of its domain, is unsuspended, and its post-effect digest equals the current domain
  effect; the authority appends the compensation (its own plan_transaction_id, referencing the request and the
  target) by compare-and-append against exactly that condition — otherwise the request is retained as
  non-applied History and the face names the governing plan ("Undo couldn't be applied — the plan changed
  first: 250 lb × 3 sets is in effect."). With several maxima, restoring an old group is a state-4 selection
  covering every maximum. A blind P2 authority never invents replacement values — it applies only the sealed
  group the request commits to. At most one compensation exists per undo request; a retry returns the
  existing transaction and its current reduction status. Undo never overwrites a later plan transaction.
A6 CONSENT FRESHNESS vs DECLINE RECURRENCE — two commitments, never one. PROPOSAL DEPENDENCY BASIS: a keyed
  digest over the exact proposal-bearing facts, the affected plan domains, guards, the SETTLED external
  eligibility decisions (never this instance's own response, apply attempt or unresolved conflict), the
  evaluation local date/time/zone (the D7 ladder moves without a new operation — rig152 Q6), the reducer/
  schema version, policy versions, the signed proposal_surface_contract_version and the mapper version.
  COVERAGE: every issuance and basis confirmation carries computed_through_watermark, proving the issuing
  current client reduced ALL accepted client operations through that point; authority protocol records
  (issuances, dispositions, receipts, apply attempts, confirmations) never invalidate a basis, and neither do
  SAME-INSTANCE DECISION OPERATIONS — the responses and conflict-resolutions to this very instance are client-
  created but BASIS-NEUTRAL for coverage while remaining members of the decision antichain (rig153 Q1: the naive
  "any client operation" rule let the athlete's own answer pause its application for ever). If any OTHER client
  operation is accepted after that watermark, application PAUSES until a current client syncs through it:
  recomputed basis unchanged → a BASIS CONFIRMATION for the same instance and application proceeds; changed →
  new generation and digests, the old answer retained as non-applied History, fresh consent required. There is NO carry-forward across a changed basis (a basis change changes the digests by
  definition). evidence_generation changes whenever any eligibility-bearing input, evidence identity, offered
  outcome, value, unit, direction, reason, copy parameter or contract version changes; a response is consent
  to consent_digest — the exact thing shown — and to nothing else. RECURRENCE IDENTITY = (proposal_family_id,
  decline_fingerprint_digest): the family is the stable action identity and includes the affected conflict
  domain or lift lineage; the fingerprint covers only lever, direction, magnitude bucket and corridor state and
  is never read globally. A routine new reading may mint a new evidence_generation without changing the
  fingerprint; a declined proposal cannot recur merely because a new reading minted a new generation.
A7 DECLARED P2 LEAKAGE (and what P1 keeps in structured columns): opaque athlete/device identifiers; operation
  count, acceptance order, server accepted_at and dispositions; exact local date, time and offset; schema and
  contract versions, lease ids; class and kind; causal-parent, device-chain and dependency structure; session,
  lineage, target, transaction and source→weight relationships; logical-set and planned-split structure;
  session-relationship and parent-lineage references; per-member provenance flags; proposal-family /
  evidence-generation linkability; issuance/confirmation/response/apply linkability; decline-fingerprint
  linkability; outcome-TOKEN equality and WHETHER an outcome produced a plan effect (only a plan-changing
  decision creates a transaction — the authority sees plan-changing vs Keep/No, never the values, wording,
  lever, direction or magnitude); conflict-domain equality and member-set equality; ciphertext length /
  padding buckets unless payloads are padded; frontier and basis metadata. KEYED COMMITMENTS: every
  P2-visible identifier or commitment derived from sealed low-entropy semantics — identity, family, decline
  fingerprint, offer, consent, basis, member-set, delta, relationship and set-slot tokens — is per-athlete,
  domain-separated and keyed with an athlete-held secret; unkeyed hashes are forbidden (rig151 Q6). TRUST
  BOUNDARY: under P1 the authority independently validates every issuance against the current engine and the
  decrypted frontier before application. Under P2 an engine-version token authenticates a claimed release, not
  the execution of unmodified code: a compromised authorized client holds the athlete's commitment key and
  can mint internally consistent opaque commitments — unless independently verifiable rederivation or
  attestation is added (blueprint), a compromised authorized client is OUTSIDE the P2 integrity guarantee.
  HMAC protects metadata confidentiality; it does not prove correct engine execution. P2 exposes no exact
  choice wording or values — no measurement, free text, athlete label, plan-field value, lever, direction or
  magnitude — beyond the declared plan-changing-versus-Keep/No effect class.

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
B · CLIENT STATES — what the athlete sees · what is committed · what is allowed · how it resolves
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Durability rule (all states): before the UI acknowledges Weigh in, LOG SET, a decision, a correction, a
tombstone, an undo request or FINISH SESSION, ONE durable local transaction writes the operation and its outbox
entry. If that write fails, nothing is acknowledged (state 3). Retries are idempotent by op_id. Copy law:
"Saved" = durable locally under a valid lease · "Sent" = left the phone · "Synced" = disposition stored and
reduced through W · "Applied" = the authority's plan transaction is committed, EFFECTIVE, and durably received.
CROSS-STATE PRECEDENCE — data paint: 18 restore/integrity > 17 standing · write permission: 17 standing > 20
lease > 3 validation/save · machine output: safety and session ambiguity > 7 contract currency > 6 sync
currency · orthogonal: the state-19 rejected count/list never replaces Today's governing state. States 7 and
20 compose: 20 governs writes, 7 governs machine output. State 11 permits logging only while the lease is valid.

1 OFFLINE. Facts, corrections, reclassifications, tombstones and direct plan edits: fully writable under the
  durability rule while the lease is valid (state 20 otherwise). TWO LAYERS on the face, each with its own
  label, never mixed: LAYER 1 — the exact locally entered fact or the complete athlete-authored plan group,
  INCLUDING ITS NUMBERS, "Saved on this phone · not yet synced" with a running count visible from Today; a
  plan group the athlete edited offline is the LIVE plan group for that domain — never the superseded
  last-sync value (rig150 Q2). LAYER 2 — derived numbers (trend, pace, corridor, maintenance, the instruction)
  and MACHINE-proposed plan state: from the last complete AUTHENTICATED frontier only, labelled "as of <last
  sync>"; for Layer 2, local operations may only WITHDRAW or SUPPRESS — they never create a derived number, a
  machine verdict or a machine instruction ([R4]). DEPENDENCY-AWARE WITHDRAWAL: every Layer-1 operation is
  checked against each Layer-2 output's declared dependency set; if it touches that set, the affected machine
  instruction, proposal and action locus are suppressed immediately and any retained number is historical
  evidence only, labelled "as of"; the direct athlete plan edit is the sole current plan for that domain
  (Sol 8: last sync said "Keep 8,000 steps"; the athlete set 10,000 offline — the stale "Keep 8,000" leaves
  the face); unrelated Layer-1 operations do not suppress unaffected outputs. Also local: a declared illness/
  pain guard, a correction, an event, or passage through the D7 ladder (3–6 missing dates: pace shown as not
  current; ≥7: Re-entry suppresses current trend, rate, maintenance and proposals) takes effect at once. A
  projection labelled "as of" never drives a current action after a locally known guard or ladder transition.
  A Layer-1 plan edit may still lose a state-4 conflict at sync; the face says so then. TWO DIFFERENT ACTIONS,
  never confused: a DIRECT plan mutation is Layer 1 live; a RESPONSE to a machine proposal is "Answer saved on
  this phone · takes effect after sync" and alters no live plan. A proposal displayed before going offline
  (its issuance) remains answerable only while no local operation touches its dependency basis; otherwise it
  leaves the face and any answer to it becomes non-applied History. OFFLINE UNDO records a durable undo
  request: "Undo saved on this phone · takes effect after sync"; it never claims the authoritative plan
  changed; at sync it applies by compare-and-append (A5) or stays non-applied and the face names the governing
  plan. A proposal not yet displayed cannot appear offline (state 6).
2 SYNC PENDING (online, outbox non-empty). Same labels as 1; automatic retry with backoff; never a spinner as
  the only signal; never blocks logging. DRAIN POINT: an outbox entry leaves active accounting only when the
  client durably stores the authenticated OPERATION DISPOSITION for its op_id (ACCEPTED with athlete_log_seq,
  or REJECTED → state 19) — send completion never drains it; a lost acknowledgement retries the SAME op_id.
  "Synced <time>" appears only after the outbox is empty AND the client has pulled and reduced through
  watermark W (a separate rejected count, state 19, may still be visible); between those: "Changes sent ·
  receiving updates". An operation held as WAITING FOR DEPENDENCY (A2) stays sent-not-accepted; unrelated
  later operations proceed; if its parent never arrives (state 12) it is shown in History as "waiting for an
  earlier entry from <device>" — never dropped, never counted as synced.
3 SAVE FAILED (local write refused: storage full / denied / corrupt / invalid input per the A4 matrix). Loud and
  blocking for that action: "This couldn't be saved on your phone. Nothing was recorded." (or the named missing
  field). One retry action; the entered values stay in the field. Nothing silently dropped; nothing silently
  kept.
4 PLAN-FIELD CONFLICT (concurrent plan transactions in one predeclared conflict domain g; none descends the
  others). At every complete frontier, reduction computes the CAUSAL-MAXIMAL transactions in g from the FULL
  retained transaction DAG — never a pairwise fold (rig147 Q1). One maximum → effective. Several concurrent
  maxima → the one with the lexicographically smallest IMMUTABLE LINEAGE KEY: for transaction t in g, let
  P_g(t) be the causal-maximal accepted plan transactions in g within t's strict causal ancestry; K_g(t) =
  [athlete_log_seq(t)] if P_g(t) is empty, otherwise min_lex{K_g(p) | p ∈ P_g(t)} followed by
  athlete_log_seq(t). Every other maximum remains superseded History. Merge is operation-set union followed by
  this reduction; winner-only folding is forbidden. Only plan transactions in the same domain contribute to K
  (rig149 Q1; 0/4000 property violations). RESOLUTION, any N: the athlete's conflict-selection carries
  seen_conflict_basis = keyed H(domain, the exact causal-maximal transaction ids, every complete alternative
  shown); the authority commits ONE plan transaction whose causal parents are EVERY current causal-maximal
  transaction in g ONLY while the current maximal antichain still equals what the athlete saw (compare-and-
  append); if another maximum arrived first, the selection is retained as non-applied History and the face
  refreshes with all N — an unseen maximum is never silently added as a parent (rig152 Q3; rig151 Q3). COPY,
  parameterized: "Earned found {N} current versions of Press's plan. It is using {group} for now. {N−1}
  alternative{s} {is|are} in History." with, for N = 2, ONE outcome-specific action "Use {alternative}
  instead", and for N > 2 ONE action locus "Review {N−1} alternatives" opening every complete alternative;
  devices are named by their real type ("this phone", "your iPad") — never "phones" unless they were. Never
  "arrived first". No silent loser.
5 PROPOSAL-RESPONSE CONFLICT (two devices answered the same proposal INSTANCE contradictorily and concurrently
  — neither response descends the other). Once both are known, NEITHER response supplies a current plan
  effect: a plan transaction sourced from only one of them becomes CONFLICT-SUSPENDED by reduction — a
  reduction state, not an operation; nothing is deleted, no machine-authored mutation exists, and no Undo is
  attributed to the athlete. History reads "Applied at W1 · conflict-suspended at W2" — never "Undone" or
  "Reversed". FALLBACK while suspended: the effective COMPLETE PLAN PROJECTION of that domain immediately before
  the suspended proposal's effect — whatever its provenance (a direct edit, an earlier consented proposal, a
  conflict resolution or migration; complete-group provenance is on EVERY plan transaction, A3) — excluding any
  value introduced only by the suspended effect and any descendant that INHERITED such a value (rig152 Q4: an
  initial 250 × 3 accepted through a proposal fell to "no accepted plan" under the v1.5 flag rule; it is the
  fallback). A later plan-mutation op counts as COMPLETE VISIBLE AUTHORSHIP only when every invariant member is
  flagged athlete_edited (typed or selected by the athlete, or explicitly confirmed in a step that names every
  member — a design board, never an implied checkbox on Save; default = inherited); a set-count edit on top of
  the suspended 255 lb carries 255 as INHERITED: preserved, not authorship, group unresolved, no dependent
  machine action. With no prior complete group at all, the fallback is "no accepted plan" (state 10). The face
  names what governs: "Two answers arrived. The earlier change to 255 lb × 3 sets is not in effect while this
  is resolved. 250 lb × 3 sets is in effect now." A later mutation never resolves the conflict merely by
  sitting on top.
  DECISION REDUCTION: proposal decisions reduce over responses AND conflict-resolutions together. A resolution
  is effective only when it causally descends the COMPLETE current contradictory DECISION antichain —
  responses and prior resolutions (rig151 Q4); concurrent Yes-resolution and No-resolution operations
  themselves stay unresolved until a later decision covers both; a later or concurrent uncovered decision
  keeps the state unresolved. Outcomes: the plan-changing outcome → state-8 compare-and-append → exactly one
  plan transaction → "Applied" + Undo; Keep/No → a settled decision + decline fingerprint only, settled ONLY as
  the unique causal-maximal decision — NO plan transaction, no "Applied", no Undo (so no no-op transaction can
  enter state 4). "Always applicable" for No means it settles the still-current fingerprint; it never
  suppresses a materially changed one. The UI says "Resolution saved" after local durability, then "Applied"
  only after an EFFECTIVE transaction is durably received. SUPERSESSION: before supersession, an explicit
  resolution may select an old outcome only while the current recomputation reproduces the issuance's
  digests; once terminally superseded, neither old answer can ever be selected or revived — a later matching
  offer is a NEW instance requiring fresh consent. Identical answers canonicalize to one response and at most
  one plan transaction.
  PATHS — ONE RULE FOR BOTH ARRIVAL ORDERS (rig150 Q1): a concurrent contradictory pair to one instance NEVER
  retires that instance by itself and NEVER settles a decline; reduction over the set gives the same result
  whichever arrived first — no effective transaction (one applied in the meantime is conflict-suspended), no
  fingerprint, resolution required. FACE, identical on every device — decline first: "Two different answers
  came from two devices. Neither answer is in effect. 250 lb × 3 sets is in effect while you resolve this.";
  accept first: "Two different answers came from two devices. The change to 255 lb × 3 sets was applied on
  <day> and is on hold; 250 lb × 3 sets is in effect while you resolve this. Choose again." Never "Nothing
  changed" after a transaction existed; no device shows "Applied" or Undo for a suspended transaction.
  B  A LATER COMPLETE ATHLETE EDIT (every member athlete_edited) to the same domain sits on top of the applied
     accept when the decline arrives: the edit governs; the machine transaction is suspended; the edit changed
     the domain, so recomputation cannot reproduce the issuance → TERMINAL SUPERSESSION, no resolution control,
     both answers History-only. Every device: "Two different answers to the same proposal came from two devices.
     Your later change to Press's plan (<device, day>) is in effect; the proposal's change is not. Both answers
     are in History." An edit with any inherited member is NOT this path.
  COPY LAW for this state: after sync every device shows the same sentence naming what is in effect, what is
  History only, and why.
6 PROPOSAL UNAVAILABLE — SYNC. The client knows it is behind (outbox non-empty, or the authority's watermark is
  ahead). Today shows: "Sync to update Today." — never a line that promises a proposal exists. Facts
  recordable; the last-synced MACHINE plan state shown as last synced, the athlete's own saved plan edits shown
  live (state 1). This state hides machine proposals only. Never a proposal computed from a stale basis.
7 PROPOSAL UNAVAILABLE — UPDATE (contract currency). Currency is defined over the signed
  proposal_surface_contract_version (engine, eligibility rules, presentation mapper). State 7 controls MACHINE
  output and proposal eligibility ONLY: Layer-1 facts and complete direct athlete plan edits remain live with
  "Saved on this phone" — state 7 never replaces them with the last accepted plan (Sol 11: the athlete saves
  255 × 3, then learns the engine is obsolete — 250 must not come back over the saved edit). A value or
  instruction produced only by an obsolete truth-affecting contract is SUPPRESSED, or shown clearly historical
  with its version/time (an update that closes event exclusion must not leave the old client's contaminated
  pace on Today as current); local safety and lifecycle withdrawals still apply. THE GOVERNING ACCEPTED PLAN
  STAYS VISIBLE: a still-effective transaction created by an earlier consented proposal remains current plan
  truth and is shown with its provenance and version — state 7 suppresses obsolete derived values, instructions,
  proposals and action loci, never the accepted plan itself. Copy: "Update Earned to keep Today current." Facts
  recordable under the valid lease; no machine proposal. Engine release and cache authority is a blueprint item
  (E).
8 APPLY (the authority's atomic compare-and-append). ISSUANCE FIRST: a proposal may be shown only after the
  current-basis, current-contract client's PROPOSAL ISSUANCE record (A3) is accepted and canonicalized to its
  INSTANCE — two current devices issuing the same offer share one instance (rig151 Q2). Under P1 the authority
  validates the decrypted record against the current engine and frontier; under P2 the exact choices and delta
  stay sealed and the authority commits only the payload previously bound to the issuance. Apply NEVER
  accepts replacement plan fields supplied ad hoc with a response. The athlete's answer is appended ONCE as an
  immutable response operation ("Answer saved") naming the issuance, the chosen outcome and its
  consent_digest. APPLICATION is a separate attempt {apply_request_id, response_op_id, seen_dependency_basis,
  current digests}; it never rewrites or resubmits the response. COVERAGE CHECK (A6): if any client operation
  OTHER than this instance's own decision operations was accepted after the issuance's
  computed_through_watermark, application PAUSES until a current client syncs through it and either confirms
  the basis (proceed) or re-issues (new generation → the old answer is non-applied History, fresh consent). At the compare-and-append boundary the authority reduces every accepted
  causal-maximal DECISION operation (responses and resolutions) for that instance: a contradictory antichain →
  commit nothing and enter state 5; identical → one canonical decision, the same result returned to every
  duplicate; an existing successful transaction → return it; otherwise → commit exactly one. UNIQUENESS: at
  most ONE successful plan effect per instance, regardless of how many response op_ids or issuance records
  exist (rig149 Q2, rig151 Q2). APPLY RESULT, always: {status: effective | conflict_suspended |
  terminally_superseded | non_applied | decision_settled_no_effect, reason_code, evaluated_through_W} — only
  EFFECTIVE produces "Applied" and Undo; decision_settled_no_effect (Keep/No) produces no transaction, no
  "Applied" and no Undo; a lost-reply retry after a concurrent decline returns conflict_suspended, never an old
  success (Sol 10). Every apply attempt is idempotent by apply_request_id (rig147 Q4). Non-applied copy is
  REASON-CODED, never a blanket "new facts": NEW_ENTRIES → "Your answer from <day> couldn't be applied — new
  entries arrived first."; LADDER_OR_DATE → "…the day moved on and the picture changed."; CONTRACT_UPDATE →
  "…Earned was updated first."; each followed by "Here is the current picture." PROVE (spine gate 4): the F→F+1
  race never applies a stale answer; an issuance computed through W10, accepted at W11 and answered at W12
  applies when no client operation arrived after W10. PROVE (Grok B8): evidence_generation — and the digests —
  CHANGE on every basis input (a new eligible reading; an event or illness interval touching the window; a
  matched-intake hole; a SETTLED decline to the same family — a contradicted concurrent decline does not
  retire the instance; a guard; a plan edit to the affected domain; the evaluation date crossing a ladder
  step; a contract version) and do NOT change on unrelated operations (a sleep entry, a note on another lift,
  steps when the lever is intake, dispositions, issuances, confirmations, apply attempts, this instance's own
  response). Both halves mutation-tested. And separately (A6): a new reading changes the generation but NOT the
  decline fingerprint.
9 COLD START. The shell paints the last-synced accepted plan labelled "last synced <time>" as a skeleton; no
  derived number is shown until the engine and the complete local frontier are loaded. PROVE on device: ≤ 2 s
  to first truthful paint on the target phone.
10 FIRST USE (empty account). Onboarding collects, or explicitly leaves absent, every required input:
  eligibility/safety declarations (D11); goal and athlete-declared phase; an explicitly chosen target pace; an
  accepted food/calorie and step plan or "no accepted plan"; current lifts, loads, set counts and rep ceilings
  or "no accepted training plan"; a dated body-composition or lean-mass source with provenance and uncertainty
  (A4) or none; review weekday. A ruled default may be OFFERED, never silently accepted. Today reads "first
  use, no accepted plan" — never "stands" or "Keep" — until the athlete accepts an initial plan transaction.
  "Start from today's session" records session FACTS only; afterwards the app may offer those facts as an
  explicit initial-plan proposal with equal, unselected choices. Logging never creates or accepts a plan.
  LEAN MASS AND PROTEIN (PROTEIN = A ratified: an athlete-entered estimated body-fat interval, labelled "your
  estimate", is an eligible source):
  A. DERIVATION (corrected — v1.5's "linked weight × central body fat" computed FAT mass, rig152 Q2). For
     canonical body weight W and body-fat FRACTION interval [b_low, b_high] with optional central b_point:
       lean_mass_low = W × (1 − b_high) · lean_mass_high = W × (1 − b_low) · lean_mass_point = W × (1 − b_point)
     and with a weight interval [W_low, W_high]: lean_low = W_low × (1 − b_high), lean_high = W_high × (1 −
     b_low). At 160 lb, 12–16 %, central 14 %: 134.4–140.8 lb, point 137.6 lb. A direct lean-mass source uses
     its own point/endpoints. Full precision; convert units afterwards; round the displayed lower endpoint down
     and the upper endpoint up. A body-fat source is eligible only when linked to an eligible body-weight fact
     under the declared effective-date / matching-window rule (linked_weight_op_id) or carrying attested total
     mass; a direct lean-mass measurement needs no separate weigh-in. The protein INTERVAL propagates from the
     complete lean-mass interval under the applicable policy. PRESENTATION ANCHOR: when no source central
     estimate exists, ONE shared presentation-only anchor — the MIDPOINT of the lean-mass interval — derives the
     lean-mass, protein and floor display points consistently, labelled "midpoint" (not "about"); it never
     supplies eligibility, an observation, a plan value or an action constraint. The point never replaces or
     clamps the range and is never stored. (rig149 Q3 engine-delta items stand.)
  B. FACE — OWNER RULING 2026-09-01: every estimate-derived figure is POINT + RANGE ON THE FACE, the calorie
     pattern — "about 160 g · 150–166 g from your estimate" (or "midpoint 160 g · 150–166 g" when the anchor is
     the midpoint). COPY BRANCHES, all drawn: D13 unresolved → no protein figure and no promise ("Protein
     guidance isn't ratified for Earned yet."); policy inapplicable to this athlete → "Earned's protein rule
     doesn't apply to your situation yet."; missing source → "Protein appears after you add an eligible lean-
     mass source."; missing matched weight/attested mass → "Add a weigh-in from the same eligible period to use
     this body-fat estimate."; another gate failed → the named gate; all gates passed → point + range. Why
     names source kind, author, effective date, linked weight identity, estimate range, lean-mass range,
     protein range, the policy id/version and, when used, the presentation anchor.
  C. PUBLIC POLICY — OPEN DECISION D13, ruled BEFORE any public protein fixture, mapper copy or design
     candidate (not merely before the father test). A personalized protein output is emitted only when a
     separately RATIFIED protein policy applies to this athlete; D13 must state whether the instruction is a
     MINIMUM, a TARGET INTERVAL or another rule, how its action constraint derives from the evidence interval,
     the formula, units, eligible population, gates and research basis. Without it the engine ABSTAINS even
     when lean mass is available. 2.5 g/kg FFM is customer #1's rule and never silently the public default.
  D. ACTION CONSTRAINT. Every estimate-derived constraint carries display_point, evidence_range and
     action_constraint {kind: MINIMUM | MAXIMUM | TARGET_INTERVAL, value_or_interval, unit, policy_id,
     policy_version, uncertainty_rule}; ONLY action_constraint gates a proposal; Why names the OPERATIVE bound
     or interval per kind — the minimum, the maximum, or the target interval Earned actually enforces. The FLOOR POLICY is OPEN DECISION D14 (name it or rule it); until ruled, floor-
     dependent proposals abstain. Entering a source records EVIDENCE only; it never accepts a protein-plan
     mutation. A source is necessary, not sufficient, for any other lean-mass-derived output.
  MIGRATION (customer #1): a lean-mass point plus one weight determines only an implied body-fat point — not
     uncertainty endpoints or authorship. A current source is created only when the manifest identifies the
     exact weight operation, the original source/author, the uncertainty identity/domain, the date and the
     correction state; a coach's-eye estimate remains a LEGACY THIRD-PARTY ESTIMATE unless the athlete
     explicitly re-attests it — the ATHLETE, in the athlete role, never "the owner". The migration preserves
     the frozen ledger's EXACT role and consent status for every value: derived guidance (the current 160 g
     protein target IS derived, under the owner's personal policy) is migrated as derived guidance with its
     policy id, never called "accepted"; an active accepted target (the intake band, the step target) stays an
     active accepted target, never demoted to History. Where the pair cannot be reconstructed, the derived
     protein figure is shown as "current derivation unavailable" and no new point/range is emitted until the
     athlete re-attests the evidence.
11 SIGN-IN REQUIRED (authentication session expired on a still-enrolled, locally decryptable device). Logging
  continues to the outbox while the lease is valid; sync pauses; one quiet line "Sign in to sync". Never
  blocks a set. Any other loss of standing is state 17, not this.
12 REINSTALL / STORAGE LOSS / NEW PHONE. Sign in → full resync from the authority (state 18 flow). Operations
  still in the lost device's outbox are gone unless that device returns and replays them (idempotent). The
  "not yet synced" count is the athlete's only warning, so it is never hidden.
13 BACKGROUND KILL MID-SET. Logged sets are operations (durable). On resume, the active session is rebuilt from
  operations and the face shows the last confirmed boundary: "Last saved: Set 1 of Press." Only when a
  durable unfinished-draft marker exists may it add "An unfinished Set 2 entry was not saved." The runtime
  never asserts a draft it did not durably mark. No ghost, no zero.
14 TWO OR MORE DEVICES, POSSIBLY ONE WORKOUT. Every set targets an immutable session-start identity and carries
  a logical set slot; a plan-backed session-start carries planned_split_slot_id, an ad-hoc start carries AD_HOC
  and copy never invents "Monday lower" for it. CANDIDATE RELATION: at each complete basis, compatible live
  session starts (across devices, adjacent local dates / offset changes and the state-15 clock-skew envelope —
  23:59 and 00:01 can be one workout) form ONE connected AMBIGUITY COMPONENT C; date and split never prove one
  physical workout. While C is unresolved, every session in C is progression-bearing NOWHERE: not as a
  synthetic combination and not separately — facts stay visible, but no earn, no next-load result and no
  proposal is emitted (rig147 Q3; Sol: mirrored sessions evaluated separately could mint a false proposal).
  CANDIDATE EDGE (Grok): two starts are candidates only when PLAUSIBLY the same physical workout — same planned
  split slot (or both within one AD_HOC window), local dates equal or adjacent only across the skew envelope
  (23:59 → 00:01), never chained across a week; a week-wide component that suppressed every earn would be a
  candidate-rule defect. DECISION: the family is keyed by {sorted start ids, component generation}; the
  generation ADVANCES on every live-component change — a late start, a tombstone, a reclassification, a
  candidate-rule change — and a new generation supersedes the pending question. Its outcome is a COMPLETE
  PARTITION of C into physical workouts (SAME WORKOUT / DISTINCT WORKOUTS is only the two-start presentation —
  rig152 Q5: pairwise SAME/SAME/DISTINCT over three starts is jointly impossible), and EVERY resulting workout
  block carries its own governing plan basis or NO_ACCEPTED_PLAN; identical concurrent partitions canonicalize;
  contradictory partitions remain unresolved (neither wins silently); a later resolution covers the complete
  decision antichain; an answered component is not asked again unless its generation advances. Ask once, after current-frontier sync: "Two sessions for Monday lower came from this
  phone and your iPad. Were these one workout or two?" (N-way: the partition picker). SAME WORKOUT also selects
  the GOVERNING WORKOUT-PLAN BASIS: if the starts disagree (250 × 3 vs 255 × 4), the athlete selects the
  complete displayed plan actually followed or explicitly attests another complete target — this interprets
  the session, it never mutates the accepted plan. SET COLLISIONS inside a combined workout are resolved per
  COLLISION COMPONENT keyed by {combined-workout identity, sorted live-set membership, generation} — a late set
  advances the generation and invalidates the old partition: the result partitions the live set operations
  into duplicate classes and distinct attempts, selects one canonical operation per duplicate class
  (progression-bearing) and assigns unique slots to distinct attempts (pairwise DUPLICATE / DISTINCT is only
  the two-set UI); contradictory concurrent set-slot decisions reduce the same way; SAME WORKOUT waits for every
  collision in the component to resolve before the line is progression-bearing; DISTINCT WORKOUTS then
  evaluates the sessions separately. A collision resolution only selects the canonical reducer INPUT — it never
  bypasses the completion, safety, lineage, plan-basis or evidence gates that follow. No set
  operation is deleted or rewritten; a later correction is another targeted operation. Only then may History
  say "Sets from two devices were combined." Combined identity derives from immutable start/resolution
  identities; a corrected effective time changes display order, never identity.
15 CLOCK. Effective local date/time/offset is athlete-attested at entry and correctable. SEMANTIC order is
  causal_parents only; athlete_log_seq is the single explicitly declared deterministic tie-break among
  concurrent operations; device_seq and device_predecessor_op_id are transport/replay order and NEVER create
  semantic causality; the device clock is evidence. PROVE: ±24 h skew, equal
  timestamps, DST, travel across zones, offline concurrency — all reduce identically on every device.
16 EXPORT. A complete export is generated from an atomic authoritative snapshot pinned to watermark W and is
  labelled "Complete through W for all synced records" — never an unqualified "complete". A requesting client
  with a non-empty outbox produces a PARTIAL export stating its watermark and the pending operation count, plus
  a device appendix of rejected local entries with reasons (state 19). Entries still saved only on another
  device are unknowable to the authority; the export flow says: "Entries still saved only on another device
  are not included. Sync that device first to include them."
17 SIGNED OUT · DEVICE REVOKED · ACCOUNT CLOSED · DECRYPTION OR RECOVERY UNAVAILABLE. Once known, the app stops
  promising that new operations will sync (no "not yet synced" count that implies a future sync); it preserves
  or purges local material exactly per the recovery/erasure contract (D2 under P1/P2) and names the one
  available resolution ("Sign in again" · "This phone was removed from your account — sign in to re-enrol it"
  · "Recovery needed — <named step>"). WRITE PERMISSION: new writes are REFUSED — the entered values stay in the
  field with the named resolution ("Sign in to keep saving"); nothing is acknowledged as Saved, because nothing
  could sync or (without decryption) be sealed. Never shown as first use.
18 RESTORE REQUIRED. Store integrity is checked before any plan or athlete data paints. Missing, evicted or
  corrupt local storage → "restore required" → sign in → restoring → restored, or failed with the reason.
  Never an empty account presented as first use. If the outbox ledger survived, evicted unsynced operations
  are declared by count; if it did not, the app says it cannot know whether unsynced entries were lost.
19 REJECTED BY THE AUTHORITY (terminal admission failure: cross-athlete reference, identity collision, instance
  collision, malformed, forged / unknown / revoked-beyond-barrier lease or outside its authorized sequence
  range; REJECTED_DEPENDENCY when a required parent was terminally rejected). The authenticated OPERATION
  DISPOSITION (status REJECTED | REJECTED_DEPENDENCY, rejection_code) and the local consequences are ONE
  ATOMIC local transaction — store the disposition, write the REJECTED-LEDGER entry (operation, commitment,
  reason), remove the entry from active retry/outbox accounting (never retried automatically) — or, if that
  transaction cannot complete, truth paint is BLOCKED behind idempotent recovery that completes it; the
  operation is excluded from current facts, plan state and projections; Today shows "N rejected entries on
  this phone" and History each item with its reason; the export appendix includes them. Fixing one creates a NEW standalone domain operation with a new op_id
  (not a correction targeting an operation the authority never accepted; a local-only link may say it
  replaces the rejected entry). The face names the governing truth: "Your 255 lb × 3-set change wasn't
  accepted. 250 lb × 3 sets is in effect." / "This entry wasn't accepted and is not included in Today's
  numbers." Listable while that local ledger exists (reinstall or erasure does not promise restoration unless
  a rejected-item vault is built — blueprint). "Synced" may appear only with the rejected count still visible.
  Never silently dropped. Delivery-order gaps are never rejections (A2: WAITING FOR DEPENDENCY).
20 OFFLINE-WRITE LEASE EXPIRED (the local write cutoff has passed by the monotonic clock, or clock continuity
  cannot be proven after a restore/reboot; A2). New writes are NOT acknowledged: the entry form names what is
  actually required — "Connect once to keep saving" · "Sign in to keep saving" · "Update Earned to keep saving"
  — and why: "Earned can't promise this phone's entries will be accepted after <date> without checking in."
  The values stay in the field; everything already saved under the valid lease stays saved and syncs later.
  Renewal re-enables saving. Never "Saved" for an operation the authority could refuse.

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
C · THE D1 FEASIBILITY GATE — installed iOS PWA, PROVE on the target phone before design freeze
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
  C1 offline cold start + writes (local store durable across relaunch)
  C2 outbox survives process kill
  C3 acknowledged local operations and a seeded non-empty outbox survive ≥ 30 idle days on every supported
     target OS/device, including storage-pressure conditions; store integrity is checked before plan or
     athlete data paints; missing or evicted storage enters restore-required (state 18), never first-use. The
     test proves the tested interval, not a permanent OS exemption. Silent eviction of acknowledged unsynced
     operations FAILS D1 and triggers native.
  C4 update / schema migration is transactional, crash-safe and idempotent and preserves every operation
     identity, envelope, payload, outbox membership and pending count, every disposition, the rejected ledger
     (membership and reasons), the lease and high-water-clock state, key epochs and sealing transitions; inject
     termination before, during and after every migration boundary; failure leaves the prior store readable or
     enters explicit recovery.
  C5 reinstall → sign-in → resync
  C6 reconnect + replay idempotency — PROVE BOTH CUTS: request lost before acceptance → replay accepts exactly
     once; reply lost after acceptance → replay returns the original disposition without duplication.
  C7 200 % text and VoiceOver
  C8 background or process kill mid-set, then resume after one hour: every acknowledged set, outbox entry and
     active-session identity survives exactly; rebuild from operations; an unsubmitted draft either survives
     through a declared draft mechanism or the UI shows only the last confirmed boundary (state 13).
  C9 low-storage behaviour = state 3
  C10 cold start ≤ 2 s to first truthful paint
  C11 the offline-write lease is enforced locally by the monotonic clock AND the authorized device_seq range: a
     write attempted after the cutoff, or at the first slot outside the range, is refused with state 20 and
     never acknowledged (test the last valid and the first invalid slot); every operation committed inside the
     lease is admitted after reconnect regardless of arrival time, the only exception being an athlete-declared
     revocation barrier (A2); cases: ±24-hour skew, clock rollback and roll-forward, DST, timezone change,
     reboot, process kill, restored old local state, reconnect after expiry, range exhaustion.
Failure of any row, or a later automatic-data-source need the PWA cannot meet → native trigger (D1).
SCHEDULE: C3 is wall-clock bound (≥ 30 idle days). The soak starts the moment a store stub exists — a bare
installed PWA with the durable store, a seeded outbox and an integrity check is enough; it does not wait for
the real app — and runs in parallel with design and blueprint. Design freeze waits for the soak result.

submission's principal (historical_principal_verified, new_submission_authorized,

INVENTORY; the LINEARIZATION of exact-response release and no-commit refusal decisions with registry mutations
and group commits, the expected-registry-tuple compare-and-append and the cutoff INVARIANT; the ruled effect of

revocation alone never reinterprets evidence already accepted; stopping the evidence is the ordinary
active = false / removal path). THE ONE OBLIGATION each way: the appendix states that the ADMITTED SET is

CHANGES v1.34 → v1.35 (Sol's appendix pass 34 — rig182 T1 and T2 CLOSED, T3 and T4 closed on their
implementation witnesses with the normative text still admitting another path; the row and precedence still
RATIFIED, no runtime-sheet reopening; four findings, every one executed in rig183; the sheet stays closed):
(1) [High] incarnation cardinality was keyed by an UNTYPED producer_id — instance-id allocation and the
one-PRESENT rule read "per producer_id", while source_id and D14.1 method_id share no declared namespace: a
source with source_id = p supplying a 2,001 floor and a D14.1 method with method_id = p registered afterwards —
read literally, adding the method REMOVED the source incarnation (record stale, D14.1 supplies another bound);
read as {kind, producer_id}, both coexist and the RECORD phase still governs (rig183 T1) → producer_key = {kind,
producer_id}; registration_instance_id is allocated monotonically and without reuse PER producer_key; at most
ONE PRESENT incarnation per producer_key, and adding one removes only the prior incarnation of the SAME
producer_key; current_submission_route_id[source_id] selects the unique PRESENT {AUTHORITATIVE_SOURCE,
source_id} incarnation; every incarnation / cardinality defect parameter names the producer_key (§D14-C registry,
§D14-F P0a, §E; v1.7.36 sheet lines 3044–3046, 3065–3068, 3151 and 3820–3824); (2) [High] the fixed-schedule envelope and the
late serialized exact selection did not compose — the envelope waits for nothing, but exact-versus-envelope was
selected only after the prerequisite: a request authorized at arrival whose group had not committed by the
deadline released nothing (authorized) and had no exact result; a revocation then won the serialized race, the
CAS restarted and the request became a refused miss — a late envelope breaks the schedule and exposes the race,
an envelope at the original deadline needs foresight, both is two responses, and blocking the revocation is a
fourth unstated behaviour (rig183 T2) → ONE CLOSED PROTOCOL: every request has ONE terminal response state,
PENDING → EXACT_RELEASED | ENVELOPE_RELEASED; an exact response may win BEFORE the fixed deadline only when its
completion prerequisite is complete AND direct_replay_response_authorized is TRUE at its serialized release
point; AT THE DEADLINE every still-PENDING request atomically becomes ENVELOPE_RELEASED, whatever the reason an
exact response is unavailable — recovery, an uncommitted group, a pending refusal decision, a lost race, or an
unauthorized principal; once ENVELOPE_RELEASED no exact response is ever sent for that request, and an
authorized source retrieves the completed result by REPLAY under the ordinary rule; so an unauthorized request
always receives exactly one envelope at the fixed time, and an authorized-but-slow request receives the SAME
envelope at the same time without making recovery, the commit or the race observable; "an authorized principal
receives the exact result" is now CONDITIONAL on readiness before the deadline (the alternative — an
authorization reservation that makes revocation wait for an eventual exact hand-off — was NOT adopted; a
revocation never waits on an unfinished response) (§D14-C, §E; v1.7.36 sheet lines 2662–2681, 3809 and 3829–3832); (3) [Medium]
REGISTRATION_PRESENCE_MISSING_OR_INVALID was unreachable under the literal validation domain — "every test …
ranges ONLY over PRESENT incarnations" filtered an entry with no presence out BEFORE any test could see it, so a
filter-first reducer returned an EMPTY, non-defective registry while a structure-first one emitted the defect
(rig183 T3: rig182 T3 still had two textual results) → REGISTRY VALIDATION IN THREE STAGES: stage 1, over EVERY
raw entry of the current registry, validates the route identity, the registration_instance_id and
registration_presence and emits the structural defects (ROUTE_IDENTITY_MALFORMED, ROUTE_IDENTITY_COLLISION,
REGISTRATION_PRESENCE_MISSING_OR_INVALID); stage 2 forms the WORKING producer registry from the validated
entries whose presence is PRESENT; stage 3 runs the active, phase, dependency and cardinality tests over that
PRESENT set only; REMOVED history participates only in identity / non-reuse validation, never in P0a membership
or an active cardinality; PRESENT_INCARNATION_CARDINALITY {producer_key, count} joins the closed enum (§D14-F
P0a; v1.7.36 sheet lines 3125–3137 and 3826–3828); (4) [Medium] the current METHOD_DERIVATION EMISSION identity still spelled
the superseded three-field route identity — r1 removed and r2 re-registered under the same producer, feed
version, phase and class differ by registration_instance_id under the general rule yet the explicit emission
identity aliased them, so a candidate identity, diagnostics, membership or an identity-keyed cache could reuse one
identifier across exactly the reincarnation v1.34 separated (rig183 T4) → {kind: EMISSION, route_identity:
{kind, producer_id, registration_version, registration_instance_id}, emitting_phase, class_id}; every explicit
route tuple, sorting key, active-route record and identity-keyed cache carries the quadruple, abstract
"route identity" references inherit it, and the v1.23 entry's "the only key anywhere" triple is marked
SUPERSEDED; the route stamp stays OUT of the source-authored content commitment — that exclusion is intentional
and unchanged (§D14-C; v1.7.36 sheet lines 2532–2534 and 1471). The v1.34 entry's "per producer_id" cardinality and the
v1.33 entry's "an authorized principal receives an exact response" are marked SUPERSEDED in place (v1.7.36 sheet
lines 859 and 950). A pre-delivery adversarial read (rig183 X1–X3) shaped the wording: the deadline is measured from
ARRIVAL by the declared release interval and is the same clock for every request of the source; an
EXACT_RELEASED request whose group later proves incomplete is impossible (its prerequisite was completion); the
athlete client's sync is unaffected by the response state — it carries stored results whenever they exist.

CHANGES v1.33 → v1.34 (Sol's appendix pass 33 — rig181 T1 / T2 / T3 / T4 CLOSED on their witnesses, the row and
precedence still RATIFIED, no runtime-sheet reopening; four alternative normative paths, every one executed in
rig182; the sheet stays closed): (1) [High] an UNVERIFIABLE miss could still create a stub BEFORE
new_submission_authorized — the high-level order put the door after both lookups, but the detailed test order
said "THEN VERIFICATION … → for a currently admitted source a REJECTION STUB", and "currently admitted" was
glossed as a PRESENT registration declaring RECORD, never the presented credential: C1 of removed r1 / epoch e1,
the source re-registered as r2 with RECORD, C1 sends new unverifiable bytes under a transport miss → the
predicate says REFUSED, the verification branch saw PRESENT + RECORD and minted a transport-keyed stub, and with
a parseable claimed scope SOURCE_ITEM_UNREADABLE entered the ledger from unauthorized input (rig182 T1) →
VERIFICATION ONLY REPORTS: VERIFIED {content_identity} or UNVERIFIABLE {reason}, committing nothing; for
UNVERIFIABLE the content lookup is NOT_APPLICABLE and counts as a miss; EVERY miss then passes through
new_submission_authorized; only when it is TRUE may the authority create the transport-keyed rejection-stub
group, under the same expected-registry-tuple compare-and-append; when FALSE, REFUSED with no durable state;
every "currently admitted source" shortcut and every ingestion-order summary now states this rule (§D14-C,

band must clear max(TODAY, PLAN_HORIZON); scheduled or one-day effects are OUT OF SCOPE for v1 and filed to §E as
a successor decision requiring a consent-bound effect interval (a runtime reopening — not adopted) (sheet lines

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
EVIDENCE (verified this session against the primary sources; links at the end)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
E1 Morton et al. 2018, Br J Sports Med 52:376–384 — systematic review, meta-analysis and meta-regression of
   protein supplementation with resistance training in healthy adults: 49 RCTs, 1,863 participants overall.
   The 1.62 g/kg/day breakpoint came from an UNADJUSTED regression of 42 study arms and 723 participants; 95 %
   CI 1.03–2.20 g/kg/day; p = 0.079 (not statistically significant) — cited from the paper's breakpoint section.
   Energy-restricted trials were EXCLUDED: "Only trials with humans who were healthy and not energy-restricted
   were accepted." The authors add: "it may be prudent to recommend ~2.2 g protein/kg/d for those seeking to
   maximise resistance training-induced gains in FFM." It is a HYPERTROPHY-PLATEAU finding, not a measured
   minimum; using 1.6 as a floor the athlete may exceed is Earned's owner-chosen reading, and Why says so.
E2 Helms, Zinn, Rowlands & Brown 2014, Int J Sport Nutr Exerc Metab 24(2):127–138 — systematic review (no
   meta-analysis) of dietary protein during CALORIC RESTRICTION in resistance-trained lean athletes: six studies
   comprising 13 groups; adults, energy-restricted, resistance-trained > 6 months, men ≤ 23 % and women ≤ 35 %
   body fat. Its 2.3–3.1 g/kg FFM range is a QUALITATIVE recommendation. Conclusion, quoted: "Protein needs for
   energy-restricted resistance-trained athletes are likely 2.3–3.1 g/kg of FFM scaled upwards with severity of
   caloric restriction and leanness." and "Athletes with a lower body fat percentage, or a primary goal of
   maintaining maximal FFM should aim toward the higher end of this range."
E3 Energy availability. The IOC REDs consensus statements (2018; 2023 update) carry 30 and 45 kcal/kg FFM/day as
   historical reference zones; the 2023 statement says the universal 30 cutoff is DEBATED, was derived from short
   studies in small samples of sedentary women, and should not be treated as a definitive clinical threshold;
   male disturbance levels are less understood and may be lower (the IOC cites male examples around 9–25
   kcal/kg FFM/day; the male-LEA review separately discusses 15–25).
   EARNED'S READING: 30 kcal/kg FFM/day is adopted as a CONSERVATIVE PRODUCT PROPOSAL-ABSTENTION BOUNDARY informed
   by the historical EA literature — not an IOC-defined universal or male clinical cutoff, not a diagnosis, not a
   clearance line.
NOT VERIFIED (do not print until sourced): the deployed engine's copy "under the 20 line where over 40 % of loss
   comes off muscle" and "the 25 where a lean male spares muscle" — no source located; engine-delta oracle: cite
   or cut.

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
D13 · PUBLIC PROTEIN POLICY — RULED 2026-09-01: OPTION A, "by phase, MINIMUM"
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
policy_id earned.protein.public · version 1 · kind MINIMUM (a floor the athlete may exceed; never a maximum)
GATE ORDER (deterministic, evaluated in this order; the first failure names the branch): (1) D11 eligibility /
  safety, which EXPLICITLY includes adult ≥ 18 (under-18s are outside every public policy) → (2) EFFECTIVE RT PLAN: resistance training on the CURRENT, EFFECTIVE, UNSUSPENDED governing plan
  (first use has no accepted plan, so this gate cannot pass until an initial plan transaction is effective) →
  (3) RESOLVED PHASE: the declared phase is current and resolved (absent, conflicted or unrecognized → abstain)
  → (4) branch-specific evidence.
BRANCHES:
  P1 DECLARED DEFICIT + effective RT plan → raw_minimum = 2.3 g/(kg FFM·day) × lean_HIGH (kg) — the UPPER endpoint of
     the lean-mass interval: the enforced minimum never falls below the 2.3 rule for ANY lean mass inside the
     declared interval (Grok's correction; the same direction as D14). Basis E2 (lower bound of 2.3–3.1).
     Requires an eligible lean-mass source (a measurement, or a body-fat estimate paired with a weigh-in per
     sheet state 10 A) that is CURRENT under the versioned LEAN-SOURCE CURRENCY rule (earned.protein.public v1,
     shared with D14): age_days = evaluation_local_date − source_effective_local_date; a measurement (DEXA and
     the like) is current iff 0 ≤ age_days < 180, an athlete estimate iff 0 ≤ age_days < 90 — a future-effective
     source is never current; beyond the window the source is STALE → abstain with "Refresh your body-composition
     source". RE-ATTESTATION creates a NEW immutable athlete-estimate source operation with current endpoints,
     its own effective date and a fresh eligible linked weight or attested mass; it never renews or redates an
     old measurement — only a new measurement receives the 180-day window. The window and the expiry date are
     disclosed in Why; 90 and 180 are OWNER-CHOSEN PRODUCT LIMITS, not findings from E1–E3. (Rationale: body
     composition moves materially over months in a deficit; the current engine drifts an anchored lean mass
     instead — the public policy abstains.) GOVERNING SOURCE when several are current: exactly ONE source
     operation governs, selected by the versioned reducer — a measurement over an athlete estimate; among the
     same kind the most recent effective date; an exact tie (same kind, same effective date): sources are
     EQUIVALENT only when ALL of these match after canonicalization — quantity kind, canonicalized values and
     units (endpoints and the presence AND value of the central estimate), provenance/author, and the linkage
     semantics (the same linked weight operation, or the same attested total mass); an equivalence class
     CANONICALIZES to one governing source whose REPRESENTATIVE is chosen by immutable order — the lowest
     athlete_log_seq, then the lexicographically smallest op_id — and the representative together with the
     SORTED membership of its class is bound into the dependency basis (two clients never bind different
     bases); the conflict ENDS when the highest-priority candidates reduce to ONE equivalence class, not only
     when one physical operation remains. Materially DIFFERENT tied sources (any equivalence field differs,
     provenance included — Why must name differing provenance) → abstain with BODY_COMPOSITION_SOURCE_CONFLICT
     and COUNT-NEUTRAL copy, total for every N ≥ 2 ("Your body-composition entries for {date} disagree ({N}
     versions) — correct or remove entries until one version remains"; N = the number of highest-priority
     equivalence classes; rig156 Q1: three same-date measurements make three classes and removing one leaves
     two) until existing corrections or tombstones leave one class (no new selection operation exists in
     the frozen schema, and none is added) (Sol's witness: a current measurement at 60–62 kg and a current estimate
     at 68–72 kg would otherwise both pass the gate with materially different minimums). This reducer, its
     conflict branch and its basis binding are SHARED by D14 verbatim. evidence_scope: MATCHED_BY_DECLARATION when the athlete is
     resistance-trained > 6 months, inside E2's body-fat limits, and D11 explicitly establishes "healthy" — the
     deficit is matched by the athlete's DECLARED phase, which does not establish observed caloric restriction,
     and Why says so; EXTRAPOLATED otherwise (novices, body fat above the limits, or an unestablished health
     match) — eligibility stays broad, and Why says explicitly that E2 did not establish the rule in that
     population. Why also
     states the full 2.3–3.1 g/kg FFM range (leaner athletes or a maximal-FFM goal argue for the upper end; the
     enforced minimum stays 2.3 — scaling with leanness or with the severity of the caloric restriction is a
     versioned successor, never a silent adjustment) and that the enforced number is computed on the high lean
     endpoint.
  P2 NOT IN A DEFICIT (maintenance or surplus) + effective RT plan → raw_minimum = 1.6 g/(kg body mass·day) ×
     body weight (kg), from the CURRENT weigh-in — a POINT: the schema defines no interval-bearing weight source, so
     no range is invented — P2 v1 is POINT-SOURCE-ONLY; interval-bearing weights require a successor policy
     version that defines their propagation, mapper and uncertainty behaviour. CURRENCY (versioned,
     earned.protein.public v1) follows the D7 counter directly: eligible while the missing-date count is < 7;
     at ≥ 7 missing dates abstain with "weigh in to refresh your protein minimum"; a reading whose effective
     date is in the future abstains with date-correction copy ("This weigh-in is dated in the future — correct
     its date"). Requires only a current body-weight fact — no body-fat source — so an athlete with a current,
     effective, unsuspended RT plan but no body-composition source gets a number in this phase. Why states E1's base (42 arms / 723 participants), that energy-restricted trials
     were excluded, the CI 1.03–2.20 and p = 0.079, that it is a gain-plateau finding read as "sufficient at
     about 1.6" and an owner-chosen minimum, and the authors' note that ~2.2 g/kg may be prudent when the goal
     is maximal muscle gain.
  P3 no effective RT plan, or eligibility not met (D11), or phase unresolved → ABSTAIN with the reason named.
TYPED FORMULAS (coefficients dimensional; every quantity computed once at full precision in SI, quantized once):
  P1  c1 = 2.3 g/(kg FFM·day)
      lean_anchor_kg = the derived central lean mass when the source has a central estimate, otherwise
                       midpoint(lean_low_kg, lean_high_kg) labelled "midpoint"
      display_point_g   = c1 × lean_anchor_kg
      evidence_range_g  = [c1 × lean_low_kg, c1 × lean_high_kg]
      raw_minimum_g     = c1 × lean_high_kg
  P2  c2 = 1.6 g/(kg body mass·day)
      display_point_g   = c2 × weight_kg (the dated point weigh-in)
      evidence_range    = absent: POINT_SOURCE_NO_INTERVAL
      raw_minimum_g     = c2 × weight_kg
  QUANTIZATION (bound, versioned): each policy declares constraint_grain {value, unit, origin} — protein v1:
  {1, g/day, 0}, FIXED — a plan's own coarser grid never alters the policy grain; as in D14, such a grid only
  determines the first representable PASSING plan value, ceil_{q_plan, o_plan}(operative_minimum); ceil_g(x) = g · ceil(x / g); action_constraint.value = ceil_g(raw_minimum) — that EXACT quantized value governs every
  proposal and is the value shown on the face (one number for shown truth and gating truth); NO intermediate
  rounding anywhere (unit conversion, lean derivation and coefficient multiplication happen at full precision);
  display: range endpoints round outward (lower down, upper up), the point rounds to nearest with ties
  half-away-from-zero. Sol's witness, recomputed: 160 lb (72.5748 kg) at 12–16 %, central 14 % → lean 60.963 /
  62.414 / 63.866 kg (approximations of 60.962814528 / 62.414310112 / 63.865805696) → point 143.5529132576 g →
  shown 144; range 140.2144734144–146.8913531008 → shown 140–147; raw minimum 146.8913531008 → operative 147 g.
FACE (the owner's "point + range on the face" ruling kept as the evidence line) — MAPPER SPLIT, worked on a
