  day record's reason field IS the day reducer's (3T) typed STAGE RESULT for the SAME cell and basis — nonempty
  blocking, empty payload — never the D14_POLICY_CHECK_RESULT (which needs a proposal band a day record does not
  have); an UNKNOWN record never re-enters candidate reduction. THE AUTHORITATIVE CODE TABLE is ordered in TIERS, and the STRICT HEADLINE RANK is
  the tier order then the order within the tier (D = day-only, C = class-only; every code is blocking unless
  marked ○; ◇ = defaultable only when it is the SOLE blocking code):
  tier 1 (gate 1): INELIGIBLE_D11 · tier 2 (gate 2): LEAN_SOURCE_NONE · LEAN_SOURCE_UNPAIRED ·

  SOURCE_ITEM_UNREADABLE. No code
  appears in two tiers;
  every failure has exactly one code; a P0a plan, partition or registry failure or a P0b domain failure and any later-tier finding never meet — both are
  terminal, and P1 (which runs between them) carries findings with blocking [] and no headline of its own, so
  the table is a RANK, not 3P's evaluation order. Scope keys: the civil_cell_key {local date, evaluation zone, zone-rules version} is the
  ONLY day identity in D14 (the basis's frozen fields); occurrence facts carry {local date, local time, UTC
  offset} for their endpoints and never a zone — their attested local dates are never a day key.
§D14-B · LOCAL-DAY EQUATION AND OCCURRENCE MODEL (per athlete-local day; never a weekly average — a low-EA
  training day must not hide): NET EEE = the energy expended ABOVE ordinary daily living during the exercise
  bout (the cited construct); EEE_day = net, NON-OVERLAPPING exercise energy for that day across EVERY exercise
  modality: (a) resistance sessions logged that day; (b) cardio / sport bouts logged that day; (c) exercise
  walking from an INTERVAL-BEARING walk record — aggregate steps {count} carry no exercise classification and no
  interval and NEVER create an occurrence (rig161 Q5); the step term stays the separate, owner-added guard,
  named as such, not IOC-derived EEE. FROZEN FIELDS ONLY (rig163 Q1): every endpoint of an occurrence is the
  sheet's athlete-attested {effective local date, local time, UTC offset} (A3/A4 — for an event's `interval`,
  under the two-endpoint reading of §D14-C); no IANA zone exists on the
  frozen operations and none is invented. An endpoint converts to an INSTANT (local time + offset) for every
  comparison — never wall time (rig161 Q5: a DST fold repeats 01:30 sixty minutes apart; rig160 Q4: back-to-back
  [17:00, 18:00) and [18:00, 19:00) do not overlap). THE OCCURRENCE SET O = every live, current occurrence fact
  of the day (or, for a plan class, every planned bout; the NO_PLANNED_BOUTS variant has O = ∅ by declaration),
  each with a SOURCE-DERIVED identity and its OWN HALF-OPEN INTERVAL [start, end) of attested endpoints taken
  from the fact itself, never from an estimate's claim — and OCCURRENCES ARE CONSTRUCTED AFTER the sheet's
  state-14 session-relationship reduction: fragments resolved SAME are ONE occurrence whose FOOTPRINT is the
  canonical finite union of DISJOINT half-open spans (never a hull — a hull would invent elapsed time and could
  manufacture overlap; two fragments 23:50–23:59 and 00:01–00:10, each contained in its own cell, touch two
  cells once declared one physical workout and abstain as CROSS_DAY); DISTINCT keeps separate occurrences; every
  interval rule in this appendix reads "span" as such a footprint; an UNRESOLVED component suppresses D14
  UPSTREAM under the sheet's own state-14 abstention and never produces a D14 code; every derived finding here
  is recomputed on any change of state-14 generation or partition, plan basis, or evaluation zone. THE QUERIED
  CIVIL-DAY CELL (rig165 Q1, rig166 Q1): a cell is the INVERSE IMAGE of a local date under the named zone and
  zone-rules version — the set of instants whose local date in that zone is that date — identified by the
  canonical civil_cell_key {local date, evaluation zone, zone-rules version}; Today's cell is the basis's evaluation local
  date under the basis's EVALUATION ZONE (a frozen basis field: the sheet's evaluation local date/time/zone) with
  the zone-rules version bound; the inverse image is a CANONICAL FINITE UNION OF DISJOINT HALF-OPEN INSTANT
  RANGES — one range on an ordinary day (25 hours on a fall-back day, 23 on a spring-forward day), more when the
  zone's rules fold a date around another (rig167 G5, tz data: America/Goose_Bay 2010-11-06 is [Nov 6 03:00Z,
  Nov 7 03:00Z) ∪ [Nov 7 03:01Z, Nov 7 04:00Z), the minute between belonging to Nov 7 before the clock jumps
  back — a single-range cell would route that minute into Nov 6); every routing predicate intersects with the
  whole union; and a wholly skipped date (Pacific/Apia 2011-12-30) has an EMPTY cell — no such civil
  day exists and nothing can route into it (the evaluation date's cell is nonempty by construction, the device
  being on a real date); a fixed-offset cell would lose exercise on a transition day (New York 2026-11-01: a basis evaluated at
  −05:00 makes a 24-hour cell starting 05:00Z, excluding a valid 00:30 −04:00 occurrence of that same local date
  — an attested rest day would then read ZERO_ATTESTED over real exercise; the civil cell runs 04:00Z → 05:00Z
  next day, 25 hours, and contains it); occurrence facts still need only their frozen offsets. DAY ROUTING
  (rig164 Q1) — ONE predicate for every VALID closed, open or conflicting span against the queried cell
  (exclusive end): NOT_TOUCHED when the instant intersection is empty; CONTAINED when [start, end) ⊆ cell;
  otherwise CROSS_DAY_BOUT_UNALLOCATED for that cell — so [Sep 1 23:00, Sep 2 00:00) −04:00 is contained in Sep 1
  and does not touch Sep 2, [Sep 1 23:00, Sep 3 01:00) −04:00 is CROSS_DAY on Sep 1, Sep 2 AND Sep 3, and a bout
  attested Sep 2 00:30–01:30 +14:00 is CONTAINED in a queried Sep 1 cell of a fixed −10:00 zone (endpoint local dates never
  decide membership; instants do). A day record's header is the cell's civil_cell_key. THE OCCURRENCE STAGE (R0) runs
  before either reducer and is nondefaultable; it routes DIAGNOSTICS separately from spans (rig165 Q2: an
  invalid interval has an empty intersection with every cell and would otherwise vanish as NOT_TOUCHED): a
  session or bout with no closing endpoint is the span [start, ∞); more than one closing endpoint is the UNION
  of the VALID candidate spans; a close with end ≤ start contributes a DIAGNOSTIC POINT at the start instant,
  and whenever valid_close_count = 0 — one invalid close or several — the unresolved remainder [start, ∞) stays
  in place beside INVALID (and CONFLICT when there are several closes) until a valid close or a correction
  exists (rig166 Q2, rig167 G2: a Sep 1 23:00 start with a lone 22:00 close, or with closes at 22:00 and 21:00,
  otherwise left Sep 2 untouched and open to ZERO_ATTESTED); an open span is
  represented finitely and symbolically as {start cell, observed_through: the evaluation cell, provisional:
  true} — it touches every NONEMPTY cell from its start cell through the evaluation cell (by the instant
  predicate; an empty cell of a skipped date is never touched) and never beyond;
  OCCURRENCE_INTERVAL_MISSING is reported for every queried cell an open span touches,
  OCCURRENCE_INTERVAL_CONFLICT for every cell the union touches or that contains the diagnostic point of an
  invalid close among several closes, and OCCURRENCE_INTERVAL_INVALID for the cell containing the diagnostic
  point (tested as instants, never by enumerating local dates: rig163 Q1, a start attested 2026-09-02 00:30
  +14:00 and an evaluation cell 2026-09-01 in a fixed −10:00 zone have a reversed local-date order yet intersect); a
  CROSS_DAY cell abstains — there is NO per-day allocation in v1 (rig161 Q4: allocating a 23:50–00:50 bout
  wholly to its start day gave the next day 1,601 where its real exercise needs 2,101 and a 1,700 band slipped
  through); splitting is the successor allocation policy (§E). PLANNED CLASSES carry NO
  clock intervals in the frozen plan (a recurring Monday 18:00 class has no immutable instant across DST), so
  D14 v1 states a DISCLOSED POLICY ASSUMPTION — planned bouts fall inside their class day (Why says so on every
  plan-horizon figure, and says which workouts the check covers) — never an athlete attestation, never plan state, never re-attestable (rig164: an
  attestation would be hidden runtime state with no frozen operation, consent surface or Undo). The HORIZON
  GUARD checks that assumption against observation and is a CURRENT DERIVED FINDING, never stored state
  (rig165 Q3, rig167 G1): the GOVERNED SET is defined by LOG ORDER from frozen fields only — no wall-clock
  "effect instant" exists in the frozen schema and none is needed. seq(plan) — the PARTITION-ESTABLISHED SEQ —
  is defined over the plan context P0a admits (§D14-F): the EFFECTIVE projection, or the FALLBACK projection
  while the partition-bearing domain is conflict-suspended — an unresolved state-4 antichain is
  EXERCISE_PLAN_UNRESOLVED_OR_SUSPENDED at P0a and never reaches the guard, so no "winner for now" is ever a
  context here: take the PARTITION CONTENT of that projection = {the class ids, the day → class map, and the
  set of stable planned-slot ids assigned to each day} — the frozen split fields that say WHICH planned slot
  falls on WHICH day, exactly what the class-day ASSUMPTION is about, fixed here and never method-relative;
  a slot's CONTENTS (its lifts, loads, set counts, rep ceilings, durations, intensity inputs) and the walking /
  step rule are NOT content — they move class bounds, never which day a bout falls on — and content only, never
  plan version, generation or transaction identity; P0a's resolved-and-unsuspended check covers the split /
  partition domain (the guard's content); the input domains of the class-bound producers are checked at P0b,
  AFTER P1 has bound which producer is active for each class (rig170 T1: before P1 no normative input-domain set
  exists — one reading let an unresolved per-lift domain of the active method escape, another let an unused
  method's conflict block a valid result); between them, no lineage winner of any domain ever governs the
  guard or a class bound; the CONTENT HISTORY is traced under the CURRENT
  reduction's classification, never re-evaluated per historical frontier: the list is every plan transaction,
  in every domain the content reads, in the CAUSAL ANCESTRY of the governing projection's current maxima,
  in athlete_log_seq order, each with the partition content of the projection AFTER it — a superseded state-4
  maximum is such an ancestor and STAYS LISTED with its content (a contested window whose alternative
  differed resets the guard: T1 100/P1, T2 150/P2, resolution 300/P1 → 300); removed are exactly the sheet's
  fallback exclusions — a currently conflict-suspended transaction and every descendant that inherited its
  values — and nothing else (no truncation at the suspended effect: T1 100/P1 → T_s 200/P2 suspended → a
  per-lift transaction 400 is not listed at all — another domain — and the content after T1 stays P1 →
  seq(plan) = 100, never an empty set); seq(plan) = the SMALLEST athlete_log_seq T in that list such that the content
  after EVERY listed transaction with seq ≥ T equals the current content (any domain, any provenance of the
  sheet's enum — authored | consented | resolved | migrated, an undo compensation included whatever its tag;
  authority-appended records carry their own athlete_log_seq under A3); a transaction that changes no field the content reads (a load,
  set-count, rep-ceiling, duration or intensity change; a walking-rule change), a no-op re-save, a resolution re-selecting the same group or a
  compensation that restores an unchanged partition therefore never moves seq(plan), and the guard's evidence
  is reset as a whole only when the partition content changes — single findings still dissolve by correction
  or tombstone, a state-14 repartition or a zone change (rig167 G1). athlete_log_seq is a strict total order over the accepted
  log (state 15 makes it the sole deterministic tie-break, which requires distinct values), so ">" is total.
  constituent_operations(occurrence) is defined for EVERY modality: the occurrence's SOURCE FACT(S) — a session's
  start / set / skip / close operations and any relationship resolution over them; a cardio / sport bout's event
  operation; an exercise-walking occurrence's interval-bearing walk record (an operation of the frozen event
  class with a walking type and its interval — never the aggregate steps {count} record) — plus every
  correction, reclassification or tombstone TARGETING one of them; a targeting operation is LIVE while it
  stands (not itself superseded by a later correction or tombstone), so a set tombstoned or corrected after
  seq(plan) makes its pre-plan session governed — "added or changed" means exactly this (rig169 S1: under a session-only list a cardio event at
  seq 120 and a walk record corrected at seq 130 were ungoverned and could never raise the class finding). A live
  occurrence (post-state-14) is GOVERNED iff ANY LIVE constituent has athlete_log_seq > seq(plan): the conservative
  direction for a workout that straddles the plan's adoption in log order; log order is not performance order,
  so a workout performed under an older plan but accepted after seq(plan) IS governed and, if it crossed a day,
  stands as a closed finding until the occurrence is corrected or the partition content changes — said in Why
  ("Plan-day checks cover workouts added or changed in the accepted log since these day classes were set."); a response accepted Sep 1 whose
  transaction the authority commits Sep 3 governs from that transaction's seq, never from the response's
  accepted_at or attested time, and state 1 has no plan effect before sync because the transaction does not yet
  exist; MIGRATION: the data port (§E, blueprint) MUST append the migrated plan transaction AFTER every ported
  historical operation or authority record that can belong to constituent_operations(occurrence) — facts,
  corrections, reclassifications, tombstones and relationship resolutions alike (rig170 T4: a plan appended
  after the facts but before a ported historical correction at seq 110 made that occurrence governed) — so
  customer #1's history is ungoverned unless a genuinely post-port operation touches a ported occurrence of any
  modality; otherwise only post-port workouts are checked — a disclosed port-order requirement, not a timestamp; every governed occurrence is routed against the
  civil-day cell of local_date(start instant, evaluation zone), never the fact's attested local date, whatever
  that cell is (a cell outside the partition's domain has no class and marks nothing); an occurrence that is not CONTAINED marks
  the class of every governed day cell it touches CLASS_CROSS_DAY_BOUT_OBSERVED {plan_basis, generation,
  class_id, occurrence id, combined temporal footprint, touched cells} — blocking the plan constraint,
  class-keyed, recomputed against the CURRENT plan basis at every evaluation and bound into the basis; a
  PROVISIONAL finding from a still-open session [start, ∞) clears by itself when an ordinary frozen
  session-close makes the span contained; a finding from a CLOSED cross-day occurrence never clears by the
  passage of time — it is recomputed at every evaluation and disappears only when its inputs change: a
  correction or tombstone of the occurrence, a state-14 repartition or generation that dissolves the footprint,
  a plan-basis change (seq(plan) or the partition) or an evaluation-zone change (rig162 Q3: two overnight bouts each landing 500 kcal on Tuesday gave per-class floors of 2,101
  while Tuesday's real EEE-high of 1,000 requires 2,501 — a 2,200 band had passed the plan horizon).
§D14-C · RECORDS. The EEE DAY RECORD is a DISCRIMINATED UNION with a COMMON HEADER on EVERY variant —
  {civil_cell_key (the queried cell's canonical key; never a bare {local date, UTC offset} pair — rig166 Q1),
  status} — and a status-specific body: ZERO_ATTESTED {low =
  point = high = 0, unit, source, completeness_basis, zero_provenance, joint_uncertainty_basis =
  CARTESIAN_ZERO_ATTESTED — the SOLE canonical zero set J_zero = [lean_low, lean_high] × {0}, no alternative
  dependence declaration exists, bound with the lean-source and completeness bases (rig157 Q2: without it one
  conforming implementation inferred a Cartesian set and another rejected the record as undeclared — with it,

  (finite, 0 ≤ low ≤ point ≤ high, high > 0), unit, source, method, version, coverage, completeness_basis,
  overlap_basis, joint_uncertainty_basis, candidate_origin (below), derivation_commitment (METHOD_DERIVATION
  only), aggregation: DIRECT | SUM (METHOD_DERIVATION and AUTHORITATIVE_RECORD — DIRECT: the method estimated one whole and its components are inputs only; SUM: the
  whole is the sum of its components), derivation_components[] (both non-operation origins: EVERY component the method
  combined, typed {component id, role CONSUMED_PARTIAL | FILLED | OTHER, coverage ids, the component's own
  lean–EEE joint set — a consumed partial's is its record's, a FILLED or OTHER component's is declared here in
  the same joint-set schema}, sorted — rig167 G8; a component is UNCERTAINTY-BEARING iff its declared joint set
  is not point-degenerate in EEE, derived from the set, never a self-declared flag), cross_partial_basis (the
  SHARED-LEAN COUPLING of §D14-E R4 — the ONE schema — a finite union of closed boxes over (lean, EEE₁ … EEEₙ)
  with the sorted component identities bound to the axes, each axis's unit and the lean binding; CARTESIAN is
  the product coupling; validated at the screen against every component's declared joint set; REQUIRED iff
  aggregation = SUM and two or more components are uncertainty-bearing, with EVERY summed component an axis;
  absent otherwise — with at most one uncertainty-bearing component the sum is that component's set translated
  by the point sum, exact without a coupling), filled_components and evidence_ids (sorted; present on a D14.1 dated output only)}
  — the DERIVED-CANDIDATE FIELDS {derivation_commitment, aggregation, derivation_components[], cross_partial_basis
  when required, and — for a STORED derivation IN THE CLASS DOMAIN — consumed_plan_domains} are carried by EVERY
  METHOD_DERIVATION candidate of either domain — the BOUNDED day record, the EEE_CLASS_BOUND and both D14.1
  outputs (the class emission and the dated default) alike — and all but the commitment and
  consumed_plan_domains by every AUTHORITATIVE_RECORD. EVERY ESTIMATE candidate (zero proofs are not candidates: they carry
  their provenance kind and the zero-proof key instead) carries ONE exclusive candidate_origin (rig170 T5): OPERATION_BACKED (a frozen operation; none for estimates in
  v1) | AUTHORITATIVE_RECORD (a source's already-complete IMMUTABLE bound or day total, identified and ordered by
  {source_id, record identity} — the record identity is the SOURCE ITEM IDENTITY of §D14-C (source_item_id,
  the source's own immutable id, made unique per source by ingestion's replay rule), the record is stamped at

  under and carries its LINEAGE fields (lineage_slot, supersedes_source_item_id — the LINEAGE RULE of §D14-C);
  it carries aggregation, derivation_components[] and any required coupling when it
  combined components, and NEVER a derivation_commitment) | METHOD_DERIVATION (any candidate COMPUTED from plan
  inputs or partial estimates — a D14.1 output, or a source record computed from plan inputs — which carries the

  CANDIDATE RECORD IDENTITY, total per origin (rig171 U4): OPERATION_BACKED (athlete_log_seq, op_id);
  AUTHORITATIVE_RECORD (source_id, record identity); METHOD_DERIVATION {kind: STORED, owner: THE_APP | source_id,
  record id — the immutable id of a materialized derived record: the app's own immutable output id for a D14.1
  dated output (owner THE_APP; it carries neither consumed_plan_domains nor lineage fields — both FORBIDDEN on

  registration_instance_id} (rig183 T4: the three-field spelling aliased a removed incarnation and its
  same-version successor), emitting_phase,
  class_id} for a standing emission, which is never stored and is unique per evaluation because a route emits
  at most one candidate per class — defined for EVERY emitted candidate whether or not it passes the screen
  (a malformed or stale emission still needs the identity its finding carries) — compared by canonical_encode;
  an absent origin or identity, or an origin that contradicts the fields present (an AUTHORITATIVE_RECORD with
  a commitment, a METHOD_DERIVATION without one) is CANDIDATE_MALFORMED at IDENTITY PARSE; step (6) screens aggregation, components and coupling identically for

  stage result for this cell and basis, evidence metadata; NUMERIC FIELDS ABSENT — no truthful bounds exist}; a record without the header is
  malformed (rig156 Q2: two UNKNOWN days were indistinguishable). RAW ESTIMATE CANDIDATES (reducer INPUTS —
  never a day record or an accepted class bound, which stay finite; rig167 G4: no legal output record could
  produce the unbounded support the accumulator types): a DAY or CLASS estimate candidate is BOUNDED_ESTIMATE
  {low, point, high finite} | UNBOUNDED_ESTIMATE {low, point, high: the literal discriminant UNBOUNDED —
  nonnumeric, never ∞ or a sentinel number}, each with the domain's non-numeric fields; a zero proof is its own
  kind (below); an UNBOUNDED_ESTIMATE can never yield a bound — its terminal disposition is UNBOUNDED_OBSERVED
  when every other screen step passes and otherwise the earlier disposition (stale, inapplicable, mismatched,
  malformed). DISCLOSED CONSEQUENCE of the frozen schema: no FROZEN operation class carries an exercise-energy
  ESTIMATE (A4 has readings, food-days, steps, sleep, events, sessions — no energy value) and A4 rejects
  non-finite numbers, so in v1 the only operation-backed evidence in this reducer is the NO_EXERCISE_ATTESTATION
  (a zero proof); every POSITIVE estimate — bounded or unbounded — needs a PRODUCER. THE PRODUCER PRECONDITION,
  stated once and referenced everywhere else, applies to POSITIVE-EEE PATHS ONLY (rig170 T2): when O ≠ ∅, Today
  reaches ONE_BOUND only if a DECLARED POSITIVE-EEE PRODUCER supplies a day estimate covering O; the plan reaches
  CLASS_BOUNDS_OK only if such a producer supplies a class bound for every governed class; ZERO_ATTESTED (O = ∅
  with a qualifying zero proof) needs NO positive producer and displays as it always did; and APPLYING an
  immediate persistent intake proposal needs 3T AND 3P, so it stays blocked until 3P is complete. Two producer kinds are admissible on the same terms: a blueprint-declared
  AUTHORITATIVE SOURCE, and the ruled D14.1 METHOD (its DATED default for Today, its CLASS method's standing
  emission for the plan). D14.1 is therefore mandatory ONLY where no qualifying authoritative source supplies the
  candidates (rig169 S4); neither is ruled yet. Until a producer exists, a logged training day's Today floor
  abstains (MISSING_EEE_ESTIMATE, the one defaultable code), and EVERY class bound — the NO_PLANNED_BOUTS bound
  included — exists only through a producer's STANDING EMISSION (§D14-F P1: at every evaluation the producer
  emits, for every governed class, one derived class-bound CANDIDATE from the plan's own content — per-slot estimates with ranges for a planned class, the plan's
  walking-rule term with its range for a NO_PLANNED_BOUTS class, [0,0,0] when no walking rule is declared —
  screened and reduced like any candidate; this is an EMISSION, not a fill, needs no activation
  precondition, and is emitted ONLY INTO ABSENCE — ABSENCE and the map share ONE predicate: a class HOLDS a
  candidate when ANY candidate — of any producer, supplied by any earlier phase of §D14-F P1, the emitter's own
  record included — passed screen steps (1)–(3) (parseable, current, applicable) AND was not excluded at step
  (7) (REPLACED | WITHDRAWN — a withdrawn or replaced record holds nothing, so the next phase emits; rig172 Y1:
  read on (1)–(3) alone, a withdrawal suppressed the D14.1 fallback and left the class CLASS_BOUND_MISSING —
  worse than never sending the record); into such a class no later phase emits anything, so an emission never stands in a CLASS_BOUND_CONFLICT with a candidate of an EARLIER
  phase, and two emissions of ONE phase cannot conflict because the registry admits at most ONE active
  emitting source registration and ONE active D14.1 version (P0a) — an emission has no operation to correct or
  tombstone, so a conflict it could not leave is prevented, never reported; when RECORDS of SEVERAL sources
  hold a class (phase RECORD), active_producer_map binds the SET of their routes (sorted by canonical_encode of
  the route identity), and for EVERY AUTHORITATIVE_RECORD route — a class held by records from several
  sources included — the derived input-domain-set commitment is the canonical UNION of
  semantic_dependency_domains over every record that route holds, which P0b validates BEFORE P2 evaluates any
  record conflict (rig177 S5: an "EMPTY" commitment let P0b pass a class whose record read an unresolved
  lift:press domain, so the two definitions gave different bases and faces); P2 then reports their
  disagreement as CLASS_BOUND_CONFLICT as before — two records of
  different sources are never equivalent, provenance being part of the validated semantic bound, so a newer
  record of either source replaces only its own candidate and cannot clear the conflict; it stands until one
  of the registrations is deactivated or a record is withdrawn or replaced under the LINEAGE RULE (v1 selects between sources no more
  than between emissions; two record-supplying sources for one scope is a blueprint configuration §E
  discloses); records of ONE source stand together unless one REPLACES another under the LINEAGE RULE below —
  distinct slots coexist and reduce like any candidates); until then 3P returns MISSING_EEE_ESTIMATE {every planned slot} for each planned class and
  CLASS_BOUND_MISSING for each NO_PLANNED_BOUTS class; and because the APPLICATION RULE requires 3T AND 3P,
  D14 v1 BLOCKS THE APPLICATION OF EVERY immediate persistent intake proposal until the producer precondition is
  met on the plan side: Today's rest-day figure DISPLAYS and is bound as the TODAY scope's constraint (a logged training day
  abstains, TODAY then a second listed locus), the plan figure abstains, and the policy check returns BLOCKED_BY
  {locus PLAN_HORIZON, the sorted class results; locus TODAY when Today failed} — a declared producer (in the plan scope selected
  by §D14-F P1's PHASE RULE — records, then every ACTIVE registration declaring SOURCE_EMISSION as one batch
  (at most one, by P0a), then the one active D14.1; in the day scope R1 screens every stored candidate at once
  and a source supplies Today only through RECORD) is therefore a PRECONDITION FOR APPLYING AN IMMEDIATE
  PERSISTENT INTAKE PROPOSAL and for any training-day or plan figure, never for the rest-day figure, said in §E and on the face; nothing here guesses a
  number in the meantime, and UNBOUNDED_OBSERVED is unreachable until a producer can emit an UNBOUNDED_ESTIMATE
  (the union exists so that the reducer is total when one does). REQUIRED FIELDS ARE PER DOMAIN (rig163 Q2): a
  DAY record carries the dated header and NO applicability_basis; a CLASS bound carries class_id and
  applicability_basis and NO dated header. ZERO PROVENANCE is tagged — {kind: ATHLETE_ATTESTATION, the
  NO_EXERCISE_ATTESTATION operation id} | {kind: AUTHORITATIVE_SOURCE, source_id, proof_record_id = the proof's
  SOURCE ITEM IDENTITY — every item a source sends (a record, a proof, a withdrawal, an answer to a re-query)
  carries {source_item_id: the source's OWN immutable item identity, canonical_content_commitment: the policy's
  named digest over canonical_encode of the item's DECLARED content — every field the source authored, the
  lineage fields included — with the transport wrapper, the receipt id and every ingestion stamp (route
  identity, requested_cell_key) EXCLUDED, a fixed point like the derivation commitment}, and INGESTION applies
  ONE REPLAY RULE per source. THE ORDER AT THE DOOR (rig179 T1: a door that tested the CURRENT registration
  before any replay lookup refused a removed source's retry of a submission whose group was durably incomplete,
  so the group could never be recovered and blocked that source for ever; rig180 T1: a door placed after the
  transport lookup but BEFORE the content lookup answered one content identity ORIGINAL / REFUSED / ORIGINAL by
  transport and registry state): the authority first AUTHENTICATES the submission's SOURCE PRINCIPAL against
  that source's registration HISTORY — the SOURCE INGESTION PROTOCOL's historical_principal_verified (a
  removed or revoked registration's principal stays authenticatable — the sequence is never dropped, below —
  and this predicate permits the INTERNAL lookups and recovery, nothing more); then authority-owned GROUP
  RECOVERY (below); then the
  TRANSPORT-EXACT replay lookup; then VERIFICATION of the submitted content, enough to derive its content
  identity; then the VERIFIED CONTENT replay lookup; ONLY WHEN BOTH LOOKUPS MISS does the submission meet the
  CURRENT-REGISTRATION DOOR as NEW content — the door IS the Protocol's new_submission_authorized, evaluated
  over the presented credential and current_submission_route_id[source_id]; a miss it refuses is REFUSED AT THE
  DOOR — not ingested, no receipt, no stub, no identity consumed, no durable state — and may be submitted
  again once the source is registered and the credential current (rig182 T1: an UNVERIFIABLE miss is a miss
  like any other — verification REPORTS, it never commits; a stub exists only when the door said TRUE);
  a submission whose principal authenticates against no registration, present or past, is refused
  the same way. THE OBLIGATION (owner ruling 2026-09-03, the split): the ADMITTED SET — which submissions pass
  the door, what a source is told, and how a response races a registry mutation — is produced by the SOURCE
  INGESTION PROTOCOL, a separate blueprint-side document with its own audit; it defines the three predicates
  of a submission's principal (historical_principal_verified, new_submission_authorized,
  direct_replay_response_authorized), the credential axis (credential_state, current_credential_epoch, signed
  cutoffs), the unauthorized response envelope, the response protocol, the leakage inventory and the
  linearization of response release; EVERY rule in this appendix holds for whatever set the Protocol admits —
  ingestion here is a FUNCTION of the admitted submission set (rig173 A2, A4; rig175 C1–C3), independent of
  who may submit; the Protocol changes no disposition, identity, currentness or reduction rule. What this
  appendix still requires of the door, because the reducer's determinism depends on it: registry mutations
  and ingestion-group commits SERIALIZE per (athlete, source); the door captures the Protocol's EXPECTED
  SOURCE-REGISTRY TUPLE (which names registration_presence, the registration_instance_id of
  current_submission_route_id and declared_phases beside the credential fields, and deliberately excludes
  the active boolean — a pause during an in-flight commit lands the item non-current, never refuses it); the
  atomic ingestion group commits ONLY by compare-and-append while that tuple still matches the registry; a
  mismatch restarts recovery and both lookups under the new state, and a remaining miss re-evaluates the door;
  a refused miss creates no receipt or stored result and adds nothing to sync; the authenticated athlete
  client receives any EXISTING or RECOVERED stored result through sync whatever the source was told;
  every ADMITTED submission has a TRANSPORT
  IDENTITY always and a CONTENT IDENTITY only once its content VERIFIES (rig178 T1: "{source_item_id,
  commitment} when both parse" made a claimed pair an identity before the bytes behind it were checked) — the
  CONTENT IDENTITY is {source_item_id, canonical_content_commitment} of a verified submission; the TRANSPORT
  IDENTITY is CONTENT-BOUND — the digest of the CANONICAL SUBMISSION (the received bytes with the transport's
  DECLARED volatile wrapper fields — attempt ids, send timestamps — excluded, a §E obligation) or, where the transport guarantees an
  idempotency key stable across retries of one submission (a §E obligation), that key BOUND to that same digest
  (a key alone is never an identity: reused over different bytes it would replay a stub for bytes never seen;
  a per-attempt transport id would mint a stub per retry, rig173 B7) — and under those identities ONE INGESTION
  STATE (a content identity and every transport identity of a submission carrying it resolve to the SAME
  state): WAITING (non-terminal, the
  sheet's own disposition lifecycle) resolving into exactly ONE IMMUTABLE TERMINAL disposition — STORED (a
  verified, ingestion-shape-valid item), COLLISION_MEMBER (a verified, ingestion-shape-valid, IDENTITY-CONSUMING
  submission whose source_item_id an earlier such item of the same source already carries with a DIFFERENT
  commitment) or REJECTION STUB; a replay first performs idempotent GROUP RECOVERY (below), then returns the
  ORIGINAL receipt and the identity's current disposition — disclosure to the source being the Protocol's,
  the athlete's client learning it always through sync — and stores nothing new (rig173 A4: dedup
  against valid items alone let every retry of a rejected submission mint a new stub and a new basis; rig172 X2:
  without any rule an identical retry changed membership, representative and basis with no new evidence). THE
  TESTS RUN IN THIS ORDER (rig175 C1: testing identity before content let a garbled retry of a valid record form
  a collision with it and turn the valid record into a hard blocker; rig177 S1: testing identity before SHAPE
  let a verified proof with a malformed domain_binding collide with the valid proof under its id in one order
  and become a harmless stub in the other; rig178 T1: an exact-replay test by CLAIMED {source_item_id,
  commitment} that ran before verification answered a submission carrying a stored item's commitment over
  DIFFERENT bytes as a STORED replay in one order and as a stub in the other): REPLAY IS SPLIT — FIRST a
  TRANSPORT-EXACT replay: the same content-bound transport identity (the canonical submission digest, above)
  as an earlier admitted submission returns
  that submission's receipt and disposition, a stub's included (an exact re-send of a rejected submission
  replays its stub); THEN VERIFICATION of the submitted canonical content, which only REPORTS — VERIFIED
  {content identity} or UNVERIFIABLE {reason} (an item with no id, no commitment, or a commitment that does not
  equal the recomputed digest) — and commits nothing (rig182 T1: a stub minted here bypassed the door): an
  UNVERIFIABLE submission is never a collision member and never a content replay (a claimed commitment is
  never trusted before the bytes behind it verify), its content lookup is NOT_APPLICABLE and it proceeds as a
  MISS to the door, where the Protocol's new_submission_authorized TRUE yields the transport-keyed REJECTION STUB group and
  FALSE yields a REFUSAL with no durable state; THEN the VERIFIED CONTENT replay — the same content identity
  {source_item_id, verified commitment} as an earlier verified submission → that submission's original
  receipt and disposition, whatever it is (STORED, COLLISION_MEMBER or a shape stub alike; nothing stored,
  nothing re-tested, and NO new route stamp — the holder keeps the stamp it arrived under; a source that wants
  equivalent content current under a newly registered route submits it under a NEW source_item_id); THEN —
  only when both lookups missed — the CURRENT-REGISTRATION DOOR (above) for NEW content; THEN
  INGESTION-SHAPE VALIDATION, which decides whether the
  submission is IDENTITY-CONSUMING: only a
  submission that would otherwise be STORED — a well-formed unresolved reference included — participates in
  source_item_id equality or collision lookup at all; a proof without a well-formed domain_binding, an item
  of an unknown kind or with the wrong required fields is a REJECTION STUB that NEVER consumes its claimed
  source_item_id and never becomes a collision member even though its commitment verifies, so a later valid
  item consumes that id in every arrival order; THEN the ROUTE STAMP — every identity-consuming submission,
  whatever its kind and whatever disposition follows, is stamped with the immutable route identity it arrived
  under (below); THEN the IDENTITY / COLLISION test — same source_item_id + a
  different verified, shape-valid, identity-consuming commitment → an IDENTITY COLLISION: the new
  submission is COLLISION_MEMBER, the earlier item KEEPS its disposition (STORED stays terminal), and the two —
  with every later verified, shape-valid, identity-consuming submission under that id — form ONE COLLISION
  COMPONENT keyed by (source_id,
  source_item_id) whose FORMATION is its own receipted ingestion event: from that event every member is HIDDEN
  from reduction (a visibility change, never a disposition change) — no member is a candidate, a zero proof, a
  withdrawal in force or a lineage target — and the scope(s) the members CLAIM (a RECORD its scope target; a
  PROOF the cell its instant set equals or its request names; a WITHDRAWAL or an ANSWER the scope of what it
  targets or answers) carry the HARD, nondefaultable finding SOURCE_ITEM_IDENTITY_COLLISION {source_id,
  source_item_id, the SORTED member commitments, claimed scope target} (tier 6; ONE params record per claimed
  scope; CONTENT, never receipts — rig175 C2: receipts made one member set two bases), bound — iff
  component_scope_current AND NOT retirement_effective, below — beside the screen
  by R1 for the cell and by P1 per claimed class exactly as a stub is, so the same admitted set reduces to the
  same truth whatever the delivery order (rig175 C3: under "the original stands" the two orders gave floors
  2,001 and 1,801; under the component both give the blocker); RETIREMENT IS A DERIVED PREDICATE (rig176 W1;
  rig177 S3): the formation event records the withdrawals targeting x present at formation and a retirement
  event records a withdrawal's arrival, both IMMUTABLE AUDIT EVIDENCE — but whether the finding is suppressed
  is retirement_effective(C), DERIVED at every basis: true iff at least one CURRENT (stamped registration
  active), NON-HIDDEN withdrawal targets C.source_item_id; and whether the finding is BOUND at all is
  component_scope_current(C, scope, basis), DERIVED at every basis too (rig178 T2: a component of a source whose
  registration was removed blocked its cell for ever — its members would have been REGISTRATION_NOT_CURRENT
  if screened, no current withdrawal existed, and an unregistered source cannot send one): true only when at
  least one member claiming that scope carries a registration-current stamp AND a currently applicable scope
  claim (a cell still queried, a class still governed under the current plan basis), evaluated with component
  hiding IGNORED; the hard finding SOURCE_ITEM_IDENTITY_COLLISION is bound for a scope iff
  component_scope_current(C, scope, basis) AND NOT retirement_effective(C); otherwise the component is retained
  in the source card and History DIAGNOSTICS only, never a finding; a registry transition recomputes BOTH
  predicates in both directions — deactivation or removal lifts the blocker, reactivation brings it back — and
  the registry epoch covers either
  transition; EXITS: a WITHDRAWAL targeting the identity x retires any component for x, formed before or after
  it, resolved before or after formation (the id stays consumed, every member stays hidden, the finding leaves
  while the withdrawal is current; the corrected report comes under a NEW id) — the same admitted set {A, B, W}
  reads ABSTAIN in every order; a supersedes_source_item_id that resolves to
  a colliding id is CANDIDATE_MALFORMED; a colliding ANSWER leaves its request UNANSWERED (the request rule
  counts qualifying answers, never members of a component, so an answer under a new id may still qualify);
  MANDATORY COPY (rig178 T4: the id space is shared across RECORD, PROOF, ANSWER and WITHDRAWAL and a component
  may hold more than two commitments, so "two versions … either" was false for a legal component) — face:
  "{source} sent conflicting submissions under one item ID. Earned isn't using any of them until {source}
  corrects it."; Why names the member count, the item kinds, the sorted commitments, the claimed scopes and the
  exit — never content. A corrected resend has a different content and therefore different identities, so it is
  a new submission; the id space is ONE per source across item kinds (a record and a proof under one id
  collide); so (source_id, source_item_id) is total and unique by construction, and ingestion is a FUNCTION of
  the admitted submission set — delivery order and retries change nothing (rig173 A2, A4; rig175 C1–C3). THE
  RECEIPT SEQUENCE, stated once: per ATHLETE and per SOURCE, consecutive integers from 1, issued to EVERY
  INGESTION EVENT — a terminal disposition (STORED, COLLISION_MEMBER, a stub), a waiting-reference resolution,
  a component formation or retirement, a stored request — so contiguity is decidable and §D14-G's frontier is
  ONE integer per (athlete, source); THE ATOMIC INGESTION GROUP (rig176 W2: a COLLISION_MEMBER disposition
  durable without its formation left the STORED item visible and a retry "stored nothing new" — a hidden
  commit; the same gap sat between a stored target and its waiting-reference resolution): the admission of a
  triggering item and EVERY deterministic event it causes — the waiting-reference resolutions whose target it
  is, the component formation or membership update it triggers, the retirement a withdrawal causes — are
  computed as a function of the stored state and committed as ONE atomic authority commit with CONSECUTIVE
  receipts, the sheet's ATOMIC DIRECT-EDIT ADMISSION (A3) applied to ingestion; the (athlete, source) sequence
  length advances only by the whole group, so no triggering disposition and no intermediate length is ever
  visible; GROUP RECOVERY IS AUTHORITY-OWNED and never depends on a source retry (rig179 T1): before ANY later
  action involving that (athlete, source) — a later ingestion, a registry mutation touching the source
  (deactivation, removal, reactivation, any edit), a sync snapshot served to a client, a reduction — the
  authority COMPLETES the pending group FIRST (the derived events are a deterministic function of the durably
  admitted triggering item and the stored state, so completion is always possible and never waits on the
  source) and, until it has, that action is BLOCKED behind recovery — never skipped, never served an
  intermediate length; a transport-exact replay performs the same idempotent recovery first; an incomplete
  group is completed, never reported done; an incomplete group in the authority's durable state blocks
  reduction over that source and every D14 application that reads it (a frontier can never point inside a
  group); whatever the source is told (the Protocol's), recovery still completes internally and the
  authenticated athlete client obtains any existing or recovered stored result through sync (a refused miss
  adds nothing);
  a receipt is
  ARRIVAL / AUDIT METADATA ONLY — never an identity, never a revision order (rig172 X1); a sequence is NEVER
  dropped (v1 has no deletion of any kind: a source whose registration is removed keeps its sequence and its
  frontier for ever); INGESTION stamps EVERY verified, ingestion-shape-valid, IDENTITY-CONSUMING
  source-authored submission — RECORD, PROOF, ANSWER and WITHDRAWAL alike, a submission whose terminal
  disposition becomes COLLISION_MEMBER included — with the IMMUTABLE ROUTE IDENTITY (kind, producer_id,
  registration version, registration_instance_id) of the incarnation it arrived under, assigned BEFORE the identity test (rig179 T2:
  "every stored proof and record" left two ANSWER members of one component with no defined stamp, so one
  implementation read component_scope_current false and another true); component_scope_current, a
  withdrawal's currentness and an answer's registration test read those per-member stamps; an authority-created
  REQUEST is the app's own item and carries no route stamp; a rejection stub carries its source_id and no
  stamp; a WELL-FORMED REFERENCE to an
  item of the same source that is not yet stored — a withdrawal's target, a record's supersedes_source_item_id,
  an answer's request id — is stored WAITING_FOR_SOURCE_ITEM with its item identity and its replay result, the
  referring item itself stored and screened as if unreferenced, DISCLOSED while unresolved on the source card
  with its kind and receipt (design — never silent: a source's retraction that waits for a record the app never
  stored is shown as waiting, rig173 B6), and RESOLVED the moment an item under the target id is stored — the
  resolution is an event of THAT item's atomic ingestion group, and the effect then applies exactly as if the
  target had arrived first; a resolution records that the reference now has a stored target and NEVER narrows
  a withdrawal to that one item (rig176 W1); a reference to an id that is only a stub's CLAIMED id stays
  WAITING (the id is unconsumed; a valid item may still arrive under it); every resolution carries ONE
  RESOLUTION OUTCOME from ONE closed REFERENCE-EFFECT MATRIX by REFERRER KIND (rig177 S2: two prose rules read
  a withdrawal-of-a-withdrawal two ways — 2,001 with the record standing, ABSTAIN with it withdrawn — and a
  supersedes pointer to a proof both "inert, the record stands" and "malformed"): WITHDRAWAL → a RECORD, PROOF
  or ANSWER identity: APPLIES · WITHDRAWAL → a collision component: RETIRES · WITHDRAWAL → a standalone
  WITHDRAWAL or a REQUEST: INERT {TARGET_KIND} · ANSWER → REQUEST: APPLIES · ANSWER → any other stored kind, or
  an impossible request receipt: INERT {TARGET_KIND | NO_SUCH_REQUEST (the decidable test below)} · RECORD
  supersedes pointer → a RECORD of the same source, scope target and lineage slot: resolves and enters the
  LINEAGE test (step (7)) · RECORD supersedes pointer → any other stored kind, scope or slot:
  CANDIDATE_MALFORMED at the screen (an evaluation-time disposition, order-free) — the outcome is recorded by
  the receipted resolution event, disclosed on the source card and in Why, and is NEVER a change of the
  referring item's disposition (dispositions are immutable): the same outcome whether the target was already
  stored at ingestion or arrives later (rig176 X1: a stub in one order and STORED in the other bound two
  ledgers) — so the same immutable item set reduces to ONE truth whatever the delivery order (rig173 A2: W
  before R made W a stub and left R current; R before W withdrew R); and INGESTION
  REJECTS — never stores as a proof, never evaluates — a proof whose domain_binding is absent or not one of the
  two shapes below, storing instead a REJECTION STUB —
  {source_id, keyed_replay_identity_commitment (the per-athlete, per-source, domain-separated keyed
  commitment over the submission's replay identity — its content identity {source_item_id, commitment} when the
  content VERIFIED (a verified but shape-malformed submission), else its transport identity (an unverifiable
  submission never carries a content identity, rig178 T1); never an unkeyed hash, A7), claimed source_item_id and claimed SCOPE TARGET (a cell key
  or a class_id) when parseable, reason; the receipt id in DIAGNOSTICS and Why only}: a stub whose claimed
  scope target is the queried cell, or a governed class of the CURRENT plan basis, enters THAT scope's ledger
  as SOURCE_ITEM_UNREADABLE {source_id, keyed_replay_identity_commitment, claimed_scope_target, reason:
  REJECTED_AT_INGESTION} (○, both domains; in the plan the claimed scope target IS the class_id and is params'
  first canonical key; rig177 S6: keyed by receipt, an unrelated stub arriving first moved this day's ledger
  and basis; the receipt is arrival metadata and is excluded from deduplication, ledger identity and the
  dependency basis) — bound into
  the scope's ledger by the day reducer for the cell (at R1, or with the terminal result when R0 is terminal)
  and by P1 per claimed class, WHATEVER the headline (a stub is not a
  candidate; it is bound beside the screen, never by it) — and Why says the source sent an unreadable report
  (a contradicting duplicate is the COMPONENT above, a blocker, never a stub); a stub whose claimed scope target is absent, unparseable, or names neither
  the queried cell nor a governed class of the current plan basis belongs to no day or class and is a
  feed-level disclosure on the source card (design), so a malformed feed never silently reads as silence), stamped route
  identity (read by the qualification rule below), its completeness guarantee, and domain_binding — ONE DISCRIMINATED value: {mode: OBSERVED,
  instant_set: the proof's ACTUAL canonical instant set (the same finite union of disjoint half-open ranges a
  cell is), rules_version: the zone-rules version it observed under} | {mode: REQUERIED, instant_set: the set
  the source re-derived from its complete raw timestamped data, requested_cell_key: the civil_cell_key of the
  request, whose zone-rules-version component is the version it re-derived under}} — both kinds qualify,
  identically, in this status law and in the reducer; agreeing zero proofs of either kind form ONE class with a
  REPRESENTATIVE by the TOTAL ZERO-PROOF KEY and the sorted membership bound (rig160 Q5; zero proofs carry no
  derivation commitment and stand outside the canonical candidate order, so ONE TOTAL ZERO-PROOF KEY orders
  them: athlete attestations first by (athlete_log_seq, op_id), then authoritative proofs by (source_id,
  source_item_id) — stubs are not zero-proof items and carry their own code, SOURCE_ITEM_UNREADABLE — rig166 Q8; rig171 U1: keyed by source_id alone, a source's older proof and its re-queried
  proof collided while binding different instant sets, leaving representative, membership, ledger identity and
  the basis undefined; the total key is also what ZERO_PROOF_INAPPLICABLE carries — always with a reason from
  ONE closed enum: DOMAIN_MISMATCH (the instant set is another cell's, single-offset and two-component cases
  included) | REQUEST_MISMATCH (a REQUERIED proof for another request) | REGISTRATION_NOT_CURRENT (its stamped
  registration is not active or declares no RECORD) | WITHDRAWN (the source withdrew it — the LINEAGE RULE) —
  and when several tests fail the reason is the FIRST in the qualification rule's fixed order,
  WITHDRAWN → REGISTRATION_NOT_CURRENT → DOMAIN_MISMATCH → REQUEST_MISMATCH — WITHDRAWN first, as in
  STALE_DERIVATION's global order, so the one reason name the two enums share reports alike (rig173 B10) — so the
  ledger record is one value — and what the basis binds, together with the REGISTRY VERDICT it qualified under
  (always — an athlete-attested day too, since a repaired registry can revive a source's positive record as
  ESTIMATE_WITHOUT_OCCURRENCE) and, for an authoritative representative, its stamped route identity and the
  active flag and declared_phases it qualified under; a new proof or stub, any change to that registration's
  active flag or declared_phases, or any change that flips the registry verdict, re-issues); COPY is parameterized by the representative's kind — athlete: "You reported no
  exercise today."; authoritative source: "{source} reported no exercise for {date}." — never "attested" for a
  source the athlete did not supply, and Why names the source, its proof_record_id, its completeness guarantee
  and its domain binding. ZERO_ATTESTED requires O = ∅ and NO
  contradictory live exercise evidence (an ESTIMATE_WITHOUT_OCCURRENCE is such evidence), and a ZERO PROOF'S
  DOMAIN must EQUAL the entire queried cell (rig167 G5: a legacy 24-hour −05:00 attestation would otherwise
  qualify for New York's 25-hour 2026-11-01): the attestation's RECORDED INTERVAL is converted to its canonical instant
  set — A4's event payload carries `interval` as a value whose internal shape the sheet does not pin; this
  appendix READS it (a blueprint reading of an existing payload field, no schema row) as two endpoints, each
  {local date, local time, UTC offset} — the same endpoint triple the sheet uses for session endpoints — and a
  client that records a single-offset interval yields ZERO_PROOF_INAPPLICABLE on every transition day, so the
  blueprint MUST require both endpoints' offsets; the instant set is then compared with the COMPLETE queried cell (every component) for EXACT EQUALITY; nothing
  weaker qualifies (rig169 S2: a pointwise offset check at the attestation's own instant passed a −05:00
  attestation made at 08:00Z on New York's 2026-11-01 — the zone had already changed — although a fixed −05:00
  day, 05:00Z → 05:00Z, is not the 04:00Z → 05:00Z cell; from frozen fields the two attestations are
  indistinguishable, so only the interval decides). A recorded interval whose endpoints carry the zone's own
  offsets on each side of a change (Nov 1 00:00 −04:00 → Nov 2 00:00 −05:00) EQUALS the 25-hour cell and
  qualifies; a single-offset 24-hour interval does not; and NO single span can equal a two-component cell
  (America/Goose_Bay 2010-11-06) — such an attestation is ZERO_PROOF_INAPPLICABLE and the card says the athlete's
  own rest-day tap cannot cover that date (a disclosed limitation, filed to §E — an authoritative source's
  proof, binding its actual instant set, can still equal such a cell); ordinary transition days remain attestable through the
  two-offset reading above; an authoritative source's zero proof QUALIFIES iff, tested in THIS order (the first failure is the
  ledger reason), FIRST it has not been withdrawn by a stored, current WITHDRAWAL of that source (else
  ZERO_PROOF_INAPPLICABLE / WITHDRAWN), then its stamped route identity names an ACTIVE registration whose declared_phases contains RECORD
  (else ZERO_PROOF_INAPPLICABLE / REGISTRATION_NOT_CURRENT — a deactivated source's proofs stop qualifying
  exactly as its records go stale; in the day scope, where P0a does not run, the registry is read as P0a would
  validate it and a registry P0a would reject makes every stamp non-current) and its
  domain_binding.instant_set EQUALS the queried cell as instant sets — whatever the rules versions for an
  OBSERVED binding; a REQUERIED binding must IN ADDITION carry requested_cell_key EQUAL to the queried
  civil_cell_key (it was asked for this cell), else it is a proof about another request and
  ZERO_PROOF_INAPPLICABLE / REQUEST_MISMATCH — the last test (the request is stored BEFORE it is sent, so an
  answer can never precede its request; an answer naming an unknown request id is nonetheless WAITING, never a stub, and
  resolves INERT {NO_SUCH_REQUEST} by ONE decidable test — request ids are receipts of that (athlete, source)
  sequence, consecutive and never reused, so an id at or below the sequence's current length that names no
  stored REQUEST can never become one, while an id above it stays WAITING; the flip is a waiting-reference
  RESOLUTION, a receipted event of the group that carries the length past the id, so §D14-G's frontier covers it) — (rig170 T3: reconstructing the source's date and zone under
  the QUERIED rules version equals the query by construction even when the source observed a 24-hour cell that
  a later rules change made 25 hours) — its binding is OBSERVED (the set it actually observed, with that
  version) or REQUERIED (a NEW immutable proof, under a NEW proof_record_id, that the source emitted by
  re-querying its complete raw timestamped data under the requested version; a REQUERY GUARANTEE declared in
  the blueprint is what lets the blueprint's ingestion side ask for one — NEVER during an evaluation: the
  reducers read only STORED evidence (proofs and rejection stubs) and abstain on what they find (they stay total
  over the current stored evidence set); a request is issued outside evaluation (when, is design's) and STORED
  by ingestion as a REQUEST {source_id, civil_cell_key, receipt id — the request is the app's own item, so
  its receipt is its identity} — the at-most-ONCE-per-(source_id,
  civil_cell_key) bound is a property of that stored record, the key carries the rules version so a rules
  change permits one more, and a request is issued only for a cell for which the source has NO qualifying
  stored proof; the source answers with the request's receipt id and INGESTION, never the source, stamps
  requested_cell_key from the stored request it answers (an answer naming a request id not yet stored is
  WAITING_FOR_SOURCE_ITEM and resolves INERT only by the decidable test above — an id at or below the
  sequence's current length naming no stored request); a request has AT MOST ONE QUALIFYING RESPONSE, chosen
  by CONTENT and never by arrival (rig175 B1: "the first stored answer" picked a different answer in each
  delivery order): answers are stored like any item under the replay rule (a retry returns its original
  receipt; two verified, shape-valid answers under ONE source_item_id are an identity collision — a COMPONENT, and the
  request stays UNANSWERED until an answer under a new id arrives); the SELECTOR POPULATION, stated once: the
  terminal STORED, ingestion-shape-valid ANSWER items naming the request whose resolution outcome is APPLIES,
  that are members of NO identity-collision component and are NOT currently withdrawn — collision
  members never participate (rig176 W3: a selector over all stored answers picked the hidden member ans-01
  and gave the valid new-id answer ans-02 REQUEST_MISMATCH, making the promised recovery unreachable) — the one
  with the LEAST source_item_id (canonical_encode order) is THE answer, whether or not it then passes the domain
  tests; every OTHER MEMBER OF THAT SELECTOR POPULATION is ledgered ZERO_PROOF_INAPPLICABLE {key,
  REQUEST_MISMATCH} and never qualifies (an answer outside the population keeps its own row — WITHDRAWN, the
  component blocker, or the INERT resolution disclosed on the source card; rig179 T3: "every other stored
  answer" gave the withdrawn answer, the hidden member and the INERT answer REQUEST_MISMATCH rows too), and a
  source that wants a later answer to govern WITHDRAWS the earlier one (the next-least eligible
  then becomes THE answer) — so a re-query can add a first qualifying proof or one more
  non-qualifying one and can never loop; the guarantee itself qualifies
  nothing); an old proof
  is never reinterpreted under the queried version alone, and the older proof, keyed apart, is simply a proof
  about another cell; a proof whose domain does not qualify is ZERO_PROOF_INAPPLICABLE {its total key, DOMAIN_MISMATCH} (ledger,
  ○ — it is about another cell, never a headline) and contributes nothing, so O = ∅ without a qualifying proof reads NO_OCCURRENCE_EVIDENCE. THE LINEAGE RULE (rig172 X1: v1.23 let a
  higher ingestion receipt of the same source and scope replace every earlier record after steps (1) and (3)
  alone, so a cardio partial erased a resistance partial, a numerically malformed later row erased a valid 500
  and let the D14.1 fallback 300 pass — 1,801 for 2,001 at lean 50 — and a record under a since-deactivated
  route erased one under an active route): every source RECORD carries lineage_slot (the source's OWN
  immutable semantic slot for the record — its day total, its resistance partial, its class bound — declared
  by the source, compared as an opaque string) and supersedes_source_item_id (absent, or the source_item_id
  of the ONE stored record of the same source it replaces); a record R' REPLACES a record R iff ALL of: same
  source_id; same scope target (the civil_cell_key, or the class_id under the current plan basis); same
  lineage_slot; R'.supersedes_source_item_id = R.source_item_id; R' is stamped under an ACTIVE registration
  declaring RECORD; and R' is FULLY WELL-FORMED — it passed every screen step (1)–(6) with no blocking
  disposition and no ledger exclusion (a terminal-valid candidate, WHOLE or PARTIAL); then R is
  STALE_DERIVATION {REPLACED, by R'} (ledger, excluded); records of DIFFERENT slots coexist and reduce like any
  candidates (disjoint partials; an overlap is PARTIAL_OVERLAP as before); a later submission that fails any
  step, or names no predecessor, replaces NOTHING and stays visible under its own disposition (a malformed
  replacement is CANDIDATE_MALFORMED beside the record it failed to replace — never a silent deletion); a
  supersedes_source_item_id naming an item of the SAME source not yet stored is WAITING_FOR_SOURCE_ITEM (the
  record stands as unpointed until the target arrives, then replaces it if it qualifies — §D14-C's reference
  rule); one that RESOLVES to a stored item that is not a record, or to a record of a different scope target
  or lineage_slot, is CANDIDATE_MALFORMED at IDENTITY PARSE — the submission replaces nothing and blocks in its
  own right (rig172 Y5: otherwise a missed pointer left two same-slot records colliding with no finding saying
  why); two
  records of one slot with no pointer between them both stand and conflict, and the source must say which it
  replaces; ingestion receipt order NEVER means replacement. A WITHDRAWAL is its own typed source item {kind:
  WITHDRAWAL, target: a source_item_id of the source's own}, and its target is an IDENTITY, never one item
  (rig176 W1: a withdrawal that resolved against the one record then stored left the component a later
  same-id item formed unretired — the same set {A, B, W} read ABSTAIN in one order and BLOCKED in another):
  replay-keyed like any item, stored like a record — under a registration declaring RECORD, the active flag
  read at EVALUATION, never at ingestion (rig172 Y3: rejecting it while the registration was inactive burned
  the id for good) — and CURRENT at evaluation iff its stamped registration is ACTIVE; while a current
  withdrawal for x exists NO WITHDRAWABLE RECORD, PROOF OR ANSWER UNDER x IS IN FORCE, present or future — a
  record STALE_DERIVATION {WITHDRAWN, by the withdrawal}, a proof ZERO_PROOF_INAPPLICABLE {key, WITHDRAWN}
  (ledger), an answer never selected — and any collision component for x is retired while the withdrawal is
  current (retirement_effective, above); a standalone WITHDRAWAL is NEVER disabled by another withdrawal and
  can be hidden only as a collision member (the REFERENCE-EFFECT MATRIX, rig177 S2); a
  withdrawal naming an id under which nothing is stored yet is WAITING_FOR_SOURCE_ITEM and takes effect on
  everything later stored under that id, its resolution (an event of the storing item's group) never narrowing
  it; a withdrawal whose target id holds, or comes to hold, a withdrawal as its first item is resolved INERT
  {TARGET_KIND} — a receipted resolution outcome, the same whether the target was already stored or arrives
  later; its disposition stays STORED, dispositions being immutable (a withdrawal is not itself withdrawable: a
  source that withdrew in error sends a new record under a new id); a hidden member of
  a component is never a withdrawal in force; a withdrawal is never a candidate, is never screened and enters
  no phase batch — it is read at formation, at step (7) and by the proof rule's fourth test; Why names a
  withdrawal as the source taking its report back. The attestation is the
  athlete's one tap "No exercise today — including training, cardio/sport and exercise walking"; it is an
  operation of the FROZEN schema's own event class: event {type: NO_EXERCISE_ATTESTATION, interval: the civil day of the
  attested date under the evaluation zone, as two offset-bearing endpoints} — no new class, the sheet stays closed; EVERY live positive exercise fact for that day
  invalidates it regardless of acceptance order and changes the basis; corrections and tombstones follow the
  ordinary event reducer; design draws the tap. Every numeric value is unit-bearing with COMPATIBLE-UNIT
  VALIDATION and full-precision canonical conversion to kcal/day applied BEFORE validation or joint-extrema
  evaluation — to the day record, to every EEE_CLASS_BOUND and to both D14.1 output types alike (an untyped 300
  could be kcal or kJ). joint_uncertainty_basis declares the dependence between EEE and lean mass as CARTESIAN,
  or as a finite union of closed boxes that ALSO carries a LEAN BINDING — {governing representative op_id,
  sorted equivalence membership, semantic source commitment} of the lean source it was justified against
  (identical marginal ranges give different suprema when the inputs are dependent; rig162 Q4: two lean sources
  with identical [50,60] marginals justified different dependence sets and floors of 2,001 and 2,101 — a 2,050
  band passed on the stale set). TWO SCOPES, never conflated: the day record belongs to ONE queried cell and
  feeds TODAY's floor; a versioned EEE_CLASS_BOUND {class_id, low, point, high, unit, source, method, version,
  coverage, completeness_basis, overlap_basis, applicability_basis, joint_uncertainty_basis, candidate_origin,
  and the DERIVED-CANDIDATE FIELDS of §D14-C — for a METHOD_DERIVATION EMISSION the four (derivation_commitment,
  aggregation, derivation_components[], cross_partial_basis when required), for a STORED METHOD_DERIVATION those
  four plus consumed_plan_domains, and all but derivation_commitment and consumed_plan_domains for an
  AUTHORITATIVE_RECORD} with 0
  ≤ low ≤ point ≤ high < ∞ and high > 0 whenever the class
  has planned bouts — an exact-zero bound on a nonempty planned class is CANDIDATE_MALFORMED (rig165 Q8: [0,0,0]
  would otherwise reach ONE_BOUND and ignore the planned exercise); exact zero belongs only to the
  NO_PLANNED_BOUTS variant; the finite POINT is REQUIRED —
  without it the class display point had three candidates, rig157 Q3; the same nearest-feasible selector
  applies to the class set — and the bound belongs to a recurring plan class and feeds ONLY the persistent plan constraint — a
  zero-attested Monday never establishes future Mondays; a NO_PLANNED_BOUTS class still carries an explicit
  declared bound (from the plan and the walking rule, never inferred from silence) and that bound is a WHOLE
  candidate (coverage = O = ∅); a declared producer (the ruled D14.1 CLASS METHOD, or an authoritative source
  that emits class bounds, on the same terms — §D14-C's producer precondition, selected by §D14-F P1's PHASE
  RULE) emits it — and every planned
  class's bound — by STANDING EMISSION at every evaluation (always a METHOD_DERIVATION: the SAME complete typed
  bound, point included, with method provenance and its derivation commitment) or, for a source's
  already-complete immutable bound, as an AUTHORITATIVE_RECORD ({source_id, record identity}, no commitment) that
  arrives in P1's FIRST screen pass rather than by emission (never through the dated-default activation route,
  which fills Today's missing occurrence ids only). The CANONICAL COMPLETE bound of every governed class —
  every field above — is bound into the proposal's dependency basis (any field changing re-issues). The plan
  constraint requires a NONEMPTY, EXHAUSTIVE day-class partition of the governed days under the effective
  exercise plan (P0a; max over an empty set is undefined and is never taken). THE PRODUCER REGISTRY (rig171 U3) is
  the scope-level list P0a validates and PARTITION_OK carries: every blueprint-declared authoritative source
  registration and the ruled D14.1 registration. A REGISTRATION is an INCARNATION with a ROUTE IDENTITY — the
  quadruple (kind: AUTHORITATIVE_SOURCE | D14_1_METHOD, producer_id (the source_id, or the method id),
  registration version (the source's feed / registration version, or the D14.1 method version),
  registration_instance_id — an IMMUTABLE, authority-issued, monotone, NEVER-REUSED number per producer_key =
  {kind, producer_id} (rig183 T1: a bare producer_id let a D14.1 method whose id equalled a source's id remove
  that source's incarnation), issued at every registration of any kind (rig182 T2: a source re-registered at the same feed version could
  either revive its removed route, making its old records current again, or start a fresh one; the version
  alone guarantees no freshness)) — which is the ONLY key anywhere
  (uniqueness, sorting by canonical_encode, candidate identity, active_producer_map), and CONTENT that is part
  of no key: active (a boolean, required on EVERY kind — the one mutable field of the registration's content,
  and flipping it changes no
  identity), declared_phases ⊆ {RECORD, SOURCE_EMISSION, METHOD_EMISSION} (a source supplies records, or
  emits, or both; D14.1 only METHOD_EMISSION), and input_domain_set (the sorted conflict domains the route
  reads — a method's per-lift domains and, when the walking rule is an input, the step-plan domain; a source's
  feed domains) together with, for every registration declaring an EMISSION phase, its DEPENDENCY FUNCTION —
  a declared, deterministic map from a class's canonical complete definition to the sorted set of conflict
  domains the method or feed reads for that class, whose range is ⊆ input_domain_set (registered per method /
  feed version, so it is part of no key and changes only with the version). THE REGISTRY'S AXES ARE
  ORTHOGONAL, each a named field (rig181 T3: "REVOKED" as a registry state with no relation to the active
  boolean read three ways — evidence still governing, REGISTRATION_NOT_CURRENT, or the route gone):
  registration_presence = PRESENT | REMOVED per incarnation (a REMOVED incarnation leaves the P0a list but
  keeps its route identity, its history, its sequence and its frontier, §D14-C) — PRESENT → REMOVED is
  TERMINAL: an incarnation never becomes PRESENT again, re-registration creates a fresh incarnation, and active
  false → true is the ONLY same-incarnation reactivation; the registry keeps AT MOST ONE PRESENT incarnation
  per producer_key = {kind, producer_id} (a registration mutation that adds one removes only the prior
  incarnation of the SAME producer_key in the same atomic mutation; a source and a D14.1 method that happen to
  share an id are two producer_keys and coexist), and current_submission_route_id[source_id] — the unique
  PRESENT {AUTHORITATIVE_SOURCE, source_id} incarnation, or none — is the TOTAL selector the
  Protocol's credential, door predicate and expected registry tuple bind to; a content replay retains its
  historical stamp, and only a NEW source_item_id submitted under the fresh incarnation can become current;
  registration.active = true | false, defined
  only while PRESENT; the CREDENTIAL AXIS — credential_state = CURRENT | REVOKED per source principal and
  current_credential_epoch[source_id] — is DEFINED BY THE PROTOCOL and is a property of a principal's
  credential, never of the route identity; global_registry_epoch is §D14-G's counter, a distinct field NEVER
  compared to any credential epoch. ONE DEFINITION OF REGISTRATION-CURRENT, read by every reader —
  step (2)'s currency test, the zero-proof rule's REGISTRATION_NOT_CURRENT, the LINEAGE RULE's stamped
  registration, a withdrawal's currentness, component_scope_current's member stamps and P1's PHASE RULE —
  "ACTIVE", wherever this appendix tests a stamped registration, means registration_presence = PRESENT AND
  active = true; the credential axis plays NO part in currentness. THE RULED EFFECT on evidence (the effect on
  submissions and disclosure is the Protocol's): reducer currentness requires PRESENT + active = true; a
  credential REVOCATION alone (a
  compromise response, or the old credential of a rotation) never
  silently reinterprets evidence already accepted — when the athlete or design also means to stop USING that
  evidence, the SAME atomic registry mutation sets active = false or removes the registration, which is the
  ordinary registry-epoch, re-issue and REGISTRATION_NOT_CURRENT path; removal means registration_presence =
  REMOVED; a rotation (old credential REVOKED, new CURRENT at the next epoch) changes no evidence (cowork's
  adoption of the clean separation; the owner may rule a different revocation effect — one is now stated);
  an ACTIVE ROUTE — what active_producer_map binds — is a route identity plus the ONE phase of
  §D14-F P1 in which its candidate was supplied, and a stored candidate reaches its registration by the route
  identity ingestion stamped on it (§D14-C), so the match is total; a route's
  INPUT-DOMAIN-SET COMMITMENT is derived by the runtime for an AUTHORITATIVE_RECORD and for the equality test
  on a stored derivation, and DECLARED by the registration for an emission (its dependency function — a §E
  obligation the runtime cannot observe, which is why the registry verdict tests the function's per-class
  output, below): the union of the SEMANTIC DEPENDENCY DOMAINS of
  every candidate the route HOLDS (§D14-C's ONE predicate: passed steps (1)–(3), not excluded at step (7)) in
  every class it is active for — defined per
  candidate by what the candidate DEPENDS ON, never by the phase that delivered it (rig172 X3: a phase-keyed
  rule let a class record whose applicability reads lift:press pass with that domain's maxima unresolved, so a
  lineage winner governed a class bound), and GENERICALLY, never by an enumeration (rig173 A3: the v1.24 list —
  split, resistance lifts, the walking rule — omitted the domain holding a cardio bout's duration and
  intensity, which the sheet says are not partition content, so a class record following a 20-minute lineage
  winner passed P0b while the 60-minute maximum stood unresolved — 1,801 shown for 2,101): the frozen sheet's
  conflict-domain partition assigns EVERY mutable plan field to exactly ONE domain, domain_of(field), and
  semantic_dependency_domains(candidate) = {domain_of(f) | f is a mutable plan field READ by the candidate's
  applicability_basis, completeness_basis, overlap_basis or method semantics} — for an AUTHORITATIVE_RECORD
  class bound that is every field of its canonical class definition (the partition → split; each planned bout's
  slot fields — modality, lift identity, duration, intensity inputs — → whatever domains hold them, the
  resistance bout's lift domain and the cardio or sport bout's slot domain alike; the walking rule → the
  step-plan domain), computed by the runtime from the basis and never declared, so it is never empty (the
  partition field is always read); for a METHOD_DERIVATION EMISSION it is the registered DEPENDENCY FUNCTION
  applied to the class's canonical complete definition; for a METHOD_DERIVATION {STORED} class record it is its
  consumed_plan_domains, a required DERIVED-CANDIDATE FIELD that must EQUAL the registered dependency function
  of its method / feed version applied to the class it names (not merely a subset of the declaration — a
  declared subset hid the cardio domain, rig173 A3), else CANDIDATE_MALFORMED at step (6) — after currency and
  applicability have excluded stale and wrong-class candidates, so a merely stale record leaves quietly as
  STALE_DERIVATION and never takes a hard blocker (rig173 B3) — bound inside
  its derivation commitment so a change re-issues; a DAY record contributes nothing to P0b, a plan stage (its
  own currency and P2's conflict govern it) — computed at P1 from the registry, the candidates and the plan
  content and bound into the basis (a producer reads only its declared domains — an obligation the blueprint places on the
  registration, said in §E, not a runtime observation). The registry is INVALID —
  PRODUCER_REGISTRY_INVALID {defects}, tier 3, terminal for 3P, raised at P0a — iff its DEFECT SET is nonempty:
  ONE closed enum (rig172 X4: an untyped {defects} let two clients encode one invalid registry two ways and
  bind two bases), each defect a typed value with its required params, the set DEDUPLICATED and SORTED by
  canonical_encode and carried as that ONE canonical value everywhere (P0a's blocking params and Today's
  registry_state alike) — VALIDATED IN THREE STAGES (rig182 T3: a retained REMOVED D14.1 incarnation counted
  toward D14_1_ACTIVE_CARDINALITY; rig183 T3: "every test ranges only over PRESENT incarnations" let a
  filter-first reducer drop an entry with no presence before any test saw it and return an EMPTY, defect-free
  registry): STAGE 1, over EVERY raw entry of the current registry, validates the route identity, the
  registration_instance_id (well-formed, never reused within its producer_key, REMOVED history included) and
  registration_presence, emitting the STRUCTURAL defects — ROUTE_IDENTITY_MALFORMED {position} ·
  ROUTE_IDENTITY_COLLISION {identity} · REGISTRATION_PRESENCE_MISSING_OR_INVALID {identity | position} (a
  registration whose registration_presence is absent or not PRESENT | REMOVED — rig182 T3: one reducer
  included it, another excluded it, a third had no code to reject it); STAGE 2 forms the WORKING producer
  registry from the validated entries whose presence is PRESENT; STAGE 3 runs every test below — active, phase,
  dependency and cardinality, P0a membership and "any registration exists" — over that PRESENT set only,
  REMOVED history taking part in no P0a membership and no active cardinality: PRESENT_INCARNATION_CARDINALITY
  {producer_key, count} (> 1 PRESENT incarnation of one producer_key) · ACTIVE_FLAG_MISSING {identity} (on a
  PRESENT incarnation only — active is inapplicable when REMOVED) · PHASES_EMPTY {identity} ·
  PHASES_KIND_INVALID {identity, phase} (a
  D14_1_METHOD registration declaring RECORD or SOURCE_EMISSION; an AUTHORITATIVE_SOURCE declaring
  METHOD_EMISSION) · INPUT_DOMAIN_SET_INVALID {identity} (absent, unsorted, naming no known conflict domain,
  lacking a dependency function on an emitting registration, or with one whose range escapes it or whose output
  for ANY governed class of the current plan basis is empty or omits the split domain — rig173 B4: a function
  returning ∅ for a class emptied the commitment and restored rig170 T1 through the declaration; this ONE
  clause is tested at P0a over the governed classes of the current plan basis, and the DAY-scope verdict, which
  reads no plan basis, omits it (every other defect is plan-independent), so Today never fails on a plan-side
  declaration defect — or
  empty on a registration declaring an emission phase) · D14_1_ACTIVE_CARDINALITY {count} (≠ 1 while any
  D14.1 registration exists) · SOURCE_EMITTER_ACTIVE_CARDINALITY {count} (> 1 — v1 admits one standing source
  emitter; several is a successor decision filed to §E: two emissions of one phase would conflict with no
  operation to correct either, rig171 U3) · DUPLICATE_ACTIVE_PRODUCER_PHASE {producer_key, phase}; the REGISTRY
  VERDICT is VALID | INVALID {that defect set}, as P0a would judge the registry, computed in the day scope
  too, where P0a does not run; DIAGNOSTICS carries it as registry_state and every payload that consumes a
  candidate or proof binds it (stated once here, referenced everywhere else); the P0a failure is a NAMED FACE
  STATE (design draws it; mandatory copy): "Earned can't verify the training-energy source setup right now.
  Your plan floor isn't available, and no intake change will be applied." — and in the day scope the
