  registry_state disclosure reads "Earned can't verify the training-energy source setup right now, so your
  source's reports aren't in use today." when a defect names an AUTHORITATIVE_SOURCE identity and "Earned can't
  verify the training-energy source setup right now, so its own training-energy estimate isn't in use today."
  otherwise (an athlete-attested rest day still displays ZERO_ATTESTED beside either line); Why names the
  affected sources by their display names and never exposes a route identity; an
  EMPTY registry (nothing ruled yet — the disclosed inert state) is NOT a defect: every
  class then holds NONE and fails at P2 as before.
§D14-D · CANDIDATE SCREEN (run ONCE per scope on that scope's candidates — by the day reducer's R1 for Today,
  by stage P1 for the plan; P2 never re-screens; every candidate receives ONE terminal disposition plus zero
  or more orthogonal projections; rig161 Q3, rig162 Q4, rig163 Q2, rig164 Q6, rig167 G3). FIXED ORDER: (1)
  IDENTITY PARSE — only what the exclusion rules need: the domain's required NON-NUMERIC fields (header or
  class_id, applicability_basis, source, unit, coverage, bases, candidate_origin with the fields its origin
  requires and forbids — §D14-C) present and typed, the coverage parseable, and the two lineage lookups of
  §D14-C — consumed_plan_domains PRESENT and typed on a stored class-domain derivation and ABSENT on a D14.1
  dated output (its EQUALITY with the registered dependency function is tested at (6), never here), and a
  supersedes_source_item_id that, when resolved, resolves to a stored record of the same scope target and slot
  (an unresolved one is WAITING, not malformed)
  (else CANDIDATE_MALFORMED, blocking, and the candidate is NOT PARSEABLE: its coverage is unusable and it has
  no descriptor); NO numeric field is inspected here (rig167 G3: typing the numeric shape first let a stale or
  wrong-class candidate acquire a hard blocker before the step that excludes it); (2) DERIVATION / REGISTRATION CURRENCY — a
  METHOD_DERIVATION whose bound raw-evidence basis digest is not the CURRENT basis or whose stamped route identity
  is not the active one (for a D14_1_METHOD route, the ONE active D14.1 registration's), ANY non-operation
  candidate whose stamped route identity names a registration that is not ACTIVE, or — for an
  AUTHORITATIVE_RECORD — whose declared_phases lacks RECORD (one lookup, the stamp against the registry as P0a
  validates it, the same test the zero-proof rule applies — in the day scope, where P0a does not run, a
  registry P0a would reject makes every stamp non-current and the day result's DIAGNOSTICS carry
  registry_state {PRODUCER_REGISTRY_INVALID, defects} — a named DISCLOSURE in the ALWAYS-present diagnostics,
  never a finding: never in blocking or the ledger, never a headline, not a failure of the day result — so no
  candidate of a defective registry is ever current, "the ONE active D14.1 registration" always has a referent
  when read, and the face can say why a source's evidence is not in use) → STALE_DERIVATION {identity,
  reason} — ONE value under the GLOBAL ORDER of §D14-D's closed enum, WITHDRAWN → REPLACED →
  REGISTRATION_NOT_CURRENT → VERSION_NOT_ACTIVE → BASIS_STALE, the first that applies (rig173 A5: without it a
  candidate stale three ways at once bound three different ledgers); a step-(7) exclusion therefore overrides
  any step-(2) reason (ledger; excluded from everything
  after — rig163 Q4a: a stale positive default on an attested rest day must not become
  ESTIMATE_WITHOUT_OCCURRENCE; replacement and withdrawal are NOT decided here — they need the replacement's
  complete screen result and are step (7)); (3) APPLICABILITY (class
  domain) — a WELL-FORMED applicability_basis that is not this class's canonical complete day-class definition →
  CLASS_BOUND_INAPPLICABLE (ledger; excluded from everything after — rig164 Q6: checking lean binding before
  applicability let irrelevant old-class evidence block the current class); (4) SUPPORT TYPING — the unit is
  validated and every declared number is converted to kcal/day at full precision, then the SUPPORT is typed
  from the CONVERTED numbers alone: FINITE {low, point, high} when three finite numbers are declared (ordered or
  not — ordering is checked at (6)), UNBOUNDED {low, point} when low and point are finite and high is the
  literal UNBOUNDED discriminant of the raw-candidate union (§D14-C), MALFORMED otherwise (an unknown unit, a
  number that does not parse, a bound with no point, or a high that is neither finite nor the discriminant) →
  CANDIDATE_MALFORMED here, yet the candidate STAYS PARSEABLE: it continues through (5)–(6), its coverage proof
  can pass into coverage_for_missing, and its descriptor carries its coverage class with support MALFORMED (so
  a whole-shaped one still suppresses defaulting); the support is fixed here and NO later step changes it;
  (5) LEAN BINDING — a present but malformed binding → CANDIDATE_MALFORMED; a well-formed binding that is not
  the governing source's basis → LEAN_BINDING_MISMATCH (blocking, scope-local); (6) REMAINING VALIDATION —
  ordering (for an UNBOUNDED support low ≤ point; an unordered one is CANDIDATE_MALFORMED and never also
  UNBOUNDED_OBSERVED — rig167 G4), the candidate's OWN declared joint basis (reported once, here — never again
  as JOINT_SET_INVALID), a METHOD_DERIVATION's or AUTHORITATIVE_RECORD's declared SHARED-LEAN COUPLING (projection equality against every
  component's declared joint set, §D14-E R4 (ii) — rig166: validated here for EVERY declared coupling,
  exhaustive or not; a derivation with aggregation = SUM and two or more uncertainty-bearing components — by
  their declared sets — without a coupling is CANDIDATE_MALFORMED, as is a METHOD_DERIVATION or AUTHORITATIVE_RECORD candidate
  whose aggregation field is absent
  — rig167 G8), completeness and overlap proofs, and — for a stored class-domain derivation — consumed_plan_domains
  EQUAL to its registration's dependency function applied to the class's canonical complete definition (§D14-C)
  → CANDIDATE_MALFORMED; only when EVERY
  other validation passed does an UNBOUNDED support read UNBOUNDED_OBSERVED (blocking) — one code per failure.
  The screen emits FOUR outputs: BLOCKERS (carried), coverage_for_missing (every candidate's coverage whose
  COVERAGE PROOF passed — the missing-set input), STRUCTURAL SUPPORT DESCRIPTORS (one per parseable candidate
  whose coverage class is WHOLE or PARTIAL — a STALE_COVERAGE or empty-coverage candidate carries its blocker
  and no descriptor — sorted: identity, coverage class WHOLE | PARTIAL, the unit-normalized support FINITE {low,
  point, high} | UNBOUNDED {low, point} | MALFORMED as typed at (4), and the candidate's OTHERWISE-VALIDATED
  SEMANTIC KEY — method, version, provenance, every basis with its lean binding, coverage — what R4's
  whole-conflict accumulator reads; a terminal-valid whole's complete validated semantic bound is read from the
  arithmetic projection through the candidate identity), and TERMINAL-VALID ARITHMETIC CANDIDATES
  (bounded, fully validated — the ONLY candidates that enter section mathematics and the only ones whose
  validated bound refines an accumulator class key) — these are ORTHOGONAL PROJECTIONS of the candidate set, not
  disjoint boxes (a valid partial appears in the coverage, descriptor and arithmetic projections; rig165 Q6);
  coverage enters coverage_for_missing iff the candidate's COVERAGE PROOF passed — a later numeric blocker (an
  unbounded high, an ordering fault) does not erase coverage whose proof passed. (7) LINEAGE — a projection
  over the FULLY SCREENED set, after (6) for every candidate, order-independent because it reads only final
  (1)–(6) dispositions: a stored source record REPLACED by a fully well-formed replacement, or WITHDRAWN, under
  the LINEAGE RULE of §D14-C → STALE_DERIVATION {identity, reason: REPLACED {by} | WITHDRAWN {by}} (ledger,
  excluded from reduction and from every projection above — the ONE step that revises an earlier disposition,
  and only to a ledger exclusion; ONE value under the GLOBAL ORDER above (WITHDRAWN → REPLACED → the step-(2)
  reasons), and when
  several fully well-formed replacements name one predecessor `by` is the least of them by canonical_encode of
  the replacement's identity while the replacements themselves stand and conflict as unpointed same-slot
  records); a chain is evaluated pointer by pointer (R'' replacing R' leaves R replaced
  by R' as long as R' was fully well-formed; a malformed R'' leaves R' current); an emission or a D14.1 output
  is never a lineage target. completeness_basis is REQUIRED and names, with its method, every modality the
  candidate covers — for a WHOLE candidate that is every modality present in O; for a PARTIAL candidate it is
  completeness over its OWN declared subset (rig163 Q2); lacking it, or covering fewer than its declared
  modalities, is CANDIDATE_MALFORMED (rig156 Q2: a resistance-only bound otherwise passed as WHOLE-day class
  evidence). COVERAGE PROOFS ARE PER DOMAIN (rig164 Q2): a DAY candidate's overlap_basis lists, PER OCCURRENCE,
  {occurrence id, modality, its canonical temporal FOOTPRINT (the finite union of disjoint half-open instant
  spans of §D14-B — one span for a simple occurrence; rig167 G6), estimate identity, provenance} and is VALID
  only when every id in the candidate's DECLARED coverage appears exactly once, no listed id lies outside that
  declared coverage, every footprint EQUALS the source-derived footprint of that occurrence and the footprints
  are pairwise disjoint — all of O is required ONLY for a WHOLE candidate (rig165 Q4: "every occurrence" made a
  PARTIAL impossible; rig159 Q3: an overlap-only check let two resistance bouts pass with one estimated — 1,801
  instead of 2,101; rig158 Q1d: the string "ok" is not a proof); a CLASS candidate's overlap_basis lists, PER
  PLANNED SLOT, {stable slot id, modality, estimate identity, provenance, method semantics} and is VALID only
  when every slot in its DECLARED coverage appears exactly once and none outside (all planned slots only for a
  WHOLE) — frozen plan fields only, no clock interval exists to reproduce; what this proves is slot COVERAGE and
  per-slot estimation; temporal NON-overlap of planned slots is a DISCLOSED POLICY ASSUMPTION of the plan's slot
  structure (each stable slot is one distinct session), said in Why, and a class whose method semantics cannot
  keep it (two slots the method treats as one bout) abstains as CANDIDATE_MALFORMED; before EQUIVALENCE is judged, every validated
  self-reference inside overlap_basis is normalized to the literal SELF (rig162 Q4). Coverage is then classified
  EXHAUSTIVELY against O: WHOLE (coverage = O, including O = ∅) | PARTIAL (∅ ≠ coverage ⊊ O) | STALE_COVERAGE
  (coverage ⊄ O — it names an occurrence no longer in O; blocking until corrected) | CANDIDATE_MALFORMED
  (coverage = ∅ while O ≠ ∅). applicability_basis (class domain) is compared by EXACT EQUALITY with the
  CANONICAL COMPLETE DAY-CLASS DEFINITION of the governed class under the CURRENT plan context: {plan identity,
  plan version, generation, unique immutable class_id, the SORTED full set of every planned bout with every
  method-consumed field (modality, stable slot id, the exercise / lift identity of every RESISTANCE bout —
  frozen plan content, no schema row — duration, the relevant intensity inputs — no clock interval exists in
  the frozen plan), the walking / step-baseline rule, and the explicit NO_PLANNED_BOUTS variant for a
  class with no planned bout} — a whole local day may hold several bouts or none, so no singular session
  signature exists as a key or a fallback (rig158 Q2: cardio 20 → 40 min with the resistance signature unchanged
  passed a stale bound, 2,001 against a needed 2,201; rig157 Q1: a complete 60-minute bound understated the
  plan floor 2,401 → 2,201). EQUIVALENCE of terminal-valid candidates compares the COMPLETE VALIDATED SEMANTIC
  BOUND — coverage, values, unit, method, version, provenance and EVERY basis (completeness, overlap with
  SELF-normalization, applicability, joint uncertainty with its lean binding, cross-partial basis) — excluding
  only candidate identity, wrappers and authority metadata (rig159 Q1: two devices carrying the same
  producer record → ONE class; rig160 Q2: identical values under a Cartesian and a dependent joint basis give floors 2,101 and
  2,051 and are NOT equivalent); each equivalence class has a REPRESENTATIVE by the CANONICAL CANDIDATE ORDER
  and its sorted membership bound into the basis. CANONICAL CANDIDATE ORDER, by candidate_origin then within it: OPERATION_BACKED
  estimate candidates (NONE in v1 — zero attestations are zero proofs and order by the zero-proof key; any frozen
  estimate class a successor schema adds would come here) first by (athlete_log_seq, op_id); AUTHORITATIVE_RECORD
  candidates by their immutable source_id then the source's own record identity; METHOD_DERIVATION candidates
  (class bounds, D14.1 defaults, source records computed from plan inputs)
  follow, ordered by (DERIVATION COMMITMENT, CANDIDATE RECORD IDENTITY) — the commitment first: the keyed
  commitment over the schema {policy_id,
  policy_version, domain DAY | CLASS, scope target (the civil_cell_key, or the class_id with its plan
  basis), sorted source operation ids, consumed partial identities (sorted), filled occurrence ids (sorted), the
  digest of the COMPLETE current raw-evidence inputs and bases, method id, ACTIVE method version (as step (2)
  defines it per route kind), the cross-partial basis the method assumed, and the CANONICALIZED OUTPUT — the output with the commitment field
  itself, candidate identity, wrappers and authority metadata EXCLUDED, so the commitment is a fixed point
  (rig162 Q6)}; then the candidate record identity of §D14-C, because identity is EXCLUDED from the commitment
  by design and two immutable records with equal commitments would otherwise tie with no unique representative
  (rig171 U4); EQUAL COMMITMENTS ARE ALIASES of one derivation — the commitment contains the canonicalized
  output and every basis, so equal-commitment candidates always fall in ONE equivalence class, whose
  representative is the least by (commitment, identity) and whose sorted membership lists every identity; equal
  inputs in any source order commit identically; a new method version, another domain or
  another scope target commits differently (rig159 Q4, rig160 Q5); zero proofs of either kind carry no
  commitment and are ordered by the TOTAL ZERO-PROOF KEY of §D14-C, never by this order.
§D14-E · THE REDUCERS — two functions, one per domain, one result contract, never mixed (rig159 Q4, rig160 Q2):
  the DAY REDUCER (3T) and the CLASS REDUCER (3P, stage P2). Both are TOTAL over the complete current evidence
  set, retain EVERY blocking finding they meet, and run in this fixed order — R0 the occurrence stage (§D14-B;
  any R0 failure is terminal for the day); R1 the SCREEN (§D14-D — run here for Today; for a class P1 already
  ran it and R1 simply CONSUMES P1's four projections), keeping the COMPETING SET (terminal-valid arithmetic
  candidates), the BLOCKERS, coverage_for_missing and the STRUCTURAL SUPPORT DESCRIPTORS — a consumed partial
  never leaves the WITNESS SET (below) — and binding BESIDE the screen every COLLISION COMPONENT whose finding
  for the cell is BOUND (component_scope_current(C, cell, basis) ∧ ¬retirement_effective(C), §D14-C) as
  SOURCE_ITEM_IDENTITY_COLLISION and every rejection stub claiming it as the ledger record
  SOURCE_ITEM_UNREADABLE (§D14-C); R2 the EMPTY-O BRANCH (day domain, precedence over everything after it, with ONE
  CARVE-OUT: a component whose finding for the cell is bound keeps it, so ZERO_ATTESTED and
  NO_OCCURRENCE_EVIDENCE are reachable only while no such component exists — with one, the blocking set is
  nonempty (its highest-ranked code is the headline) and the zero proof's would-be result rides in DIAGNOSTICS,
  a source that contradicts itself about this day being contradictory evidence like an
  ESTIMATE_WITHOUT_OCCURRENCE): when O = ∅ a positive
  parseable candidate is ESTIMATE_WITHOUT_OCCURRENCE (blocking, contradictory evidence against any zero proof),
  otherwise the day is ZERO_ATTESTED (payload selected_eee 0, zero-proof representative and membership by the
  total key, and the representative's complete provenance — §D14-C) or
  NO_OCCURRENCE_EVIDENCE (none — never silently zero, never defaultable; every non-qualifying proof for the cell is
  in the ledger as ZERO_PROOF_INAPPLICABLE {key, reason} and every rejection stub claiming the cell
  as SOURCE_ITEM_UNREADABLE) (rig163 Q4a: with the stale default
  already in the ledger, the attested rest day is ZERO_ATTESTED); for a class with O = ∅ (NO_PLANNED_BOUTS) its
  declared bounds are WHOLE candidates and go to R4 — none at all is CLASS_BOUND_MISSING (nondefaultable: there
  are no occurrence ids for a dated default to fill; a declared producer's standing emission (§D14-C) makes it
  unreachable, rig162 Q6), and two non-equivalent declared bounds are
  CLASS_BOUND_CONFLICT (rig163 Q4b: [0,0,0] and [100,100,100] would otherwise compete silently for floors 1,501
  and 1,601); R3 COVERAGE — every parseable applicable candidate is classified WHOLE | PARTIAL | STALE_COVERAGE
  (§D14-D), every terminal-valid partial is reduced (identical partials canonicalize; non-equivalent partials
  with IDENTICAL coverage → PARTIAL_CONTRADICTION; partially intersecting coverage → PARTIAL_OVERLAP), and the
  UNCOVERED IDS are computed INDEPENDENTLY of any partial's validity — the ONLY definition: M_raw = O minus
  union(coverage_for_missing), where coverage_for_missing is every candidate's coverage whose COVERAGE PROOF
  passed, whatever later numeric blocker it carries (rig166: a candidate covering r whose ordering fails still
  leaves M = {c}, not {r, c}; rig163 Q4d: contradictory resistance partials beside an uncovered cardio bout
  report BOTH PARTIAL_CONTRADICTION and MISSING_EEE_ESTIMATE {cardio}; rig164 Q6); R4 the WHOLE BRANCH — taken whenever at least one WHOLE-SHAPED parseable applicable candidate
  exists (rig162 Q1: consulting U/M beside a valid whole produced MISSING for the whole day): a whole SHAPE
  suppresses defaulting; every whole with a canonicalizable support enters the accumulator below, but ONLY
  terminal-valid bounded wholes enter section mathematics — when no such whole exists the result is the
  carried blockers (rig163 Q4c: one unbounded whole-shaped estimate reads UNBOUNDED_OBSERVED, never a
  defaultable MISSING; a whole with a MALFORMED support reads CANDIDATE_MALFORMED and a whole that failed
  later validation reads its own blocker): (i) ONE
  WHOLE-CONFLICT ACCUMULATOR (rig165 Q8, rig166 Q5): a candidate's SUPPORT is a typed value — FINITE {low, point,
  high} | UNBOUNDED {low, point, no upper bound — the raw-candidate union's typed discriminant, never a
  non-finite number, since the day record forbids those} | MALFORMED (exactly as §D14-D step (4) types it) —
  fixed at the screen's SUPPORT TYPING, unit-normalized, and never changed by a later validation failure; every
  whole-shaped candidate with a
  CANONICALIZABLE support (FINITE or UNBOUNDED) is placed in ONE structural class by its CLASS KEY — the typed
  unit-normalized support, refined for a TERMINAL-VALID whole by its complete validated semantic bound (§D14-D
  equivalence; rig160 Q2: identical values under a Cartesian and a dependent basis are two classes), for an
  UNBOUNDED whole by its OTHERWISE-VALIDATED SEMANTIC KEY from the descriptor (rig167 G4: {100, 150} kcal and the
  same raw numbers in kJ — 23.90, 35.85 kcal — are two classes, and so are unbounded twins under different
  methods or joint bases; two UNBOUNDED wholes are one class only when normalized support AND key agree), and
  for a whole — FINITE or UNBOUNDED — that failed a later validation by the literal marker INVALID, which
  overrides either refinement (it carries its own blocker; the conflict beside it is reported, never hidden); this ONE pass IS the whole-equivalence step; more than one
  class → ONE
  finding, EVIDENCE_CONTRADICTION (day) | CLASS_BOUND_CONFLICT (class), carrying the complete sorted class set,
  emitted exactly once (no second report from a later equivalence step) alongside the candidates' own blockers
  (bounded A + unbounded U → CLASS_BOUND_CONFLICT + UNBOUNDED_OBSERVED); a MALFORMED support suppresses
  defaulting but never enters the accumulator — so the complete blocking set is a function of the typed
  supports and validation outcomes alone (rig158 Q1a: [200,250,300] and [350,400,500] gave order-dependent floors 1,801 /
  2,001; both are named, sorted, until a correction or tombstone leaves one — no new selection operation, the
  sheet stays closed); (ii) the PARTIAL / WHOLE CONSISTENCY GATE, keyed EXPLICITLY to EVERY terminal-valid whole
  class (rig163 Q4e), applied to EVERY terminal-valid partial in the WITNESS SET — every competing partial AND
  every partial consumed by a derivation (consumed partials leave the competing set as candidates but remain
  immutable consistency witnesses; rig163 Q6a: removing the consumed partial permitted a false 1,801 where its
  proven boundary is ≥ 1,901) — consumption through a registered, validated derivation method never waives it
  (rig162 Q2a) — and evaluated on EXACT SAME-LEAN SECTIONS, never on global marginals (rig162 Q2c: a whole
  reaching EEE 500 at lean 50 but ≤ 100 at lean 60 passed a global test against a partial ≥ 400; the same-lean
  case requires 2,201). The gate has TWO tests and ONE outcome (rig164 Q4 — the Minkowski intersection had silently adopted
  Cartesian semantics across partials: two exhaustive partials each [0,100] beside a whole [0,50] passed, floor
  1,701, while the admissible dependence (t, 100 − t) makes the total exactly 100, requiring 1,751 — a 1,725
  band would have run at EA 29.545): (a) the DEPENDENCE-FREE test, always applied — for every lean value a and
  every set S of pairwise-disjoint-coverage witnesses, Σ_{P∈S} inf S_P(a) ≤ sup S_W(a) (the total is ≥ Σ inf
  under ANY dependence); (b) EXHAUSTIVE witnesses (a disjoint family whose coverage union = O, and only a
  NONEMPTY family — the empty family is vacuously exhaustive and constrains nothing, rig164 Q7): if every
  witness is POINT-DEGENERATE (low = point = high) the family's sum is a single point and the whole's section
  must CONTAIN it at every a (rig162 Q2b: 10 + 10 beside a whole of exactly 100 fails; 20 is the only possible
  total), and if at most ONE witness is not point-degenerate the family's sum is that witness's section
  translated by the point sum — exact at every a without any coupling; else if the whole is of EITHER
  non-operation origin — a METHOD_DERIVATION, whose derivation commitment binds its inputs, or an
  AUTHORITATIVE_RECORD, whose immutable {source_id, record identity} and typed derivation_components[] bind them
  (rig171 U2: a wearable's day total summing two uncertain exhaustive partials under a validated coupling was
  PARTIAL_WHOLE_UNVERIFIABLE while the identical method-derived whole passed, although the screen validates the
  same fields for both) — that CONSUMED exactly this family and declares its
  cross_partial_basis — the SHARED-LEAN COUPLING, the ONE schema for this field everywhere in this appendix
  (§D14-C): a finite union of closed boxes over the axes (lean, EEE₁ … EEEₙ) with the sorted component
  identities bound to the axes, each axis's unit, and the lean binding; VALIDATED at the SCREEN for EVERY
  declared coupling (exhaustive or not — a malformed coupling never leaves a derivation "terminal-valid"); its
  projection onto each (lean, EEEᵢ) must EQUAL that component's declared lean–EEE joint set — a consumed
  partial's record set, a FILLED or OTHER component's set declared in derivation_components (a coupling that does not
  reproduce its components is CANDIDATE_MALFORMED for the combining whole — rig165 Q5: a declared coupling {(0,0)}
  beside partials [0,100] and [0,100] would otherwise have passed at 1,701); semantically equivalent box unions
  canonicalize (normalized disjoint boxes, sorted); the whole's COMPLETE COMPONENT SET is the record's
  typed derivation_components[] (roles CONSUMED_PARTIAL | FILLED | OTHER), so "derived solely from the family"
  is decidable — every component is CONSUMED_PARTIAL and the set equals the family — the test runs
  under that coupling with the EXACT summed union preserved: the exact summed union governs CONSISTENCY and
  SELECTION, while the combining whole is a CONSERVATIVE ENCLOSURE that governs range and floor and must CONTAIN
  the summed support at every a — intersection alone is insufficient (rig165 Q5: a valid Cartesian coupling
  sums to [0,200]; a combining whole [0,50] intersects it yet discards the possibilities that require 1,851; such
  a whole is PARTIAL_WHOLE_INCONSISTENT); else the consistency CANNOT be established and the whole is
  PARTIAL_WHOLE_UNVERIFIABLE (blocking, nondefaultable — v1 declares no cross-partial dependence and never
  assumes Cartesian); (c) any failed test → PARTIAL_WHOLE_INCONSISTENT {whole class, witness ids} (hard,
  nondefaultable; rig161 Q1: a whole [200,300] beside a resistance partial [400,500] read 1,801 where the partial
  alone requires ≥ 1,901); (iii) the SELECTED EEE VALUE — when exactly one terminal-valid whole class remains,
  R4 RETURNS an explicit post-partial value EEE_sel* for the lean anchor, with an ADJUSTMENT {direction: NONE |
  RAISED | LOWERED, reason: NONE (iff the direction is NONE) | JOINT_SECTION (the stored point was infeasible
  in the whole's own section at the anchor — no witness involved; rig166 Q8: a dependent whole can move 100 →
  300 with no partials) | PARTIAL_MINIMUM (moved by the witnesses' Σ inf restriction) | COUPLED_SUM (selected
  inside the family's exact summed section — point-degenerate, translated or coupled)}: the FEASIBLE SET at
  the anchor is S_W(anchor) restricted to ≥ the largest Σ inf over every disjoint witness set, and — only under
  test (b), in each of its three exact cases: point-degenerate witnesses, at most one non-degenerate witness
  (the translated section) or a validated SHARED-LEAN COUPLING — intersected with the family's exact summed
  section at the anchor; EEE_sel* = the whole's stored point when it lies in that set, else the nearest point of
  that set (ties → lower); the REASON is then decided by ONE fixed comparison, in this order — NONE when
  EEE_sel* equals the stored point; else JOINT_SECTION when EEE_sel* equals the point of S_W(anchor) nearest the
  stored point (ties → lower); else PARTIAL_MINIMUM when it equals the point of S_W(anchor) restricted by Σ inf
  nearest the stored point (ties → lower); else COUPLED_SUM; an empty feasible set is
  PARTIAL_WHOLE_INCONSISTENT (rig163 Q5: whole [200,500] with stored point 250 beside a proven 400 → EEE_sel* 400,
  RAISED / PARTIAL_MINIMUM, display 2,050; exhaustive point witnesses {10}+{10} beside a whole [20,100] with stored point 100 → the
  feasible set is {20}, EEE_sel* 20, LOWERED / COUPLED_SUM, display 1,670, where a lower-only remap would have shown 1,750);
  §D14-F, 4T / 4P and the display rule CONSUME EEE_sel* and the adjustment — never the stored point, which would
  have shown 1,900 again — and the copy is keyed by the REASON alone and is domain-neutral (rig164
  Q7, rig165 Q9, rig166 Q8: component copy only when a component actually constrained the value):
  JOINT_SECTION → "Adjusted to the nearest estimate feasible at your lean-mass anchor."; PARTIAL_MINIMUM →
  "Adjusted up to the minimum the component evidence already proves."; COUPLED_SUM → "Adjusted to the nearest
  estimate consistent with the component evidence."; NONE → no label;
  consistent witnesses otherwise NEVER tighten the whole's evidence range in v1 (tightening is composition,
  §E); R5 the PARTIAL / MISSING BRANCH — taken only when NO whole-shaped parseable applicable candidate exists:
  any partial failure is blocking and retained; M = M_raw; M ≠ ∅ → MISSING_EEE_ESTIMATE {ids = M} (with one
  resistance partial on a day with exercise walking, M = {walk}, not {resistance, walk}; rig161 Q2) — the ONLY
  defaultable code, and only when it is the sole blocking code (rig160 Q1: an applicable unbounded estimate
  alone → UNBOUNDED_OBSERVED, not defaultable; bounded A + unbounded U → CLASS_BOUND_CONFLICT +
  UNBOUNDED_OBSERVED, never ONE_BOUND(A)); M = ∅ with partials present → WHOLE_ESTIMATE_REQUIRED
  (nondefaultable: nothing is missing and composing the pieces is not a v1 operation). The reducer never sums,
  merges or averages estimates — one accepted whole estimate is the day's (class's) EEE set, and only that set
  (with EEE_sel* as its point) enters the lean–EEE joint set; any method that combines components (a D14.1
  default adding a per-session cost and a walking cost; a wearable's day total) does so INSIDE its own
  versioned method, DECLARES the cross-partial basis it assumed, and emits ONE whole estimate whose inputs are
  bound — by its derivation commitment (METHOD_DERIVATION) or by its immutable {source_id, record identity}
  with its typed derivation_components[] (AUTHORITATIVE_RECORD, which never carries a commitment). Component composition by the reducer is a SUCCESSOR decision filed to §E with the
  requirements already established (rig160 Q3): the component joint set must be validated before summation —
  nonempty, finite, well-formed, unit-normalized, every coordinate projection EQUAL to its declared component
  marginal (a declared box {200}×{100} under marginals [200,300] × [100,200] summed to [300,300] and a floor of
  1,801 instead of 2,001) — the EXACT summed union must be preserved for point selection (a hull over [0,70] ∪
  [100,200] manufactured a feasible 80 → 1,730 where the nearest feasible point gives 1,720), and the aggregate
  point for dependent sets must be defined. A planned rest day with no logged session is NO_OCCURRENCE_EVIDENCE,
  not zero — an unlogged ad-hoc workout is the counterexample; absence of a logged session is never converted
  into zero at any hour.
§D14-F · THE PLAN STAGES, in this ORDER: P0a → P1 → P0b → P2. P0a PLAN / PARTITION STATE (and the PRODUCER
  REGISTRY of §D14-C) — TERMINAL for 3P on failure; a SCOPE-LEVEL typed result, never class-keyed (no plan, an empty partition and a coverage hole have
  no class to key on; duplicate ids cannot key a unique result); its blocking set is COMPLETE, never one code for
  several defects (rig160 Q4: Monday twice + Tuesday uncovered + an empty block + an outside member + a duplicate
  id → five codes at once): an accepted plan exists (else NO_ACCEPTED_EXERCISE_PLAN — reserved for ACTUAL
  absence), is resolved and unsuspended IN the split / partition domain (the guard's content, §D14-B) (else
  EXERCISE_PLAN_UNRESOLVED_OR_SUSPENDED {domains: [split]} — unless the runtime's governing FALLBACK PROJECTION
  (the sheet's pre-effect complete projection) exists and qualifies for the suspended domain, in which case it is
  the plan context and the basis names it), and its governed day-class partition is well-formed: DAY_CLASS_PARTITION_EMPTY |
  DAY_CLASS_PARTITION_INVALID {duplicate class ids} | DAY_CLASS_BLOCK_EMPTY {class ids with no member day} |
  DAY_CLASS_MEMBER_OUTSIDE_DOMAIN {class_id, day} | DAY_CLASS_PARTITION_MULTIPLY_COVERED {days} |
  DAY_CLASS_PARTITION_INCOMPLETE {uncovered days} — and its producer registry is well-formed (else
  PRODUCER_REGISTRY_INVALID {defects}) — PARTITION_OK only when the set is empty (unique immutable
  ids, every block nonempty, every member inside the governed domain, every governed day covered exactly once,
  a well-formed registry), with payload {governed day-class set, plan_basis, generation, producer_registry}. P1 SCREEN — a CARRIER stage, PREPROCESSING ONLY, never
  terminal: it ALWAYS returns headline SCREENED with blocking [] and carries, per class, in its PAYLOAD
  {carried blockers, ledger, the sorted structural support descriptors of §D14-D, coverage_for_missing,
  terminal-valid arithmetic candidates} plus the stage-level active_producer_map (rig165 Q6: valid A + unbounded U
  is carried, not a P1 failure): the candidate screen (§D14-D) over every candidate class bound of
  every class, individually and globally, once — plus the HORIZON GUARD (§D14-B: CLASS_CROSS_DAY_BOUT_OBSERVED,
  class-keyed, carried as a blocker) and every COLLISION COMPONENT whose finding for a governed class is BOUND
  (component_scope_current(C, class, basis) ∧ ¬retirement_effective(C), §D14-C), placed in that class's
  carried_blockers as SOURCE_ITEM_IDENTITY_COLLISION (§D14-C; P1 stays blocking [] — it is
  CARRIED; P2's union carries it, so CLASS_BOUNDS_OK is unreachable while the component stands; the phase rule
  still runs below — a hidden member is held by no class, so the emission is screened, reduced and shown beside
  the blocker) — in this ORDER — the PHASE RULE (rig171 U3: "the declared
  producer … unless a source qualifies first" named no selection, so enumeration order chose between a
  qualifying source emitter and D14.1 and with it the candidate, the map, P0b's domains and the floor): each
  phase's candidates are screened as ONE BATCH before the next phase is consulted, and — step (7) LINEAGE running at the CLOSE of each phase's batch screen, over that batch together with
  every earlier batch, BEFORE the hold predicate is tested and before the map is bound — a class that HOLDS a
  candidate (the ONE predicate of §D14-C: one passed steps (1)–(3) and was not excluded at (7)) after a phase
  receives nothing from any later phase — phase RECORD: EVERY STORED candidate of every class, whatever its origin (AUTHORITATIVE_RECORD;
  METHOD_DERIVATION {STORED} — a source's record computed from plan inputs; OPERATION_BACKED, none in v1 —
  rig172 X3: enumerating two origins left the legal stored-method candidate unrouted); phase SOURCE_EMISSION: for each class holding none, EVERY ACTIVE registration
  whose declared_phases contains SOURCE_EMISSION emits its candidate and all are screened together, so no
  enumeration order exists to matter (by P0a there is at most one); phase METHOD_EMISSION: for each class still holding none, the ONE active
  D14.1 registration's CLASS method (exactly one whenever D14.1 is registered, by P0a; when it is not
  registered this phase emits nothing) emits; a class holding none after all three
  phases holds NONE — and P1 BINDS active_producer_map[class_id] = the sorted set of ACTIVE ROUTES (§D14-C:
  route identity + the one phase in which the candidate was supplied) whose candidates the class HOLDS (§D14-C's predicate:
  passed (1)–(3), not excluded at (7)), with each route's DERIVED INPUT-DOMAIN-SET COMMITMENT (§D14-C), into
  its payload and the basis (any change of route, registration or commitment re-issues); ledger records are bound and excluded, EVERY blocking finding is CARRIED
  into P2, never dropped (rig160 Q1: rejecting an applicable unbounded estimate as "nonblocking" left an empty valid set that
  read as defaultable MISSING; with bounded A it read ONE_BOUND(A); rig159 Q2: reducing "complete, applicable"
  candidates before those checks made valid A + stale B either ONE_BOUND(A) or a conflict depending on
  prefiltering — validation precedes reduction). P0b PRODUCER-DOMAIN STATE — tier 3, TERMINAL for 3P on failure,
  scope-level, success headline PRODUCER_DOMAINS_OK: the UNION of the input conflict domains of every
  active route in active_producer_map — exactly each route's DERIVED input-domain-set commitment of §D14-C,
  the union of the SEMANTIC DEPENDENCY DOMAINS of the candidates it holds, bound here with its states —
  (semantic_dependency_domains of §D14-C: the dependency function's output for an emission, a stored
  derivation's consumed_plan_domains, the domain of every plan field a record's bases read) must be resolved
  and unsuspended (else
  EXERCISE_PLAN_UNRESOLVED_OR_SUSPENDED {domains} — the same code P0a raises with {domains: [split]} — with the
  same fallback-projection exception per suspended domain as P0a); a domain read by no HELD candidate never
  blocks (rig170 T1: both classes on a source whose candidates read no per-lift domain → ok; one class on the
  D14.1 method with lift:press unresolved → blocked, named; rig172 X3: a class record whose applicability
  names lift:press with that domain unresolved → blocked, named). P2 REDUCE — TERMINAL: the CLASS REDUCER (§D14-E — R0 for a class is
  discharged by P1's horizon guard, R1 consumes P1's payload, then R2–R5) per class; its BLOCKING is the canonical deduplicated union of the common shape
  {code, params — class_id inside} over every class result, sorted by [rank, canonical_encode(params)], its
  ledger the same union of class ledgers, its headline CLASS_BOUNDS_OK only when the blocking union is empty; its DIAGNOSTICS
  always carry class_results — every class's full typed result, sorted — and its payload (success only) the
  accepted per-class bounds, the only thing 4P reads; each class's accepted whole bound, with its EEE_sel* and
  adjustment, becomes its EEE set for the horizon; P2 never returns ZERO_ATTESTED.
§D14-G · APPLICATION-TIME SOURCE COVERAGE (rig173 A1). The sheet's A6 COVERAGE CHECK pauses application when a
  CLIENT operation was accepted after the issuance's computed_through_watermark; source records, proofs,
  withdrawals, stubs, waiting references and registry transitions are NOT client operations, yet their
  identities are in the basis — so a withdrawal ingested after issuance changed the basis with no client
  operation, A6's predicate stayed false, and an honest-but-stale client could apply the old answer. D14
  therefore EXTENDS THE SHEET'S OWN COVERAGE COMMITMENT — beside computed_through_watermark, NEVER inside the
  basis-fact digest (a frontier in the basis would make every unrelated item a changed basis and a fresh
  consent, contradicting the sheet's "receipts never invalidate a basis" and "do NOT change on unrelated
  operations"; rig173 B1): every D14 issuance and basis confirmation carries, with its watermark, the SOURCE
  INGESTION FRONTIER — for EVERY source that has ever held a registration for this athlete (a sequence is never
  dropped, §D14-C), the issuing client's CONTIGUOUS REDUCED-THROUGH receipt: the largest n such that the client
  has stored and reduced every ingestion event of that (athlete, source) sequence with receipt ≤ n — a claim of
  contiguity, never a high-water mark (rig175 A1: a client holding receipts 1, 2 and 4 — 3, the withdrawal of
  the only day record, still in flight — matched the authority's mark 4 and applied the stale answer at 2,001
  while the truth was ABSTAIN; its contiguous frontier is 2, and the attempt pauses); receipts are issued to
  every ingestion event (a disposition, a resolution, a formation, a retirement, a request — §D14-C) in
  ATOMIC GROUPS whose receipts are consecutive and whose every event carries authenticated {ingestion_group_id,
  first_receipt, last_receipt}; the client STAGES a group, verifies every consecutive member, and stores and
  reduces it in ONE durable local transaction, its frontier advancing directly from first_receipt − 1 to
  last_receipt (an interrupted or incomplete group leaves the prior frontier unchanged and suppresses
  source-dependent output — rig177 S4: consecutive numbers alone let a cut sync response leave the frontier at
  2, inside the group), so a frontier can never point inside a group and is an atomic snapshot of everything
  the client can know about that source — and the REGISTRY EPOCH — which also covers every
  retirement_effective transition (§D14-C) —
  the global_registry_epoch of §D14-C's axes, an authority counter incremented by every change to the
  registry's canonical encoding: a registration ADDED or REMOVED, an active flag, declared_phases,
  input_domain_set or dependency-function edit, and every credential-axis change the Protocol defines
  (credential_state, credential epoch, cutoff) — distinct from every source's credential epoch and never
  compared to it; registry mutations and ingestion-group commits serialize per (athlete, source), the group
  committing only by compare-and-append on the Protocol's expected registry tuple (§D14-C; the Protocol's
  response-release steps join the same serialized order) (a removal flips
  the verdict and stales every stamp naming it, so it must advance the epoch — rig173 B8; and every registry
  mutation touching a source first completes that source's pending ingestion group, §D14-C); both are values a
  client can learn ONLY by syncing. D14's COVERAGE PREDICATE is A6's with two more event kinds: application
  PAUSES when any client operation, any source INGESTION EVENT (a disposition, a resolution, a formation, a
  retirement, a request — §D14-C's receipt sequence) or any registry transition lies after the point the
  issuance or confirmation covers — the authority decides
  it at the compare-and-append boundary by comparing the attempt's frontiers and epoch with its OWN current
  sequence lengths and epoch, a pure equality per source, no reduction; the attempt then does NOT commit (it stays idempotent by
  apply_request_id and is retried after the confirmation — no disposition is minted, and in particular NOT the
  sheet's REJECTED_DEPENDENCY, which is a terminal admission failure with a rejected-ledger entry and copy of its
  own; rig173 B2) and application PAUSES exactly as under A6 until a current client syncs through the frontier
  and either confirms the basis (recomputed and UNCHANGED — the item was about another cell, another class, a
  non-qualifying proof — → BASIS CONFIRMATION, proceed; this branch is reachable precisely because the frontier
  is in the coverage, not the basis) or re-issues (CHANGED → new generation, the old answer non-applied History,
  fresh consent) — never a silent apply. ONE DISCLOSED ROW AND ONE PRECEDENCE (Sol pass 26: a row named after
  the source misattributes a registry transition, and several causes can coincide): the sheet's non-applied
  copy is reason-coded by a closed table (NEW_ENTRIES | LADDER_OR_DATE | CONTRACT_UPDATE) and none is truthful
  when a NON-OPERATION INPUT changed; a fourth, CAUSE-NEUTRAL code D14_INPUT_STATE_CHANGED → "Your answer from
  <day> couldn't be applied — what Earned knows about your training-energy sources changed first." (followed,
  like the others, by the sheet's "Here is the current picture.") is filed to §E as a REQUIRED SHEET ROW
  together with the PRECEDENCE that picks ONE reason when several causes are present — CONTRACT_UPDATE →
  LADDER_OR_DATE → D14_INPUT_STATE_CHANGED → NEW_ENTRIES — which orders the three existing codes too; a cause
  is PRESENT only when the recomputed basis INPUTS differ (a frontier or epoch inequality alone leads to a
  BASIS CONFIRMATION, never a row); the row is emitted only on the RE-ISSUE branch; it is unreachable while the
  registry is empty (the disclosed inert state) and a blocking precondition for the FIRST registration of ANY
  kind — D14.1 included, since registering D14.1 is an epoch transition that can re-issue an answered proposal
  — the one row and one precedence this appendix names, both RATIFIED by Sol (pass 27). What §D14-G uses UNCHANGED is frozen:
  basis confirmation, the idempotent apply attempt and pause-until-synced; the COVERAGE commitment is the ONE
  mechanism D14 extends, and only by the two event kinds its own non-operation inputs require; D14 declares
  what its coverage covers, as the policy that introduced non-operation inputs.
  JOINT-SET VALIDATION (at 4T for Today's FINAL lean–EEE set, at 4P for each class's final set — the accepted whole estimate's set combined with the governing lean interval; a candidate's own declared basis was already validated at the screen): J is either CARTESIAN — the product
                    [lean_low, lean_high] × [EEE_low, EEE_high] — or a FINITE UNION OF CLOSED BOXES (products
                    of closed finite intervals), unit-converted first; J is VALID only when it is nonempty,
                    every box is well-formed (low ≤ high, finite), and its projections onto lean and onto EEE
                    EQUAL the declared marginal intervals exactly — cover them, no gaps, nothing outside
                    (a disconnected projection is not a valid declaration: declare the hull, or the day is
                    UNKNOWN); anything else → JOINT_SET_INVALID (rig156 Q3: sup over an empty set is not a
                    number; a box outside the lean marginal raised raw_high 1,830 → 2,100).
  section S(a)    = the union of the EEE intervals of the boxes whose lean interval contains a; NONEMPTY for
                    every a in [lean_low, lean_high] by projection equality (rig156 Q3: 400 validated unions,
                    0 empty sections).
  EEE_sel         = the NEAREST-FEASIBLE SELECTOR on S(lean_anchor) applied to EEE_sel* — the reducer's
                    SELECTED EEE VALUE (§D14-E R4 (iii)), which is AUTHORITATIVE here: the day record's or class
                    bound's stored point is never re-read at this stage (rig163 Q5: recomputing from the stored
                    point 250 showed 1,900 after the reducer had raised the value to 400 → 2,050) — EEE_sel* when
                    EEE_sel* ∈ S(lean_anchor) (the feasible central tuple — always the case for a CARTESIAN set
                    without witnesses); otherwise the point of S(lean_anchor) nearest to EEE_sel*, ties → the
                    LOWER value (Sol's witness: section [0,50] ∪ [250,300] at 150 — the hull midpoint 150 is
                    unattainable; the selector gives 50; at 160 → 250, at 140 → 50); order-independent (0 of 400
                    differed under shuffle).
  display_point   = k × lean_anchor + EEE_sel, labelled "nearest feasible exercise estimate at your lean-mass
                    anchor" whenever EEE_sel differs from the stored point, plus the reducer's ADJUSTMENT copy by
                    REASON (JOINT_SECTION → "Adjusted to the nearest estimate feasible at your lean-mass
                    anchor."; PARTIAL_MINIMUM → "Adjusted up to the minimum the component evidence already
                    proves."; COUPLED_SUM → "Adjusted to the nearest estimate consistent with the component
                    evidence."; NONE → nothing — rig166 Q8: component copy only when a component constrained
                    the value); TOTAL after the joint-set validation — an empty section after
                    validation is an implementation fault and abstains as JOINT_SET_INVALID, never a computed
                    point (DISPLAY_POINT_INFEASIBLE retired); {display_point, evidence_range,
                    action_constraint} is always complete or that scope abstains by a code from the table
  raw_high        = max over the boxes c of J of (k × lean_high_c + EEE_high_c) — the supremum, attained
  raw_low         = min over the boxes c of J of (k × lean_low_c + EEE_low_c) — the infimum, attained
  evidence_range  = [raw_low, raw_high] (joint extrema, not independent corners, where the inputs are
                    dependent; rig156 Q3: the dependent three-box witness gives 1,470–1,830, coinciding with the
                    Cartesian corners only because its boxes reach both — a set that reaches neither corner
                    gives a tighter, still attained range); the same validation and formulas apply to every
                    governed class's set (the class's lean interval × its EEE_CLASS_BOUND, or its declared
                    joint basis) before the plan constraint's maximum is taken
  ONE QUANTIZATION AUTHORITY: the policy's fixed constraint_grain {q = 1 kcal/day, origin o = 0}, with
                    ceil_{q,o}(x) = o + q·⌈(x − o)/q⌉ and succ_{q,o}(x) = o + q·(⌊(x − o)/q⌋ + 1);
  operative_minimum = succ_{q,o}(raw_high) — the smallest policy-grain value STRICTLY greater than raw_high;
                    this IS the floor. A plan whose own grid is coarser (a 50-kcal grid) does not get a
                    different floor: its first representable PASSING endpoint is ceil_{q_plan, o_plan}
                    (operative_minimum) — for a 50-kcal grid with origin 0, ceil_{50,0}.
  action_constraint = {MINIMUM, operative_minimum, kcal/day, earned.floor.public v1, constraint_grain {1,
                    kcal/day, 0}, uncertainty_rule: "strict successor of the supremum over the declared joint
                    uncertainty set"} — ALWAYS wrapped in a discriminated SCOPE (rig158 Q3: the bare shape
                    cannot say which it governs): TODAY {civil_cell_key, constraint} | PLAN_HORIZON {plan_basis,
                    governed_classes, co_maximal_classes, constraint}; the scope and its applicability are bound
                    into the proposal basis with the constraint.
  BOUNDARY: EA ≤ 30 is forbidden; an intake band PASSES only when its LOWER endpoint is ≥ operative_minimum.
  APPLICATION RULE (D14 v1 SCOPE — rig159 Q5): the frozen runtime has NO scheduled-effect semantics — no
                    effect interval, target day, activation or expiry exists in A3–A5 or state 8; every accepted
                    proposal takes effect AT ONCE (or after sync, still immediately at the authority). Therefore
                    D14 v1 governs IMMEDIATE PERSISTENT proposals ONLY: such a proposal must pass BOTH the current
                    3T (the TODAY scope for the basis's evaluation civil day) AND 3P (its PLAN_HORIZON scope), and
                    its band must clear max(TODAY, PLAN_HORIZON); a failed 3T or a failed 3P blocks APPLICATION of
                    the proposal (named on the face), never the independent computation or display of the other
                    scope (Sol's witness: Today 2,301, plan 2,001, band 2,100 → blocked at 2,301; with Today
                    NO_OCCURRENCE_EVIDENCE the proposal is blocked while "Floor for your plan: 2,001" still
                    shows). A basis whose evaluation date has passed re-issues under the existing basis-coverage
                    rule, so "current" is always the basis's own date; and by §D14-G the issuance's COVERAGE
                    commitment (v1.25 / v1.27), its confirmation and the apply attempt carry the SOURCE
                    INGESTION FRONTIERS and the REGISTRY EPOCH,
                    so a source item or registry change after issuance forces sync-and-confirm or re-issuance
                    exactly as a client operation does under A6 — never a silent apply. Scheduled, future-only or one-day effects
                    are OUT OF SCOPE for v1 — a successor version would need a consent-bound effect interval and
                    scheduled transaction semantics, which is a RUNTIME REOPENING: filed to the blueprint (§E) as
                    a future decision, not adopted here.
  Sol's witness (rig154): lean_high 50 kg, EEE_high 300 kcal/day, a Cartesian set → raw_high 1,800 (the raw
  boundary; a band starting at 1,800 gives (1,800 − 300) / 50 = 30 exactly — forbidden); operative_minimum =
  1,801; on a 50-kcal plan grid the first representable passing endpoint is 1,850 — the floor is still 1,801.
  Synthetic athlete A, an attested no-exercise day (ZERO_ATTESTED; lean 60.962814528–63.865805696 kg from E1 +
  W1): range 1,828.88443584–1,915.97417088 → shown 1,828–1,916 kcal/day; raw_high 1,915.97417088 →
  operative 1,916 (grain 1).
  HORIZON: an accepted intake band is a PERSISTENT transaction, so its PLAN CONSTRAINT = max over the governed
  day classes (rest days and each training day-class under the effective plan) of operative_minimum(day class),
  each class computed from its versioned EEE_CLASS_BOUND (never from a dated day's status);
  the band passes only when its LOWER endpoint ≥ that maximum; the governed day-class set and each class's
  canonical complete bound are bound into the proposal's dependency basis (a change re-issues); if 3P fails,
  PERSISTENT proposals abstain (per the APPLICATION RULE) and the face names the sorted failure set. TIED MAXIMA
  (rig157 Q3: classes with EEE [0,300] and [200,300] at the same lean both give 1,801): the horizon reduces to
  the CO-MAXIMAL CLASS SET — every class whose operative_minimum equals the maximum — canonically SORTED by
  class_id; the whole set is bound into the basis and NAMED on the face; its FIRST member is the deterministic
  REPRESENTATIVE whose display_point and evidence_range are shown, with the tie disclosed ("set by lower-body
  and upper-body training days — tied"). The PROPOSAL FACE names the governing class set and its value
  SEPARATELY from Today's floor ("Floor for your plan: 2,301 kcal/day, set by training days · Today's floor:
  1,801 kcal/day — you reported no exercise today"). Sol's witness: a 1,900 band clears a
  rest-day operative minimum of 1,801 (raw boundary 1,800) and fails a training-day operative minimum of 2,301
  (raw boundary 2,300) — the plan constraint is the training day's, and the proposal abstains until the band
  clears it.
  UNBOUNDED_OBSERVED, CANDIDATE_MALFORMED, EVIDENCE_CONTRADICTION and CLASS_BOUND_CONFLICT ALWAYS abstain and
  are NEVER defaultable; NO_OCCURRENCE_EVIDENCE is never defaultable (the per-session and walking defaults REQUIRE
  occurrence evidence — they estimate the energy of a bout that happened or is planned, never whether one
  happened); EXACTLY MISSING_EEE_ESTIMATE — occurrence evidence present, estimate absent for one or more
  occurrence ids — and ONLY when it is the SOLE failure kind in the reducer's complete set, is the one value a
  versioned default may fill, with a disclosed range: OPEN DECISION D14.1 — the versioned
  exercise-energy DEFAULTS and their ranges (candidates: the current engine's 300 kcal per resistance session
  and its walking-cost model, each with a disclosed low/high) need a source spec in the blueprint. TWO D14.1
  OUTPUT TYPES, never crossed: the CLASS METHOD emits, by STANDING EMISSION at every evaluation and with no
  activation precondition and ONLY INTO ABSENCE (a class already HOLDING a candidate — one that passed screen steps
  (1)–(3) and was not excluded at step (7) LINEAGE, from ANY producer in any earlier phase of P1's phase rule,
  v1.23 / v1.24 — receives none), one derived
  class-bound CANDIDATE per governed class — the SAME complete
  EEE_CLASS_BOUND (point included) with method provenance and its derivation commitment, the walking-rule term
  for a NO_PLANNED_BOUTS class — screened and reduced like any candidate, feeding only the plan constraint; a
  D14.1 DATED default is a one-shot FILL of Today's missing occurrence ids, NORMALIZED into EXACTLY ONE
  EEE_DAY_RECORD {header {the queried cell's civil_cell_key, status: BOUNDED}, candidate_origin METHOD_DERIVATION, coverage (= the frozen M ∪ the consumed partials' coverage = O), low, point, high, unit kcal/day, source (the default's
  identity), derivation_commitment, method, version, filled_components (the modalities the default supplied, sorted), evidence_ids (the
  occurrence facts it estimated, sorted), completeness_basis, overlap_basis, joint_uncertainty_basis, and the
  DERIVED-CANDIDATE FIELDS of §D14-C — aggregation DIRECT | SUM, derivation_components[] (every component the
  method combined, typed by role with its own declared joint set, sorted) and cross_partial_basis (the
  SHARED-LEAN COUPLING, REQUIRED iff aggregation = SUM with two or more uncertainty-bearing components, every
  summed component an axis; absent otherwise — rig167 G8)} that then
  passes the SAME validation as any BOUNDED record — header, 0 ≤ low ≤ point ≤ high < ∞ with high > 0, unit
  conversion at full precision, completeness over every modality, overlap_basis as a proof, joint set — and
  feeds only Today's floor (rig158 Q1c); a class bound never fills Today (it has no dated header: it describes a
  recurring class, not a day); a default fills ONLY the components that are missing and never overwrites,
  masks or reinterprets unbounded, overlapping or contradictory observed evidence; a default never manufactures
  ZERO_ATTESTED, and an inverted, stale, exact-zero or cell-mismatched (civil_cell_key inequality) default fails validation. D14.1
  ACTIVATION PRECONDITION (Sol pass 13): a default runs as a ONE-SHOT, NON-RECURSIVE transition — (i) the raw
  evidence is reduced (R0–R5) and, ONLY when the headline is MISSING_EEE_ESTIMATE as the sole blocking code, the
  MISSING set M is FROZEN (the exact uncovered occurrence ids, bound; a NO_PLANNED_BOUTS class without its bound
  is CLASS_BOUND_MISSING and never enters here, rig162 Q6); (ii) the versioned default method fills exactly
  those ids, taking the PARTIAL estimates as inputs; (iii) it emits ONE
  whole-day estimate (this route is Today's only; the class emission is standing) that CONSUMES its inputs — the consumed partial estimates leave the candidate
  set, so no overlap or contradiction is manufactured between an input and its derivative; (iv) the emitted
  estimate passes terminal validation as any BOUNDED record; a derived estimate never re-enters the reducer as
  raw evidence for another default, and a change to any input re-runs the whole transition from (i). The
  ACTIVATION CONTRACT binds, in the emitted estimate's derivation commitment: the exact FILLED occurrence ids
  (= the frozen M), the CONSUMED partial identities (sorted), the complete inputs-and-bases digest (any change
  to O, to a partial, to a zero proof or to a basis INVALIDATES the output — complete-basis invalidation),
  policy_version, method id and ACTIVE method version, the domain and scope target, the cross-partial basis the
  method assumed, and the CANONICALIZED output — with the commitment field itself, candidate identity, wrappers and authority metadata EXCLUDED, so
  the commitment is a fixed point (deterministic output binding: the same inputs commit to the same output;
  rig162 Q6); the emitted whole estimate is then subject to the partial/whole consistency gate like any whole
  (consumption never waives it — the consumed partials leave the COMPETING set but remain in the WITNESS set,
  rig163 Q6a: removing them permitted a false 1,801 where the proven boundary is ≥ 1,901); DERIVATION CURRENCY:
  an output is CURRENT only while the raw-evidence basis digest it binds equals the CURRENT basis — an input
  change makes the earlier output STALE_DERIVATION (ledger, excluded) even under the same active method version
  (rig163 Q6b; this is the exclusion route — no two-output conflict is ever reported between an output and its
  own superseded predecessor); ALIAS / COLLISION RULE: the alias namespace is {policy_id, policy_version,
  domain, scope_target, method_id, method_version} — at most one CURRENT derivation per alias; outputs of a
  non-active method version are STALE_DERIVATION; two CURRENT outputs under one alias with EQUAL commitments
  are aliases of ONE derivation and reduce to one equivalence class by §D14-D (v1.23 — a duplicate immutable
  record is never a conflict); two CURRENT outputs under one alias with DIFFERENT commitments are a
  contradiction / conflict like any two non-equivalent whole estimates.
UNCERTAINTY RULE: the operative minimum is the strict successor of the supremum of (k × lean + EEE) over the
  declared joint uncertainty set (lean-mass uncertainty and exercise-estimate uncertainty together); the face
  shows point + range with the operative minimum named, in kcal/day, and on a training day attributes the range
  to BOTH estimates. Synthetic A, attested no-exercise day: "Earned's floor today: 1,916 kcal/day · about 1,872
  · range 1,828–1,916" (k × 62.414310112 = 1,872.42930336 → 1,872; the central tuple (lean_point, 0) is
  feasible); a
  training-day face adds "from your lean-mass and training-energy estimates".
ELIGIBLE POPULATION: adults; the evidence population (E3) is mostly sedentary women in short protocols — use in
  men and in trained athletes is marked evidence_scope: EXTRAPOLATED, and Why says so; the public policy
  deliberately does not adopt the contested lower male thresholds (a successor version may, with its own basis).
EFFECT ON PROPOSALS: any machine proposal whose resulting intake band's LOWER endpoint would sit below the floor
  ABSTAINS and names the floor and the policy; Why states that a number is never clearance and never a
  diagnosis, that 30 is Earned's conservative product boundary informed by historical EA literature (short
  protocols, mostly sedentary women; male levels less understood), and that the exercise-energy estimate is an
  estimate with its method and range.
CUSTOMER #1: 25 kcal/kg FFM hard floor stays his personal policy (earned.floor.owner v1), owner-attested, ported
  as his setting.
GATES: exactly the GATE ORDER above — (1) → (2) → 3T ∥ 3P (P0a → P1 → P0b → P2) → 4T / 4P — with the RESULT CONTRACT's code table as
  the only enumeration of codes; a declared producer's day estimate (an authoritative source's whole, or the ruled D14.1 DATED default —
  the latter only where the headline is MISSING_EEE_ESTIMATE alone) may satisfy 3T, and a declared producer's
  standing class-bound emission (selected by P1's PHASE RULE of §D14-F — records, then every ACTIVE registration
  declaring SOURCE_EMISSION as one batch (at most one, by P0a), then the one active D14.1 — v1.23 / v1.24, plan scope)
  is an ordinary
  candidate at 3P (no headline precondition; emitted only into absence — a class holds a candidate iff it passed
  screen steps (1)–(3) and was not excluded at step (7) LINEAGE, v1.24; screened and reduced like any other) —
  the producer precondition of §D14-C, stated once; there is no second gate list.

WHAT CHANGES DOWNSTREAM
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
· Runtime sheet state 10 branches gain their policy ids; the "D13 unresolved" branch is DEAD in the public build
  (defined, unreachable, not drawn). Design draws the three-value protein face and the floor face with the
  operative minimum named.
· Engine-delta oracle: protein by phase (P1 FFM-based on lean_high, P2 body-weight point), the floor as the
  strict successor of the joint supremum with the local-day non-overlapping equation and the day/class split,
  lean-source currency, the two unverified copy claims, plus the items already opened by the sheet.
· The no-exercise attestation uses the frozen event class (type NO_EXERCISE_ATTESTATION) — no schema row is added.
· OPEN D14.1 (owner + blueprint): versioned exercise-energy defaults with disclosed ranges — and, said plainly
  (v1.20, wording unified v1.21): because no frozen operation class carries an exercise-energy estimate, the
  PRODUCER PRECONDITION of §D14-C must be met before any immediate persistent intake proposal can be APPLIED
  and before any training-day or plan figure exists — a declared positive-EEE producer (a blueprint-declared
  authoritative source, or the ruled D14.1 method; D14.1 is mandatory only where no source qualifies) supplying
  every required candidate; it never applies to ZERO_ATTESTED: Today's rest-day figure displays and is bound as
  the TODAY scope's constraint, every training day abstains, 3P returns MISSING_EEE_ESTIMATE for every planned class and
  CLASS_BOUND_MISSING for every NO_PLANNED_BOUTS class, and the application rule (3T AND 3P) blocks every
  immediate persistent intake proposal until 3P is complete.
· §E DISCLOSED LIMITATION (Sol pass 21): a no-exercise attestation qualifies only by exact instant-set equality of
  its recorded interval with the civil-day cell; A4's opaque `interval` is READ as two offset-bearing endpoints (a
  blueprint reading, no schema row) and the client MUST record both offsets — a single-offset interval fails on
  EVERY transition day, not only on two-component cells; a date whose cell has two components (America/Goose_Bay
  2010-11-06) cannot be covered by the athlete's own tap at all (an authoritative source's proof, binding its actual instant set,
  still can) — the card says so.
· §E BLUEPRINT OBLIGATIONS (Sol pass 23): the PRODUCER REGISTRY of §D14-C — every authoritative source and the
  D14.1 method registered with a unique (kind, producer_id, registration version, registration_instance_id), declared phases and a declared
  input-domain set, D14.1 with exactly ONE active version, and a producer reading only its declared domains (an
  obligation on the registration; the runtime cannot observe a read), every registration carrying an `active`
  flag and at most ONE active registration declaring SOURCE_EMISSION (several standing source emitters is a
  SUCCESSOR decision, not adopted — it would need a selection or composition rule v1 does not have), and the
  registry's defect set typed by the closed enum of §D14-C with its named face state; SOURCE INGESTION (v1.24)
  — every source item carries {source_item_id, canonical_content_commitment} and ingestion applies the REPLAY
  RULE (v1.25 / v1.27 / v1.31 / v1.32: a submission is first authenticated against its source's registration
  history, recovered, looked up as a transport-exact replay, verified and looked up as a verified content
  replay — a hit on either returns the holder's prior result internally, disclosed to the source as the
  SOURCE INGESTION PROTOCOL rules; a NEW submission, a miss on both, that the Protocol's door refuses consumes
  nothing; every ADMITTED submission has a
  TRANSPORT identity always and a CONTENT
  identity {source_item_id, commitment} only once its content verifies — and, from the NON-terminal state WAITING, exactly ONE immutable
  terminal disposition, STORED | COLLISION_MEMBER | REJECTION STUB; the ORDER: a transport-exact replay where
  the raw / canonical submission digest is already admitted → verification of the submitted content → the
  verified content replay → ingestion-shape validation (which alone decides whether a submission consumes its
  source_item_id) → the identity / collision test; COLLISION_MEMBER requires a verified, ingestion-shape-valid,
  identity-consuming submission whose id another such item of the source holds with a different commitment — a
  receipted COMPONENT that hides every member and, while component_scope_current and not
  retirement_effective, blocks every scope the members claim (SOURCE_ITEM_IDENTITY_COLLISION, hard); a
  rejection stub never consumes an id and never becomes a member), the
  commitment being the named digest over the source-authored content, a well-formed
  reference to an item of the same source not yet stored (a withdrawal's target, a supersedes pointer, an
  answer's request id) stored WAITING_FOR_SOURCE_ITEM and resolved when the target arrives, proof_record_id and record identity ARE the
  source_item_id, the per-source receipt counter is arrival metadata only, every verified, shape-valid,
  identity-consuming submission of any kind (record, proof, answer, withdrawal — collision members included) is
  stamped with the immutable route identity it arrived under before the identity test, an authenticated
  principal's transport-exact AND verified-content replay lookups and authority-owned group recovery precede
  the current-registration door (the SOURCE INGESTION PROTOCOL — a separate blueprint-side document with its
  own bounded audit — defines the door predicate new_submission_authorized, historical_principal_verified,
  direct_replay_response_authorized, the credential axis with its epochs and signed cutoffs, the unauthorized
  response envelope, the response protocol, the declared leakage inventory and the linearization of response
  release; this appendix's obligation is that every rule here holds for whatever set the Protocol admits, and
  the Protocol's is that it admits into this order and this registry model and changes no disposition,
  identity, currentness or reduction rule; REGISTRATION INCARNATIONS: the route identity
  carries an authority-issued never-reused registration_instance_id allocated per producer_key = {kind,
  producer_id}, PRESENT → REMOVED is terminal, re-registration is a fresh incarnation, at most one PRESENT
  incarnation per producer_key (adding one removes only the prior of the same producer_key),
  current_submission_route_id[source_id] = the unique PRESENT {AUTHORITATIVE_SOURCE, source_id} incarnation,
  active false → true the only same-incarnation reactivation; the EMISSION candidate identity and every
  explicit route tuple, sorting key, active-route record and identity-keyed cache carry the quadruple; the
  registry is validated in THREE STAGES (structural defects over every raw entry, then the PRESENT working
  set, then the active / phase / dependency / cardinality tests over it) and the closed defect enum gains
  REGISTRATION_PRESENCE_MISSING_OR_INVALID and PRESENT_INCARNATION_CARDINALITY {producer_key, count};
  LINEARIZATION as this appendix needs it: registry mutations and ingestion-group commits serialize per
  (athlete, source), the group committing only by compare-and-append on the Protocol's expected tuple, a
  mismatch restarting recovery and both lookups; the AXES registration_presence, active and
  global_registry_epoch as distinct fields, the credential axis the Protocol's, with the one ruled effect on
  evidence of §D14-C), a content replay never
  receives a new route stamp, sync carrying only existing or recovered stored results (a refused miss adds
  nothing), nothing NEW is stored from an unregistered
  source or under a
  registration whose declared_phases lacks RECORD (two record-supplying sources for one scope is a configuration
  to avoid: their records conflict until one registration is deactivated or a record is withdrawn or replaced
  at the feed), every record carries lineage_slot and supersedes_source_item_id and is replaced only by an
  explicit, slot-matched, fully well-formed replacement (the LINEAGE RULE), a WITHDRAWAL is a typed source item
  naming one of the source's own item identities and its effect is defined EXCLUSIVELY by §D14-C's closed
  reference-effect matrix by referrer kind, every emitting registration declares a DEPENDENCY FUNCTION (class definition → the sorted domains it reads,
  range ⊆ input_domain_set) and every stored class-domain derivation declares consumed_plan_domains EQUAL to
  that function's output for its class, and an item ingestion cannot store — a proof without a
  well-formed domain_binding, an answer or withdrawal whose reference is absent or malformed (a well-formed
  reference to an item not yet stored is WAITING, above, disclosed on the source card while unresolved) — is
  stored as a REJECTION STUB that the queried day's ledger, the governed class's ledger and the source card
  disclose, while a verified, ingestion-shape-valid, identity-consuming submission colliding under a held id
  forms a COMPONENT the same three disclose as a BLOCKER while its finding is bound; the
  RECEIPT SEQUENCE is per (athlete, source), consecutive, issued to every ingestion event and never dropped; a transport idempotency key is used as a replay identity only
  where the transport guarantees it stable across retries and only bound to the canonical submission digest,
  never alone, and every transport declares the volatile wrapper fields that digest excludes; and the REQUERY GUARANTEE a source may
  declare, under which the ingestion side (never an evaluation) asks for a NEW proof for a civil_cell_key — the
  request STORED, only when no stored proof of that source qualifies for the cell, at most once per (source_id,
  civil_cell_key), requested_cell_key stamped by ingestion from the stored request the answer names, AT MOST ONE
  QUALIFYING response per request — several terminally stored answers may exist and are reduced by the
  deterministic selector over the terminal STORED, ingestion-shape-valid ANSWER items naming the request whose
  resolution outcome is APPLIES, in no collision component and not currently withdrawn, least source_item_id
  first, selection preceding the answer's domain-qualification tests — and the source
  re-derives it from its complete raw timestamped data; the ATOMIC INGESTION GROUP — a triggering admission and
  every deterministic resolution, formation, membership update or retirement it causes committed as one atomic
  authority commit with consecutive receipts, no intermediate sequence length ever served, AUTHORITY-OWNED
  group recovery before any later ingestion, registry mutation, sync snapshot or reduction involving that
  (athlete, source) — never dependent on a source retry, a replay recovering first too — an incomplete durable
  group blocking reduction over that source and D14 application;
  CLIENT-ATOMIC GROUPS — every ingestion event carries authenticated {ingestion_group_id, first_receipt,
  last_receipt}; a client stages the complete group, verifies every consecutive member, and stores and reduces
  the group in one durable local transaction; its reduced-through frontier advances directly from
  first_receipt − 1 to last_receipt; an interrupted or incomplete group leaves the prior frontier unchanged and
  suppresses source-dependent output; sync either returns complete groups or exposes the boundaries this
  staging rule needs; INGESTION ORDER — the Protocol's principal authentication against the registration
  history, then authority-owned pending-group recovery, then transport-exact replay where the submission
  digest is already admitted, then verification that only REPORTS (VERIFIED | UNVERIFIABLE, committing
  nothing), then the verified content replay (NOT_APPLICABLE for UNVERIFIABLE — a miss), then — for every
  miss — the Protocol's door (false: REFUSED with no durable state; true: the transport-keyed rejection-stub
  group for UNVERIFIABLE, else on), then ingestion-shape validation (which alone decides whether a submission
  consumes its source_item_id), then the route stamp, then the identity test, then the Protocol's serialized
  response release; the closed
  REFERENCE-EFFECT MATRIX by referrer kind; retirement_effective and component_scope_current derived at every
  basis (the formation and retirement events are audit only; a component of a source with no current member
  stamp is diagnostics, never a finding, and reactivation brings the finding back); SOURCE_ITEM_UNREADABLE keyed by the
  per-athlete, per-source, domain-separated keyed replay-identity commitment with the receipt in diagnostics
  only; and a withdrawal that targets an identity, its effect given by the reference-effect matrix alone,
  never one member; and
  APPLICATION-TIME SOURCE COVERAGE (§D14-G, v1.25 / v1.27) — the authority keeps ONE consecutive receipt
  sequence per (athlete, source), issued to every ingestion event and never dropped, and one REGISTRY EPOCH
  counter incremented by every registry change (a registration added or removed included); every D14 issuance
  and confirmation and every apply attempt carries the issuing client's CONTIGUOUS reduced-through frontier
  per source (the SOURCE INGESTION FRONTIER — never a high-water mark) and the epoch, both learnable only by
  syncing, in the COVERAGE commitment beside the watermark, compared by the authority at the compare-and-append
  boundary with its own sequence lengths and epoch (unequal → the attempt does not commit; pause-until-synced,
  then confirm or re-issue).
· §E REQUIRED SHEET ROW AND PRECEDENCE (raised by Sol pass 25, shaped by pass 26, RATIFIED by Sol pass 27): the state-8
  non-applied copy table (NEW_ENTRIES | LADDER_OR_DATE | CONTRACT_UPDATE) needs a fourth, cause-neutral reason
  code D14_INPUT_STATE_CHANGED → "Your answer from <day> couldn't be applied — what Earned knows about your
  training-energy sources changed first." (then "Here is the current picture.") and ONE PRECEDENCE that picks
  a single reason when several causes are present — CONTRACT_UPDATE → LADDER_OR_DATE → D14_INPUT_STATE_CHANGED
  → NEW_ENTRIES — which orders the three existing codes as well; one row and one precedence; a cause is present
  only when the recomputed basis inputs differ, and the row is emitted only on the re-issue branch; unreachable
  while the producer registry is empty (the disclosed inert state) and a BLOCKING PRECONDITION for the first
  registration of ANY kind, D14.1 included.
· §E FUTURE DECISION, NOT ADOPTED (Sol passes 14–15): per-day ALLOCATION of a bout that crosses local midnight or a
  UTC offset change, Today or planned (v1 abstains for every day such a bout touches — no source-supplied split
  exists in v1 either).
· §E FUTURE DECISION, NOT ADOPTED (Sol pass 13): reducer-side COMPONENT COMPOSITION (summing several partial
  estimates) — requires component-joint-set validation with exact projection equality, the exact summed union
  for point selection, and an aggregate point for dependent sets; v1 accepts one whole-day estimate only.
· §E FUTURE DECISION, NOT ADOPTED (Sol pass 12): scheduled, future-only or one-day proposal effects would need a
  consent-bound effect interval and scheduled transaction semantics — a runtime reopening; D14 v1 governs
  immediate persistent proposals only, both current 3T and 3P required.
· Reviewer audit scope (bounded): are E1–E3 quoted faithfully; is the policy shape consistent with the sheet's
  action_constraint contract; is the uncertainty rule the conservative direction in each case; do the gates and
  branches cover every athlete state without a silent default.
